Chapter 2794
Translator: Henyee Translations Editor: Henyee Translations
Family members
Mo Yijian slowly walked toward Luo Zheng, a faint smile still on his face.
His every move seemed careless, as if he had complete control over his emotions.
"We meet again," Mo Yijian said.
Luo Zheng glanced at him and said, "You haven't paid for my sword. Eight million divine crystals."
"You don't have to be so stingy, do you? I haven't sold any Sword Comprehension Liquid these past few months. You've earned all those divine crystals, "Mo Yijian said with a smile.
Luo Zheng shrugged. "I earned it with my own ability. You didn't give it to me."
Mo Yijian had warned Luo Zheng, but after returning to the Heart Flow Sword Sect, someone had also warned Mo Yijian.
There were a dozen people in the Heavenly Palace who could make Sword Comprehension Liquid, but they had given this privilege to Mo Yijian.
In the past few years, Mo Yijian had used this privilege to accumulate a lot of divine crystals. Naturally, someone had asked him to give this privilege to Luo Zheng …
"Paying you a few million divine gems is not a problem, provided that you have the strength to defeat me. I wonder how long you will have to wait?" Mo Yijian slightly raised his eyebrows. His provocation became clear.
No disciple in the Heart Flow Sword Sect could contend against Mo Yijian.
In the past few years, he had never found anyone who could threaten him in the Heart Flow Sword Sect. In fact, there were only two or three disciples of the same generation in the entire Heavenly Palace.
But there seemed to be one more in front of him …
"Of course I'm not in a hurry to pay this debt, but I believe it won't take long," Luo Zheng said with a shrug.
Mo Yijian smiled. "I'll wait for you, but I hope it's not … forever."
Then he took Han 'er's hand and walked away.
"Did you hear that? This Luo Zheng seems to have some conflict with Mo Yijian? "
"That's not the point. The point is that this guy can make Sword Comprehension Liquid!"
"So he's a genius who comprehended the Dao with a single thought. No wonder … a genius is a genius. He's extraordinary no matter where he goes!"
Compared to the result of Luo Zheng reaching the tenth floor of the Heart Flow Pagoda, the news revealed by Mo Yijian shocked them even more.
After all, the Heart Flow Pagoda was only a test of the Heart Flow Sword Sect. It was possible to cultivate a special method to resist the Heart Fire, but comprehending the Dao with a thought was a standard. Geniuses who met this standard had limitless potential in the future.
There were hundreds of millions of people in the Heavenly Palace Domain. Since ancient times, how many geniuses had comprehended the Tao with a single thought?
"Comprehending the Dao with a single thought …"
The bewitching woman suddenly came to a realization, and a look of anger appeared on her face.
It was impossible for the Heavenly Palace not to know that such a genius had come up the mountain, but no one had warned her!
Xiaoyin and Xiaoxin were already immersed in great joy. They almost couldn't accept this result. When they saw Luo Zheng walking towards them, their cheeks flushed red.
"Young, Young Master Luo …"
Xiaoyin thought of her complaints about Luo Zheng, and her expression became a little unnatural.
"I said that I wouldn't let you down. You shouldn't be disappointed, right?" Luo Zheng asked with a smile.
"How could that be …" Xiaoxin quickly shook her head.
"A genius like Young Master Luo doesn't need to be idle to cleanse his heart. Xiaoxin and I were blind," Xiaoyin said.
"That's good. Let's go back," Luo Zheng said as he prepared to leave with the two women.
At this time, the bewitching woman said, "Wait a minute."
Luo Zheng turned around and asked, "What's the matter?"
"According to the rules of the Heart Flow Sword Sect, you and Li Can have passed the seventh floor of the Heart Flow Pagoda, so you have to move out of your residence," the bewitching woman said politely as she stared at Luo Zheng.
Although Luo Zheng was just a new disciple, his potential was comparable to Mo Yijian, and he even had the potential to surpass Mo Yijian. Naturally, she would change her attitude.
Xiaoyin and Xiaoxin's hearts bloomed when they heard the bewitching woman's words. Looking at Luo Zheng's wooden expression, they almost wanted to agree on Luo Zheng's behalf.
The attendant, Luo Zheng, said, "There's no need. That courtyard is already a very good residence for me."
Xiaoyin and Xiaoxin's expressions became very interesting. They didn't know how to describe Young Master Luo.
"That won't do. These are the rules of the Heart Flow Sword Sect. Luo Zheng, please move in. Otherwise, I'll be in a difficult position." The bewitching woman's tone was gentle, but she didn't intend to give in. After all, this was her duty.
Luo Zheng looked at Xiaoyin and Xiaoxin. The two women in white seemed to be saying, "Move in, move in …"
He smiled helplessly and said, "Okay, let's go back and prepare. We'll move in …"
Watching Luo Zheng and the two women leave, the woman in white called Su Yin bit her lip.
In a daze, she bit her lip until it started bleeding.
Thinking of how she had mocked Xiaoyin before, she felt that what was happening now was more like a huge conspiracy, silently mocking her ignorance.
The other women in white were also agitated.
Two hours ago, they were still pitying Xiaoyin and Xiaoxin, but they were about to be chased down the mountain.
Two hours later, they had followed the young masters to those independent courtyards. Moreover, this Young Master Luo could even be mentioned in the same breath as Mo Yijian. This huge disparity was like a heavy hammer pounding on their chests, suffocating them.
… …
… …
Those independent courtyards were very spacious.
There were six entrances and six exits. There were even three courtyards.
There were small pavilions and jade ponds in the courtyards. The arrangement was picturesque and very livable.
People like Luo Zheng who cultivated did not have any requirements for the external environment.
If it was not on the top of Greater One Mountain, a True God would be able to build such a courtyard within four to six hours.
After walking around the courtyard, Xiaoyin and Xiaoxin followed closely behind.
After moving into the independent courtyard, their remuneration had tripled.
For the children of ordinary people, the return of divine crystals was a very considerable number.
But the two women had their own little thoughts.
Since Young Master Luo knew the mechanism of the [Heart Flow Tower], he also understood the importance of the [Heart Flow Sword]. If Young Master Luo wanted to continue to study the [Heart Flow Sword], he could not escape from this.
Xiaoyin and Xiaoxin were still young, so how could they not admire someone like Luo Zheng?
At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly asked, "Since I live alone in this courtyard, can I bring my family to live in it for the time being?"
Ning Yudie, Xi Youqin and the other girls had been staying in his inner world. It was time to let them out for a breath of fresh air.
"Family?"
Xiaoyin and Xiaoxin exchanged a glance, then Xiaoyin said, "This courtyard is given to Young Master by the [Heart Flow Sword Sect], the sect will not interfere, but... does Young Master Luo have a family?"
In their eyes, Luo Zheng's skeletal age was very young, so they naturally did not expect this detail. Their reaction was very surprised.

Chapter 2795
Translator: Henyee Translations Editor: Henyee Translations
Stumbling
With a single thought, Luo Zheng created an Inner Body Incarnation and appeared in Immortal Mansion.
When the girls heard this news, they all had different expressions.
Ning Yudie's eyes flashed as a look of anticipation appeared on her face. Xi Youqin, on the other hand, was cheering excitedly.
Su Lingyun looked calm and indifferent. Luo Zheng returned to Immortal Mansion every once in a while and accompanied her for a while. She was already very satisfied.
As for Xun, although she did not say a word, she also stopped cultivating. She also wanted to see the legendary Mother World.
With a flash of white light, the girls appeared in the open space in front of the courtyard. Their faces were full of curiosity.
Xun stretched out her hand and sniffed. She quietly sensed and said, "This place … doesn't seem to be much different from the Divine Realm."
"Indeed, there isn't much difference." Luo Zheng nodded.
There was a huge difference between Huan Yu and the Divine Realm. After all, Huan Yu was only an Inner World. It was fundamentally different from the real world in terms of rules. Although the Divine Realm was created by Chi You, it was no different from the Mother World in terms of level.
Xi Youqin strolled around the courtyard. After all, this courtyard was prepared for the elites of the Heart Flow Sword Sect. The courtyards and pavilions were built in picturesque disorder. It was indeed a good place to live. It was not inferior to Immortal Mansion in any aspect.
But Xi Youqin's focus was different from the other girls. She returned to Luo Zheng's side and her gaze immediately fell on Xiaoyin and Xiao Xin. Seeing their beautiful faces, she said in a playful tone, "These two must be Husband's lovers?"
Luo Zheng knew that this would happen if he let Xi Youqin out. But if he asked Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin to come out alone, how could Xi Youqin compromise?
He was just about to explain when Xiao Xin bowed to Xi Youqin. "Madam, we are only Lord Luo's servant girls."
Xi Youqin blinked. Ning Yudie, Xun and the other girls were also a little puzzled.
With Luo Zheng's personality, he naturally did not need any servant girls to serve him.
"So you are servant girls?" Xi Youqin smiled. "Since we are out, Husband doesn't need you to take care of him. You can leave."
Hearing this, Xiao Xin was speechless and did not dare to refute. But Xiaoyin's choice.
She looked at the women beside Luo Zheng with mixed feelings. These women were all extraordinary in appearance and bearing, and each of them had their own unique characteristics.
Previously, she had thought that Luo Zheng was an ascetic cultivator who did not care about love affairs. It turned out that he had hidden these women in his Inner World. This was far beyond her expectations. However, on second thought, Young Master Luo was so outstanding that he naturally did not jump out of a stone. It was normal for him to attract the attention of other women.
But Xi Youqin's words made her completely depressed.
She and Xiao Xin had gone through so much suffering and met Luo Zheng's miraculous performance. It was not easy for them to enter this independent courtyard, but this woman wanted to drive them away with just a few words. How could she give in?
"Madam, don't you think you are too domineering?" Xiaoyin said in a low voice.
Xi Youqin raised her eyebrows. "Domineering? How can a servant girl talk like that? "
"We were ordered by the Sword of Heart Sect to take care of Young Master Luo. Naturally, we can't be dismissed so easily. In the future, Young Master Luo's cultivation and the affairs of the Sword of Heart Sect will be handled by us …" Xiao Yin explained.
How could Xi Youqin know what the Heart Sword Sect was?
Among the girls in the Immortal Mansion, Xun was more concerned about Luo Zheng's cultivation. Every time Luo Zheng appeared, she would ask for advice. Luo Zheng would also teach her everything he knew.
Ning Yudie was concerned about her husband's future. She would ask where he was and what kind of power he had. Sometimes, she would give Luo Zheng advice.
As for Xi Youqin, she did not have many thoughts other than being with Luo Zheng …
Xiaoyin's words stunned Xi Youqin.
But Xi Youqin's bright eyes flashed. "Okay, you are just here to pass on the message, right?"
She reached out and a purple sword floated beside her hand. With a light wave of her finger, the purple sword moved.
"Slash …"
The sword drew a straight line in the courtyard.
"Of course I will take care of my husband. The other side of the courtyard is yours. Other than passing on the message, you can't go there," Xi Youqin said generously.
Xiaoyin was furious at Xi Youqin's submissive attitude.
She thought this girl was gentle and quiet and easy to talk to. She did not expect her to be so difficult.
Ning Yudie, Xun, and the other girls smiled at Xi Youqin and shook their heads inwardly. They were used to Xi Youqin being like this.
Ning Yudie looked at Xiaoyin and Xi Youqin's distressed expression. She reached out and took the sword from Xi Youqin's hand. "Okay, don't make trouble. Since our husband has entered the Heart Flow Sword Sect, he has to follow the rules. Don't make things difficult for him."
This was not the Divine Realm, but the Mother World, the Great Yi Heavenly Palace. Her husband was just a disciple here, so it was impossible for him to act as unbridled as he did in the Divine Realm. Regarding this point, Ning Yudie was much more thorough than Xi Youqin.
Under the persuasion of Su Lingyun and Ning Yudie, Xi Youqin finally gave in. But Xi Youqin still set a rule: They were not allowed to enter Luo Zheng's room!
…
…
For the next few days, the days passed very quietly.
Xiaoyin left the courtyard early in the morning. When she returned, she was holding a brocade box in her hand.
She went straight to the second floor of the courtyard with the brocade box. Xi Youqin took the opportunity to lean against the side of the pavilion and block her path.
"This is a secret item that the Heart Flow Sword Sect gave to Young Master Luo. I need to personally hand it to him!" Xiaoyin said.
Everyone lived in peace for the past few days, but Xiao Yin and Xi Youqin were still stumbling around.
"What is it? I will personally hand it to my husband. You are not allowed to step in," Xi Youqin said proudly.
"You are not qualified to touch such a precious item!" Xiaoyin also put up a tit for tat.
"You!" Xi Youqin could not tolerate this and was about to explode.
At this moment, a coughing sound suddenly came from the courtyard. The two women turned to look at the source of the sound, and their expressions changed slightly.
At some point, a handsome middle-aged man with a long beard had appeared in the courtyard.
When Xi Youqin sensed this middle-aged man's aura, there was a faint trace of fear in her eyes. Even if she did not know the immensity of heaven and earth, she knew that this person's strength was unfathomable.
"I will personally deliver the Heart Flow Sword Manual. I still have some things to discuss with Luo Zheng." The person who came was Qiu Yinhe.

Fiend Star
After their last encounter, Luo Zheng knew that Qiu Yinhe was a casual person.
He smiled. "Elder Qiu, why are you so sure that I'll choose the Heart Flow Sword Sect?"
"Because I'll be waiting for you on the mountain. If you choose the Murdering Sword Sect or the Absolute Formation Sword Sect, I'll drive you back to the right path," Qiu Yinhe said casually.
Such a sincere answer made Luo Zheng speechless. After a while, he said, "Elder Qiu, it seems that you're really from the Heart Flow Sword Sect?"
"Yes." Qiu Yinhe nodded and pushed the box in front of Luo Zheng. "You've already chosen three True Meaning Sword Scriptures. You should have some success in all of them. But I hope you can spend as much time on the Heart Flow Sword as possible."
"Why?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Luo Zheng had many things to study now.
The Thousand Burying Sword of the Fusion God Sword Scripture needed to be continuously honed to level up. After obtaining the Mind's Eye, Luo Zheng also looked forward to the power of the sword pattern. Although he had almost mastered it, he still needed to master it.
Also, he wanted to explore the two Sanskrit words that Luo Nian helped him translate …
"Because there's not enough time," Qiu Yinhe said seriously. "You need to increase your cultivation as soon as possible."
Increasing cultivation meant going to the other side. But what did this have to do with cultivation?
Seeing Luo Zheng's confused expression, Qiu Yinhe continued, "Strictly speaking, the Heart Flow Sword isn't a sword art divine art. It's a divine art that cultivates the mind. Its most powerful aspect isn't in this world."
"Elder Qiu, you mean it's on the other side?" Luo Zheng asked.
The Heart Flow Sword was said to train the mind, but "mind" was a general term. In the past, those people did not study the secrets of the soul thoroughly. They called the heart the soul, but the soul was actually in the mind.
Luo Zheng's test in the Heart Flow Tower was essentially a test of the soul.
"Correct again." Qiu Yinhe nodded with satisfaction.
"Elder Qiu, what did you mean by not enough time?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"Of course it's related to the Paramita Realm. You're still in the First Heaven and can't touch that level. So after comprehending the Heart Sword Technique, you need to enter the Paramita Realm as soon as possible. That's the source of power. Our Heart Sword Sect will naturally make arrangements for that," said Qiu Yinhe.
Qiu Yinhe Sword Scripture, no matter what sword technique Luo Zheng's Sword, the increase in Shen Jun power was limited.
However, if one were to obtain a Nirvana Realm Token from the Nirvana Realm, the power of Shen Jun would most likely increase exponentially …
Qiu Yinhe didn't say anything more and took his leave.
"What will the Heart Flow Sword Sect do?" Luo Zheng wondered.
He remembered that Lingshuang had said that the Heavenly Palace had its own fixed forces and temples in the other side. Therefore, she did not care about the Heavenly Palace's Sword Scripture and was not in a hurry. It seemed that the Heart Flow Sword Sect was the same as the Taiyi Mountain. Or rather, the Heavenly Palace would take special care of these elites and not let them roam freely.
If so, he was looking forward to entering the other side. But before that, he still followed Qiu Yinhe's instructions and comprehended the Heart Flow Sword Scripture.
Luo Zheng opened the brocade box and took out the Heart Flow Sword Scripture.
…
…
Blood Qi filled the sky at the foot of Mount Hades in the center of Prison State.
Mount Hades had 33 grotto-heavens with 33 clans living in it.
Mount Hades was not famous in the mother world. Those who gathered here were all branches of some clans.
The Human Clan also had a branch called the "People of the Nineteen Grotto-Heavens."
There were not many experts in these 33 grotto-heavens. Even the top experts in the "People of the Nineteen Grotto-Heavens" were only at the Second Heaven of the Nirvana Realm. The other clans were similar.
Because of this, the 33 clans contended with each other and lived in peace.
If they were invaded by other clans, the 33 clans would join hands to defend their pure land.
But what happened today made the clansmen of the "People of the Nineteen Grotto-Heavens" panic.
The rich blood Qi spread up Mount Hades and into the Nineteen Grotto-Heavens. The smell of blood rushed into their nostrils.
A few two-winged bats flew to the Nineteen Grotto-Heavens. One of the two-winged bats spoke in human language. "Squeak squeak … Why don't you persuade him? That guy is going to slaughter all of us!"
The leader of the "People of the Nineteen Grotto-Heavens" smiled bitterly. "What can I do? You were in the wrong first. Who told you to provoke such a fiend? "
"Squeak squeak. We only sucked the blood of one of his Lion Pythons. It's just one Lion Pythons! Just one! At most, we'll compensate him with Divine Crystals. 100 Divine Crystals! 1,000 Divine Crystals will do! He wants to slaughter our entire clan. What kind of logic is this! "the two-winged bat said angrily." Aren't our 33 grotto-heavens united? Now that our Bat Clan is in trouble, you're just going to stand by and watch? "
The leader of the Nineteen Grotto-Heavens waved his hand. He gritted his teeth and said, "Then I'll go persuade him again!"
With that, he jumped down from the mountainside and arrived at the bottom of Mount Hades.
Countless two-winged bats were torn to pieces. Their blood mixed into the soil and dyed the ground black.
This was only the outside of the grotto-heavens. The inside of the grotto-heavens was probably even bloodier.
"Boom!"
"Boom!"
"Boom …"
In the third grotto-heavens of the Bat Clan, there were continuous explosions.
Countless two-winged bats wanted to escape from the grotto-heavens. But as the blazing white light flashed, they were all annihilated.
Logically speaking, the 33 grotto-heavens would attack that fiend when faced with such a situation.
But the "Blue Ghoul Clan" of the second grotto-heavens was helping the "Bat Clan". Now, 90% of the Blue Ghoul Clan had been slaughtered. Even the leader of the Blue Ghoul Clan had died. How could the other grotto-heavens dare to provoke this fiend?
They all closed their grotto-heavens and refused to come out.
The people of the Nineteen Grotto-Heavens didn't dare to provoke him. They didn't want to meddle in such matters …
But this fiend happened to be a human!
Based on the friendship of the same clan, the people of the Nineteen Grotto-Heavens sent two people into the grotto-heavens to persuade him. But who knew that the two people would be thrown out as soon as they entered the grotto-heavens. Only two bloody and headless corpses were left.
How could the people of the Nineteen Grotto-Heavens dare to persuade him?
The key was that from the beginning to the end, it was just a mount, the "Soaring Lion".
While he was on his way, he had tied the "Lion King" he had captured to one side. The Bat Clan lived by sucking blood, especially the blood of the "Lion King". How could he hold back when he saw the tamed Lion King?
In the end, they found the wrong target. If they had the chance, they wouldn't have dared to do anything to this fiend's Soaring Lion.
Seeing the "Bat Clan" being slaughtered, the leader of the people of the Nineteen Grotto-Heavens couldn't refuse their pleas. He could only go personally.
He walked to the entrance of the third grotto-heavens and looked inside with vigilance.

The woman in the long dress.

The clan leader's ribs were crushed. If the malefic man increased his strength, his body would have been torn apart.

Driven by his survival instinct, he could only say so.

The other clans in the 33 caves saw how powerful the malefic man was. They were afraid of following in the footsteps of the Blue Ghouls and the Bat Clan, so they all shut themselves in.
However, they were all carefully spying on the movements below. When they heard the malefic man's words, their expressions changed.

The clans conversed in their own language.
Then, the boundaries of the caves opened one by one. The clans in the 33 caves, no matter if they were weak or strong, all came out.
Some of them were huge and strong like bulls, while others were short and small, but their bodies were filled with poison ….
The clans of different shapes and sizes gathered into a torrent and rushed toward the malefic man.
The malefic man still stepped on the clan leader's chest and said, "Why? Hahaha … Because of one sentence. Those who aren't of my clan will have a different heart! "
Then …
He suddenly turned around. Strands of five-colored energy gathered like a new moon. There was no fear on his face except for a sinister smile.
He had grown up in the midst of killing.
"Those who aren't of my clan will have a different heart" was complete nonsense.
Back in that world, he had killed over ten times more human beings than non-humankind beings. After he had killed all the geniuses of a continent, he would move on to another continent until he was the only one left in the entire world. No one would dare to say a word against him!

None of the creatures in the entire Netherworld Mountain could recognize the energy used by this fiend. They all thought that this fiend had a special Nirvana Token, but in fact, he didn't even borrow the power of the Nirvana Token.
Violent energy shockwaves pulverized all living beings that dared to approach.
Broken limbs were scattered all over the ground …
In the first round of slaughter, the top experts of the 33 Caves had all fallen.
The leader of the human race from the 19th Paradise stared blankly at the malefic star floating in the air. His eyes were filled with fear …
He had heard that the powerful Human Clan would recruit some peerless geniuses. However, those peerless geniuses could not be compared to the person in front of him.
"Come on! Hahaha! "
The non-humankind beings in the 33 Caves felt that powerful Divine Jun Energy. They fell into despair. Many of them began retreating.
But would this malefic star allow them to do so?
Several crescent moons floated by his side once again as he chased after those non-humankind beings!
At the same time …
There was a huge pitch-black ship floating 5,000 kilometers above the Prisoner Prefecture.
This ship looked dilapidated. It was emitting a heavy aura of death. It was like a ghost ship drifting slowly.
There were many skeletons embedded on the side of the ship. These skeletons belonged to the Human Clan, the Jinwu Clan, the Nuwa Clan, the Heavenly Music Clan, and the Yan Clan!
Furthermore, the owners of these skeletons had undergone the Chaotic Origin Rebirth. They were all experts from these huge clans …
"Ka …"
A hand that was as pale as a piece of paper gently picked up a human head from the railing of the ship.
The owner of this hand was a human girl wearing a black gown. This girl was extremely beautiful, but she did not have a single strand of life force. If she could not move, she would be a corpse.
She placed this human's head at the tip of her nose and gently sucked on it. Strands of green flames drifted out from the eye sockets of this skull and entered her mouth and nose.
"There are so many skeletons on the ship, yet you chose his skull." A sinister voice sounded from behind her.
This person was wrapped in a black silk thread. His original appearance could not be seen. However, his body was thin and long like a bamboo pole.
"After all … he is my lover who has been with me for 100,000 years. His taste is the sweetest." The girl chuckled softly. She continued sucking on the skull with an intoxicated expression.
"Truly perverted." The bamboo pole grumbled.
At this moment, a unique energy ripple appeared below.
Even though it was thousands of miles away, the ghost-like ship could faintly feel it.
The girl in the black gown tilted her head slightly. Her eyes revealed a strand of doubt. That bamboo pole felt it too.
"It seems to be Dao-fusion energy?" The girl in the black gown asked.
"It doesn't seem to be, it is. Moreover, this person has suppressed the Dao-fusion energy to the twelfth layer. The Dao-fusion energy's degree of perfection is also very high." The bamboo pole said with certainty.
"There are still people who cultivate this nowadays. I'm very curious … should I go down and take a look?" The girl in the black gown asked.
"Up to you." The bamboo pole shook his head. "As long as you don't delay the captain's matters."
And so, the girl in the black gown jumped down from the ship. Thousands of kilometers in the sky, she emitted a dense aura of death. She was like a comet that brought death as she fell straight towards Nether Mountain.
Below Nether Mountain was an Asura purgatory. The smell of blood soared to the sky.
The only race that had survived in the 33 Caves was the human race.
"The scenery of this Abyssal Mountain is beautiful, and it's also a pretty good place to cultivate. I've already exterminated the other alien races. If you submit to me, I can forgive you and protect you." The fiend said to the leader of the nineteen Paradise.
"What crime have we committed that you have to forgive us?"
The leader thought bitterly in his heart. But how could he dare to say this out loud? With this fiend's personality, as soon as he opened his mouth, he would most likely die.
Just as the leader hurriedly nodded, a dense aura of death swept over from above …
The moment the aura of death infected the blood and severed limbs on the ground, they seemed to gain a strange vitality.
"Bzzz …"
The blood began to flow into the severed limbs.
Whether it was red, black, brown, or blue blood, it was the same.
The countless severed limbs began to join together. These severed limbs included heads, arms, mouthparts, bone blades, and so on.
As they continued to combine, they formed a huge monster.
This monster's appearance was so ugly that just looking at it would make one feel nauseous.

Chaotic Origin Reconstruction
This black-robed woman was extremely beautiful, but she emitted an aura that made people uneasy.

"In the past, there were a total of seven methods to use Dao Fusion energy. The most perfect ones were the Fusion God Sword Scripture and the Transformation God Fusion Technique. "Which peerless expert created another one? It shouldn't be … The Fusion God path has been proven to be unworkable. No one would be unfaithful to it. Could it be that he created it himself? "
After deducing, the black-robed woman's eyes revealed a hint of surprise.
At this moment, the fusion monster under her feet had already been blasted into a huge hole by the fiend. A few more attacks and it would shatter into pieces again.
"I want to see … your true strength." The black-robed woman lightly tapped the ground with the tip of her foot.
Black death energy wandered within the severed limbs. The fusion monster's strength increased explosively. A violent aura was emitted from the fusion monster.
At this moment, the Fiend's face finally changed quite a bit!
"Just this little bit is not enough for me to fight!"
The fiend roared, and Dao Fusion energy erupted once again.
The crescent-shaped Dao Fusion energy changed and became a rhombus. The surface of the rhombus was divided into layers.
The woman in the black dress glanced around and counted the levels. Her eyes were filled with shock again. "There are actually 21 levels …"
The level of the new moon-shaped energy could not be seen at a glance, and one could only comprehend it. This meant that this fellow had more than enough control over it. However, he should have realized that the higher the level, the harder it was to control the energy. After exceeding the twelfth level, he could only change his shape, and he could not hide the level he contained.
This method looked simple and clumsy, but it was actually effective and powerful. More importantly, this simple method was definitely something that he had figured out himself!
Just as the woman in the black dress was about to increase the strength of the fused monster again, a deafening explosion rang out.
The entire fusion monster had been reduced to ashes …
What the woman in the black dress saw was a blazing white light.
Before her eyesight had recovered, a figure had already pounced on her. One hand twisted her neck, and a knee directly hit her lower abdomen.
"Bang!"
Then, the woman in the black dress was pressed to the ground.
The malefic star did not hesitate at all. He knew that this woman's strength was beyond his imagination.
If he wanted to defeat her, he could only catch her off guard!
"You're so beautiful. You're suitable to be my woman. Hahaha … However, what a pity!"
As he laughed, he formed a rhombus-shaped Dao Fusion energy. Without any hesitation, he viciously stabbed toward the woman in the black dress' face.
"29 levels? Heavens! How many levels can this guy push the Dao Fusion energy to … "
The woman in the black dress did not resist. She did not move. All of her attention was focused on the Dao Fusion energy. It was as if she had already disregarded life and death. Or rather, she did not care about her own life at all.
"Boom!"
The blazing white light exploded again.
In the malefic star's eyes, such a terrifying power was enough to destroy everything.
No matter how powerful this woman was, she could not withstand this attack.
However, when the light disappeared, the woman's peerlessly beautiful face appeared in front of the malefic star unscathed.
She was not injured in the slightest …
Her long hair, which looked like it had been stretched out by a pendant, was scattered on the ground. Not a single strand was broken.
There was not a single scar on her fair face. It was as if that previous attack did not exist at all.
After appreciating the astonished expression of this male, the woman in the black dress chuckled: "Have you heard of the Chaotic Origin Recreations?"
She did not mind this male pressing her down. Instead, she continued explaining as if she was telling a story, "When you break through to a certain level in the Paramita Realm, there will be a limit to what you can endure. No matter who it is, you will not be able to take another step forward. Otherwise, your fleshy body will be crushed by the Paramita Energy. If you want to continue advancing in the Paramita Realm, you have to undergo the Chaotic Origin Recreations. At that time, your fleshy body will be strengthened to a terrifying level …"
"You … have undergone the Chaotic Origin Recreations before?" That malefic star coldly asked.
"Mn." The woman in the black dress chuckled: "Hence, even if you are stronger now, you are like an ant biting on metal. You can't injure me at all …"
Hearing those words, that malefic star felt as if he had fallen into an ice cellar.
How high was his talent?
It's a pity that he had been trapped in his inner world and knew too little about this Great World.
The level mentioned by the woman in the black dress had completely exceeded the scope of his imagination and understanding …
But even so, the malefic star did not have any intention of begging for mercy. His eyes were like that of a snake as he stared coldly at this woman. It's just that his full-strength attack could not injure this woman at all. For a moment, he did not know what to do.
The body of the woman in the black dress was just like a snake. She gently twisted her body. That arm of hers seemed to be boneless as it coiled around the body of this malefic star.
That seemingly soft body contained a strength that did not allow him to resist.
Not long after, she actually pushed the man to the ground. She lowered her head to look at the man and said with a smile, "You've got guts … You're far from qualified to make me your woman. How about you become my disciple?"
This malefic star did not expect that she would suddenly make such a request. If it was any other person, that person would most likely agree in a hurry under such circumstances. But he gave a sinister smile, "The world gave birth to me with Evil Deities. I have learned it without a teacher. Nobody in this world can be my teacher or father! You can just kill me! "
"Evil Deities …"

Chapter 2800
Cumbersome Transactions
The last time he came to the Sky Sunflower Temple, there were already three groups of people waiting here.
The disciples of those small factions had crossed the other shore. These people might have some Soul Pills in their hands, but even a First Heaven Paramita Key was not something that could be easily obtained. They were naturally happy to try their luck at the entrance of the various temples.
It was normal for some people to wait here for ten days to half a month, or even a month or two.
This time, there were actually more than a hundred people waiting here!
With the appearance of Luo Zheng, everyone's gazes fell on him.
A physical body appearing on the other shore was very eye-catching no matter what. These people whispered to each other, discussing the weirdo Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng walked directly to the entrance of the Sky Sunflower Temple. These people swarmed over, wanting to follow him.
Seeing this, Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
Although no one could take away the things in the crack at the top of the temple, he still did not want anyone to see it.
"Brother, can you open the temple?" a middle-aged man asked in a steady tone.
This group of Yang Souls naturally understood their own weight, so they were very polite to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng turned his head and swept a glance over these people …
He was afraid that when he opened the Sky Sunflower Temple, they would swarm in.
"I can open it," Luo Zheng answered.
Hearing Luo Zheng's answer, everyone was delighted.
The days of waiting here were boring. Finally, there was someone who could open the door, so they were naturally happy. Although this person was very strange, standing here with his physical body, it did not affect their exploration of the temple.
"That's naturally good, hehe!" the middle-aged man laughed. The others also rubbed their palms.
"But only I can enter the Sky Sunflower Temple. Don't follow me," Luo Zheng said again.
The smiles on everyone's faces immediately froze.
"This … Friend, the Sky Sunflower Temple is so big. If we all go in together, we won't get in each other's way …"
"That's right. One Paramita Key for only you to enter. Isn't that too wasteful?"
"Brother, we are of the same race. It's convenient for each of us …"
These people immediately begged.
Hearing these pleas, how could Luo Zheng waver?
However, when he took a look at this dilapidated temple, his heart skipped a beat. He then said, "None of you have fused with the Paramita Token, right?"
"Yeah, we just entered the Nirvana Realm not long ago."
"I've only been here for two months. I haven't been able to find a suitable Nirvana token."
"…"
Luo Zheng then said, "Look, how about using the bricks of this temple as your Nirvana token?"
Although the Sky Sunflower Temple was a temple in the First Heaven, the bricks used to build the temple also contained the power of the Nirvana Realm, and it was not weak.
At the very least, it was far stronger than those Nirvana Tokens in the first heaven …
Luo Zheng had planned to knock down these bricks, and now it was an opportunity.
"These bricks?"
"Can you knock it down?"
"If you can get a brick, I'll give you a hundred soul pills in exchange!"
"I'll give you two hundred soul pills!"
When the temple was dilapidated, there were always people who were lucky enough to get one or two bricks from the temple as their Nirvana token. However, such opportunities were too rare. Although the Sky Sunflower Temple was dilapidated, not a single brick could be dug down.
These people also realized that it was possible. After all, Luo Zheng had a real physical body!
After a few bids, someone had already bid five hundred soul pills.
They were willing to wait bitterly in front of the temple, but they were not rich people.
Under the watchful eyes of the crowd, Luo Zheng walked to a corner of the temple, clenched his fist, and began to circulate his power.
"Bang!"
With a muffled sound, a crack appeared on the brick at the edge of the temple.
"Bang!"
After the second punch, the brick was completely broken.
Luo Zheng held up the broken half of the brick and said, "How about this, I won't charge too much for this brick. Three hundred soul pills will do."
Almost as soon as he finished speaking, a person rushed in front of Luo Zheng, and a bunch of soul pills gushed out from his Yang Soul. "Three hundred soul pills, I'll take it!"
These people had a rough judgment of the energy in the Nirvana Realm. The power of the Nirvana Realm contained in this half of the brick was at least five or six Jun. It was already considered a top-grade Nirvana Token in the first heaven.
Luo Zheng held the brick and waved it in front of the person. "I have one more condition. I'll enter the temple in a while. You guys can't enter. You have to guard the door for me."
"Okay!" The person agreed quickly.
These people entered the temple to look for the Nirvana Token. This half of the brick satisfied them. Naturally, they had no motivation to enter the temple, not to mention that there were many dangers in the temple.
Next, Luo Zheng began to knock on the brick …
He had fused with the Mind's Eye and obtained the help of the Fearful Eye Demon Clan. He didn't really want to tear down the temple, so he carefully knocked on the brick along the periphery, knocking out half of it at a time, and then exchanging it for soul pills.
Not everyone could take out so many soul pills. Faced with such an opportunity, some people quickly left the Nirvana Realm and tried to find a way to get more soul pills.
As for those who got the brick, they couldn't wait to fuse it.
But after more than ten transactions, Luo Zheng quickly encountered a new problem.
There were too many soul pills, and he had no place to store them.
There was a Sumeru Ring in the mother world. No matter how big an item was, it could be stuffed in casually.
The body of the Yang Soul could also hold some things. For example, the few candles were stored in Luo Zheng's Yang Soul.
Luo Zheng couldn't fuse these soul pills into his body. Previously, he had Ling Shuang's help, but he hadn't entered the Nirvana Realm for so long. Ling Shuang might have left long ago.
Among these people, there was no one he could trust. If these people took the soul pills and fled the Nirvana Realm, Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to do anything.
In fact, the transactions in the Nirvana Realm were extremely risky. The Rat Alliance and some large merchant alliances would have a comprehensive set of methods to deal with them. Luo Zheng didn't have such conditions, so he had to think of another way.
Luo Zheng thought for a moment and stopped knocking on the brick. He placed more than two thousand soul pills at the entrance of the Sky Sunflower Temple. At the same time, he said, "Those who didn't get the brick, help me guard these soul pills. I'll be back in a while."
The remaining people couldn't wait to buy the brick. Although the pile of more than two thousand soul pills wasn't a small amount, the square brick of the temple was more attractive to them. Moreover, if they snatched the soul pills, they would offend Luo Zheng. Naturally, no one was willing to do so.
After Luo Zheng left the inner world, he took the Nirvana Key in his hand and chanted according to the Sanskrit words that Luo Nian had deciphered for him. Traces of lines flowed continuously in his palm.
After activating the Nirvana Key, Luo Zheng once again appeared in front of the temple.
He placed his hand on the door of the Sky Sunflower Temple. After opening the temple, he moved all the soul pills into a hidden corner of the temple. Then, he went to knock on the brick to carry out the transaction.
After repeating this several times, most of the hundred people obtained their own square brick.
According to their previous agreement, they had to help Luo Zheng guard the entrance of the Sky Sunflower Temple.
After entering the temple, Luo Zheng once again left the Nirvana Realm.
This time, he activated the True Meaning of Dao and let his Yang Soul escape to the Nirvana Realm.
Those people didn't know that Luo Zheng's physical body had already left, so they should be diligently guarding the temple. Luo Zheng could enter the temple with his Yang Soul Body and take the soul pills.
However, just as Luo Zheng's Yang Soul escaped to the Nirvana Realm, Ling Shuang's Yang Soul followed closely behind.

Continuous Ascension

Along the way, there were some Sun Souls accompanying them. Now, they were heading to the same destination as Luo Zheng.
After walking for another four hours, Luo Zheng saw the third temple.
According to Ling Shuang, the Nirvana Key of this temple was extremely rare. No one had heard of anyone entering it.
With the strength of Luo Zheng's body, he might be able to break through the temple's door and force his way in. But when he thought of the race behind these temples, Luo Zheng gave up the idea.
Moreover, his main purpose this time was to grow quickly in the Nirvana Realm. Lingering in the First Heaven was meaningless.
One day and one night later …
Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang's Sun Souls finally reached the end of the path. Here, Luo Zheng saw tens of thousands of Sun Souls sitting there.
In front of those Sun Souls, there was a large white star ring floating.
Luo Zheng could feel the power contained in the white star ring even before he got close to it.
This kind of power was somewhat similar to the chill of the Sea of True Intent, but its power was several times stronger. If Luo Zheng, who had just stepped into the Sea of True Intent, came here, he would not be able to get close to this white star ring.
However, after absorbing a large amount of soul crystals in the Sky Sunflower Temple, Luo Zheng's Sun Soul had become much stronger. Although there was no change in its nature, it was still considered a Sun Soul and was several times stronger than an ordinary Sun Soul.
"The First Layer of Heaven and the Second Layer of Heaven are connected by the Star Ring. If you want to go to the Second Layer of Heaven, you need to enter the Star Ring," Ling Shuang said to Luo Zheng.
While Luo Zheng was still watching, a few Sun Souls stood up and entered the white Star Ring.
When these Sun Souls entered, the white Star Ring began to spin faster and faster. At the same time, something seemed to be gushing out from the Star Ring.
"Hua …"
After a few breaths, the white Star Ring suddenly trembled.
The few Sun Souls that had just entered were directly thrown out and smashed to the ground.
"We failed," Ling Shuang said, shaking her head.
After passing through the white Star Ring, one could pass through the First Layer of Heaven and enter the Second Layer of Heaven. However, these people's Sun Souls were clearly not strong enough.
The Sun Souls that were thrown out were on the verge of death and were heavily injured. Although this place was not as dangerous as the Sea of True Intent, failure would still have a price to pay.
"It's our turn," Ling Shuang said.
Luo Zheng nodded and followed behind Ling Shuang, walking towards the white Star Ring.
This was not the first time Ling Shuang had passed through the white Star Ring, so it was naturally very easy for her. Luo Zheng was also very confident.
After the two of them entered the white Star Ring, the Star Ring also began to spin at high speed.
"Hua …"
Under the crazy rotation, Luo Zheng almost felt that his soul was about to be crushed.
He could not release his divine sense, nor could he investigate his surroundings. Everything became chaotic.
After a while, the white Star Ring once again spewed out a ray of white light. This ray of light flew up at a vertical angle …
When the others saw this scene, they also whispered to each other.
They were not envious. After all, whether it was Luo Zheng or Ling Shuang, the soul aura they emitted was too strong. They thought that Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang had descended from the upper Nirvana Realm. They could not be envious of such people.
That ray of white light wrapped around Ling Shuang and Luo Zheng's Sun Souls and flew up.
After about the time it took to burn an incense stick, Luo Zheng felt his Sun Soul begin to recover. He once again transformed into a human form, and his divine sense also returned to normal.
When he opened his eyes and looked around, he was actually in a huge square.
There were even more Sun Souls in this square. There were actually more than 100,000 of them. In the center of the square, there was also a white Star Ring, but the aura this white Star Ring emitted was even more fierce. It was more than three times stronger than the First Heaven.
Of course, the Sun Souls sitting in this square were also much stronger than the Sun Souls in the First Heaven.
"This is the Second Heaven?" Luo Zheng asked. Su Kuan had been trapped in the Second Heaven. He did not know if he was also in this huge square.
"Yes, this is the Second Supreme Brightness Heaven." Ling Shuang nodded. "Luo Zheng, if you are tired, shall we rest first?"
"Let's continue," Luo Zheng said.
To Luo Zheng, going from the First Heaven to the Second Heaven was almost no challenge at all. How could he be tired at all?
He also understood why Ling Shuang could reach the Fourth Heaven of the Nirvana Realm in such a short time … When the Sun Soul was strong to a certain extent, it was too easy to cross the First Heaven.
However, to prevent any accidents from happening, Luo Zheng replenished several Soul Elixirs. The two of them walked towards the Star Ring again.
Just like the First Heaven Star Ring before, Luo Zheng felt his divine sense being sealed again. His Sun Soul was also crushed. Immediately after, he began to ascend again.
Not long after, the two of them arrived at the Third Heaven of Purity and Innocence.
Going from the First Heavenly Layer to the Third Heavenly Layer in one go was already a very rare thing, and it could not be done without a powerful soul. However, the first base of the Heavenly Palace was in the Fourth Heavenly Layer, so the two naturally would not stop here.
However, the aura contained in the Third Heaven's white Star Radiance already faintly threatened Luo Zheng. Naturally, they did not enter blindly.
Ling Shuang's first attempt to break through the Fourth Heaven also took half a month to rest and reorganize.
However, Luo Zheng only stayed for two days. The two of them replenished dozens of Soul Elixirs and stepped into the Star Ring again to break through to the Fourth Heaven of the Dark Embryo Heaven.

Chapter 2803

Trust
Luo Zheng looked around and realized that the architectural styles of these temples were different.
The temples that Luo Zheng saw in the First Heaven had very different styles. It was as if they were not of this era.
After all, every race had their own unique aesthetic standards. Naturally, the style of their buildings was very different.
However, although the four temples in front of him were different, they were all built in the style of humans.

The races in the mother world were very diverse. Being able to build their first temple in the Fourth Heaven was a proof of their strength.

The Jiuli tribe …
Three of the four orthodoxies of the human race were gone.

In the early days, the Human Race was weak. In order to survive, the four orthodox Human Race had huddled together. At that time, all the Human Race was one family. It was also a precious honeymoon period.
The Four Divine Temples of the Human Race were built at that time.
Not only did the Four Divine Temples exist in the Fourth Layer of Heaven, the Human Race also had Four Divine Temples in the upper Nirvana Realm.
But this honeymoon period did not last long. The biggest conflict was naturally the conflict between the Jiuli Tribe and the You Xiong Tribe.
The conflict between the two tribes was not enough to affect the entire Human Race.
At that time, the Taiyi Heavenly Palace was also a bystander. Donghuang thought that the conflict between the You Xiong Tribe and the Jiuli Tribe would not develop to an irreversible stage.
After all, the Human Race was not the only powerful race in the Mother World. After a long conflict, the other races would only benefit from it. Even Chiyou thought so.
The war between the two tribes was very similar to the war between the Taiyi Heavenly Palace and the Jinwu. The flames of war were only controlled in one prefecture. Rather than a war, it was more like a trial ground for the two tribes.
But the addition of the Lie Shan Shennong changed the situation.
The Jiuli Tribe was forced to retreat under the pincer attack of the two orthodox Human Race forces. Chiyou's defeat shook the Mother World. After all, it was not easy for a superpower to be born, and it was also not easy to be destroyed. But the alliance of Lie Shan and Dihong Tribe did it.
As for the Jiuli Tribe's Divine Temple in the Nirvana Realm, it was naturally taken over by the You Xiong Tribe.
"What was the reaction of the Taiyi Heavenly Palace after the Jiuli Tribe was destroyed?" Luo Zheng asked.
"As far as I know, Donghuang did not say anything. But he went to Li Mountain," Ling Shuang said.
Luo Zheng naturally understood when this was mentioned.
The conflict between the Human Race was clear. It was because they chose different paths.
These top experts cultivated to become demons. They were people with great perseverance and ambition. They would not easily change their Dao!
Donghuang knew that he could not fight against the alliance of the Dihong Tribe and Lie Shan Tribe alone. If he did not join them, he would have to find a new ally.
And Li Mountain had chosen the same path as the Taiyi Heavenly Palace.
Luo Zheng had learned this from the mouth of the Autumn Yin River. Now that he had confirmed it with Ling Shuang for the second time, Luo Zheng felt even more reassured.
He had chosen to join the Taiyi Heavenly Palace by accident. It was a very correct path.
9527 probably knew these things as well, but it had never said anything. Instead, it had subtly guided him to the right path.
The two whispered to each other as they walked to the square in front of the temples.
There was originally a statue erected at the entrance of each of the four divine temples. In front of the divine temple belonging to the Greater One Sky Palace was a giant sword, in front of the divine temple belonging to the You Xiong Clan was a giant bear that was extraordinarily domineering, and in front of the divine temple belonging to the Lie Shan Clan was a giant ruler.
As for the last statue in front of the temple, it had been destroyed. Only a pair of legs could be seen.
Even though Luo Zheng had never been here before, he knew that the statue erected there should have been a statue of Chiyou with three heads and six arms. Now that it had been destroyed, he could only sigh.
But Luo Zheng's greatest interest was the giant ruler in front of the temple of the Lie Shan Tribe.
The giant ruler was beautifully carved, and its appearance was not much different from his Infinite Ruler.
"What is the origin of the ruler in front of the temple?" Luo Zheng asked.
"The Shennong Ruler," Ling Shuang answered casually. This was common knowledge, but Luo Zheng had come from outside the mother world, so Ling Shuang was used to his ignorance.
"The Shennong Ruler … I wonder what connection it has with the Infinite Ruler," Luo Zheng muttered to himself. But on second thought, since the Shennong Ruler had been carved into a statue and worshipped by the Lie Shan Tribe, it must have an extraordinary origin. He was probably overthinking things.
When the two reached the gate of the Heavenly Palace, two people stopped them. "If you are a disciple of the Heavenly Palace, we need to verify your identity!"

Chapter 2804
A gift from the Heavenly Palace

There were only three major sword sects on Greater One Mountain, and the cultivation method of the Heart Sword Sect was the most unique. Ling Shuang had probably heard of it, and the meaning of her words could not be more clear.
But Ling Shuang naturally did not know that there were not only two servant girls serving in the independent courtyard, but also Luo Zheng's female family members.

After Xiaoyin left, Luo Zheng used the method of using the other side key to recite the syllables on the small token. Like the other side key, several lines appeared on his palm.
But these lines were not as complicated as the other side key.
When Luo Zheng returned to the Heavenly Palace Temple again, there were more lines on his palm, which was the unique soul mark of the Greater One Heavenly Palace.
After the two people in front of the temple verified the lines, they naturally let him pass.
Ling Shuang waited patiently at the door. After Luo Zheng entered the temple, she didn't ask about the maid, which made Luo Zheng feel relieved.
There were quite a few people in the Divine Temple, and the vast majority of them had Yang souls that contained the incisive and eternal true meaning of sword fate. Sensing these auras, Luo Zheng had the illusion that he was in the Heavenly Palace.
Under Ling Shuang's lead, the two of them entered the door on the left side of the temple.
Inside the door was a wall made of bronze. In the middle of the bronze wall, there was a relief sculpture of a bronze longsword.
Before Ling Shuang and Luo Zheng went in, there were already several Heavenly Palace disciples inside.
One of the Heavenly Palace disciples stretched out his hand and patted the bronze longsword carving. The surface of the bronze longsword began to brew with a rich soul aura. Then, rice-sized white dots of light began to drip down from the tip of the green-red longsword.
"Soul Crystal!"
When Luo Zheng saw the small white dots of light, he quickly made a judgment.
When they were in the Sky Sunflower Temple, Ling Shuang had told Luo Zheng that soul crystals were very rare. After all, this thing was different from soul pills. Soul crystals could directly increase the power of the Yang soul!
But wasn't the amount of soul crystals in front of them too little? It was only the size of a grain of rice …
Even so, the Heavenly Palace disciple seemed very happy. He carefully absorbed the small white dots of light into his Yang soul and then retreated to the side in satisfaction.
Next, several Heavenly Palace disciples also patted their hands on the bronze longsword. They could also obtain soul crystals, and each Heavenly Palace disciple obtained a different amount of soul crystals.
Some people's soul crystals were like the tip of a needle. If one didn't look carefully, it was almost impossible to notice. Some people's soul crystals were slightly more.
"You can obtain soul crystals in the temple. This is according to the evaluation of the Heavenly Palace disciples. If it's Tai Yiwei, it's according to their merits. The difference is very big," Ling Shuang explained.
"How many did you get?" Luo Zheng asked.
Ling Shuang only smiled and didn't answer.
However, after Ling Shuang returned from the Fourth Heaven to the First Heaven, her soul aura had indeed become much stronger. It seemed that she had obtained a lot of soul crystals.
After waiting for a while, it was finally Luo Zheng's turn.
The people from before didn't leave. They also wanted to see how many soul crystals Luo Zheng could obtain.
As Luo Zheng reached out and patted the bronze longsword lightly, the sword flashed. A wisp of white light swept down the blade and gathered at the tip of the sword.
As the soul crystals gathered, they formed a white dot of light. The white dot of light continued to expand and finally became the size of a fist!
The Heavenly Palace disciples who stayed behind to watch were so shocked that their eyeballs almost fell out when they saw the fist-sized soul crystals.
"So many soul crystals?"
"Is this cheating? Such a rare thing. How can he obtain so many at once? "
"Who … is this person?"
The amount of soul crystals Luo Zheng received was dozens of times more than the average person.
"It seems that the Heart Flow Sword Sect values you very much." Ling Shuang was also a little surprised when she saw the fist-sized soul crystals.
She had begged her grandmother to obtain the soul mark. With her grandmother's status, the Heavenly Palace would give her the maximum amount of resources.
Even so, the amount of soul crystals she received from the temple was about the same as Luo Zheng's.
Luo Zheng also knew the reason.
His performance in the Heart Flow Pagoda was enough to make the Heart Flow Sword Sect value him. Otherwise, he wouldn't have been able to obtain so many soul crystals.
However, the soul crystals Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang absorbed in the Sky Sunflower Temple were the size of watermelons. In the face of this fist-sized soul crystal, Luo Zheng's heart was naturally calm.
Under the envious gazes of the Heavenly Palace disciples, Luo Zheng absorbed the soul crystal.
Ling Shuang took Luo Zheng out of the door and brought him to the next room.
There was a Sun Soul stationed in this room, but this Sun Soul wasn't in the form of a human. Instead, it was a huge mutated beast. This mutated beast's Sun Soul exuded a rich True Meaning of Void Disillusionment.
"That's a Void Beast. Go, it will help you forge a Sumeru Ring." Ling Shuang pushed Luo Zheng.
Not all mutated races were enemies of the Human Race. Some mutated races were few in number but had some unique talents. They would also choose to be protected by some big clans. This Void Beast was one of them.
When Luo Zheng approached the Void Beast, he heard a strange voice. "Extend your finger."
As Luo Zheng extended his hand, the Void Beast lowered its head. Two sharp tentacles extended from the top of its head and surrounded Luo Zheng's middle finger.
After a while, a bright frame appeared on Luo Zheng's fingertip.
This frame was exactly the same as Ling Shuang's. It was a Sumeru Ring used in the Nirvana Realm. Luo Zheng had used the Sumeru Ring for many years, so he quickly became familiar with how to use it.
After leaving the room, Ling Shuang transferred the soul pills obtained from the Sky Sunflower Temple, as well as the metal flower branch, into Luo Zheng's Sumeru Ring.
"No wonder so many people want to join the Heavenly Palace. There are so many benefits after entering the Fourth Layer," Luo Zheng sighed as he looked at the frame on his fingertip.

Token of Choice

The Four Categories

Chapter 2814
Translator: Transn Editor: Transn
A special cyclone.
During this period of time, Luo Zheng encountered a strange problem when he was studying the Heart Sword Technique.
The Heart Sword Technique was very different from other supreme sword techniques. It was a method to cultivate the mind. Moreover, there were several extremely powerful killing moves recorded in the Heart Sword Technique, but there was no specific method to cultivate them.
According to the records, it seemed that if one stayed in the Heart Flow Pagoda long enough, they would be able to comprehend the moves on their own. Therefore, Luo Zheng had not cultivated a single remarkable ability after studying it for so long.
However, the Sanskrit that Luo Zheng had just recited could transform one's mind into a sword. It was somewhat similar to the "Heart Killing Sword" in the Heart Sword Technique. Luo Zheng did not know what the connection was.
The translation written by Luo Nian was about a thousand words long.
Luo Zheng suppressed the wisp of sword light in his mind and continued to recite the words on it.
As he continued to recite, Luo Zheng clearly felt that the sword light accumulated in his mind was getting stronger and stronger.
When he recited the fiftieth word, the power contained in the sword light was so strong that Luo Zheng could not underestimate it. It was like an active volcano that could erupt at any time.
Once this sword light slashed out, the degree of destruction it would bring would probably be far greater than the one that had cut open Luo Nian's soul.
Luo Zheng frowned and had to stop. The sword light that had been accumulating slowly dispersed.
He had once comprehended the Dao of Nature and One Thought of Good and Evil. Although he was burdened with heavy pressure, he was still able to move forward step by step. Although the pressure was great, it was not fatal.
However, it was so dangerous to comprehend this Sanskrit. Had he made a mistake?
"Even a True God who does not have a good compatibility with the Sword Intent of Eternity would not face such a strong resistance when cultivating it for the first time. Could the starting point be wrong?"
"Those two Sanskrit words were originally behind the Sword Intent of Eternity. Must they be connected?"
Thinking of this, a light flashed in Luo Zheng's mind.
Therefore, he recited the Sword Intent of Eternity silently again …
Now, Luo Zheng was reciting extremely quickly without any hindrance. In about a hundred breaths, he had already recited the last word.
At the same time, Luo Zheng's gaze fell on the paper and he continued to recite the content that Luo Nian had deciphered.
"Buzz …"
This time, the sword gleam in the depths of his mind did not appear.
Instead, an invisible cyclone started to rise slowly. The moment this cyclone rose, Luo Zheng had a strange feeling in his heart.
"What is this …"
Although Luo Zheng couldn't identify the cyclone, he had a rough idea of what it was.
If he chanted directly from the two Sanskrit words, it would produce a sword light that would harm him. If he chanted along with the Eternal True Meaning of Sword Fate, it would produce a cyclone.
However, regardless of whether it was the cyclone or the sword light, the energy contained was about the same.
However, to Luo Zheng, this cyclone had become gentle and harmless …
Luo Zheng only paused for a moment before continuing to chant. As he continued to chant, the cyclone began to expand, and the pressure of the past once again descended.
After about a hundred words, Luo Zheng could no longer continue reading. Even with the strength of his current Yang Soul, he was still unable to bear the pressure brought about by the cyclone.
Feeling the cyclone in his mind, Luo Zheng's eyes showed excitement.
"It seems that the last two Sanskrit words are indeed the continuation of the Eternal True Meaning of Sword Fate!"
Whether it was cultivating the Eternal True Meaning of Sword Fate or the True Meaning of Nature, no special energy had been born. These True Meanings were essentially the way to use and understand energy, which was different from the Dao of Shinto.
Now that a new cyclone had been born, it naturally had a new meaning.
But how should this cyclone be used?
Luo Zheng repeatedly tried to get it out of his mind or transfer it to the long sword, but to no avail.
"Perhaps this cyclone is too weak and has not yet fully formed …"
In the next five days, Luo Zheng either tried to break through the limit of "a hundred words" or tried to use the cyclone. However, both were unsuccessful. But every time he tried, he could always feel the changes in the cyclone.
This change was not within Luo Zheng's control. He faintly felt that this cyclone was like a living thing. When the cyclone was dispersed, it even turned into countless tiny invisible things and began to wander around!
And each time, the path it took was generally similar, but Luo Zheng could not say why the cyclone was wandering like this.
Five days later, Li Can appeared in his courtyard again. Today was the day of the Seven Mountains Meeting.
Luo Zheng prepared and walked out of the courtyard with Li Can.
"Where is the Seven Mountains Meeting?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Brother Luo Zheng, please follow me," Li Can said with a smile.
Following these independent courtyards, they did not walk far to the right. Gao Qizheng and more than 20 people were waiting in front of an empty field.
An extremely exquisite flying boat was lying in the center of the field.

Sneak attack
