Incomprehensibly powerful
Caught in a three-pronged attack, everyone knew that the Heart Flow Sword Sect was in danger.
Ling Shuang sat on the boulder and sighed helplessly.
If not for Feng Ge, she could've convinced Song Feiyu to help, but Feng Ge's boulder attack was too fierce. Taiyi Mountain couldn't even take care of itself.
Even so, Ling Shuang still focused most of her attention on Luo Zheng.
The Heart Flow Sword Sect had miraculously turned the tables. Perhaps the situation wasn't as bad as she thought?
After Luo Zheng chanted a dozen words, the cyclone in his mind quietly took shape.
He'd been standing on two talisman arrays, constantly releasing true intent aura.
The cyclone's appearance caused a slight change in his true intent aura!
Hum!
The control talisman on his left foot and the acceleration talisman on his right began to shine brightly, and the patterns on the boulder flickered with strange light.
"This is …"
Luo Zheng was taken aback.
When he'd first stepped into the control talisman array, he'd felt that the array was similar to the Imperial Sword Seal. As long as he released his true intent aura, they would constantly demand it.
But now, the two talisman arrays demanded even more of his true intent aura!
In a depression on the upper level of the cave.
Qiu Yinhe suddenly stood up, not even noticing the scalding tea splashing on him. His eyes were fixed on the array on the boulder.
He Chi, Lin Zhanting, and the old man who'd given out the flags were fine. They just found it strange.
The metal mountain in the cave had been left behind in the past. The gravity talisman arrays and laws had been designed by He Chi, but the three arrays on the boulder had been created by Qiu Yinhe.
This game wasn't about who was stronger. Otherwise, a competition would've been enough. It tested the cooperation and cooperation of the elite disciples.
The three arrays Qiu Yinhe had created were actually variants of the Imperial Sword Seal. This way, the elites' strength would be limited to the same level.
Of course, because of the variant Imperial Sword Seal, the One Thought Dao Cultivators still had a considerable advantage.
But at this moment, Qiu Yinhe sensed something strange through the Imperial Sword Seal!
"Brother Qiu, what's wrong?" the old man asked curiously when he saw Qiu Yinhe's excitement.
Qiu Yinhe stared at Luo Zheng with an incomprehensible expression and said, "Luo Zheng's Eternal Sword Intent … has a problem!"
"Is there a problem?" The old man asked with a smile.
All the sword cultivators in this world comprehended the Sword Fate Eternal True Intent.
Whether it was in the depths of the chaos or in the mother world, not a word more or less would be tolerated.
In terms of problems, even the most ingenious Supreme Sword Scripture would have a flaw. Only the Sword Luck Eternal True Intent would not have a flaw. Or rather, all the True Intents of Dao would not have a flaw.
These True Intents of Dao were the only ways to utilize one's strength. Apart from following the prescribed order, there were no shortcuts.
Therefore, when He Chi and the others heard Qiu Yinhe's words, they also found it hard to understand.
Amidst this incomprehensible situation, the boulder under Luo Zheng's feet suddenly began to flutter!
Because the boulder itself was heavy, even if everyone worked together to increase its speed, it still looked a little cumbersome.
However, at this moment, Luo Zheng's boulder became as light as a butterfly.
The moment the boulder leapt high into the air, the elites of the Murdering Sword Sect and Absolute Formation Sword Sect all opened their mouths wide. Time seemed to have slowed down at this moment. A question flashed through their minds. How did this guy do it?
Before this question could be answered, the boulder in the sky suddenly shot down.
"Peng, peng!"
The boulder's mass coupled with the inertia from the acceleration created a terrifying impact.
The Murdering Sword Sect and Absolute Formation Sword Sect did not dodge at all and were hit by Luo Zheng's boulder.
"Boom!"
"Boom …"
Without any suspense, the boulder from the Murdering Sword Sect and Absolute Formation Sword Sect crashed into the ground.
Even though Sang Hou ordered people to open the barrier around the boulder at the last moment, the barrier was still crushed by the impact of the boulder. It was unable to save the Murdering Sword Sect.
The elite disciples of the two major sword sects rolled all over the ground. Sang Hou and the rest were lying on the ground, looking at the seven people from the Heart Flow Sword Sect in the air. Their faces were filled with shock.
"They … they cheated!"
"How can it be so agile? How can it have such a strong impact? "
"This boulder can't have been tampered with …"
"They might have used a special Paramita Token!"
Some Paramita Tokens had strange abilities that could catch people off guard. However, this was against the rules!
In fact, Gao Qizheng had used his Paramita Token to hit the boulder back. This was also against the rules. It could be considered a loophole in the rules, so Ling Xi and the rest did not pursue the matter.
When Sang Hou heard this speculation, he immediately became excited. He jumped up and shouted at Ling Xi on the metal mountain, "They cheated! The Heart Flow Sword Sect cheated! We have to do it again! "
Ling Xi and the rest of the people in the Land of Sword of Grief were also stunned. She also found it hard to imagine that the remaining seven people could control the boulder so nimbly and explosively with such speed.
"I heard that there is a mysterious Paramita Token that can turn material into nothingness and reduce its weight," said a tall man beside Ling Xi.
"If they really turned the boulder into nothingness, it's impossible for it to have such a strong impact. However, they might have used other abilities," another person nodded and said.
Ling Xi could not see the problem and could only look up at the top of the cave.
She knew that He Chi and the rest were watching the battle from above. If there was really something going on, they would probably step in.
Just as she looked up, He Chi's voice rang in her ears, "No one cheated. Let them continue."
Ling Xi nodded and made a gesture of denial to Sang Hou and the rest. "No one cheated. You can leave now!"
"Then how do you explain that guy's speed?" Sang Hou's face was filled with dissatisfaction.
"Yes! That's not normal at all! "
"Even an Enlightened One can't do that! We have to start the competition again! "
The two major sword sects had been eliminated by Luo Zheng in an instant. How could they be satisfied? Some of them even walked towards the boulder on the ground. They really wanted to return to the competition.
When Lingxi saw this, her eyes narrowed slightly. With her thumb and little finger connected, strands of invisible threads suddenly appeared around those people.
"Hiss …"
In the blink of an eye, these people were tightly bound by the threads and pulled up the metal mountain, hanging upside down in front of Ling Xi.
"You dare to question me? Get out of here, "Ling Xi spat out a few words from her thin lips. Then, those people flew backward like kites and smashed into the mountain wall.
Those who came out of the Land of Sword of Grief did not have good tempers.

Chapter 2824
Give Up
Gao Qizheng and the other elites of the Heartflow Sword Sect sat on one side of the cliff, looking at the sorry state of Sang Hou and the others with a meaningful smile.
The Murderous Sword Sect and the Formation Sword Sect were of the same origin as Greater One Mountain, but these two sword sects had been targeting the Heartflow Sword Sect for a long time.
After Luo Zheng's miraculous performance, Gao Qizheng and the others recovered from their shock and felt much better.
"Gao Qizheng! What are you laughing at?! "Sang Hou looked at Gao Qizheng's happy face with anger.
Gao Qizheng shrugged. "I'm laughing at some people who shot themselves in the foot!"
"Do you … have a death wish?" Sang Hou's eyes flashed.
Gao Qizheng looked relaxed. "This isn't a good place to fight. Let's go somewhere else and I'll take you on anytime."
In terms of strength, Gao Qizheng didn't fear this group of people.
Whether it was his cultivation or his Paramita token, he was at the top of the three major sword sects, but his potential wasn't as good as Luo Zheng and Mo Yijian's.
Thinking of Gao Qizheng's move, Sang Hou gave up. He snorted and led the others to lean against the cliff, cursing in his heart.
In the last Seven Mountains Meeting, they had been crushed by that damned Mo Yijian. This time, they thought that since Mo Yijian wasn't here, the Heartflow Sword Sect would be a soft persimmon for them to crush. But this time, another guy had popped out of nowhere, and this guy was even more abnormal than Mo Yijian …
When they looked up, they saw that the Heartflow Sword Sect's boulder had changed direction and was flying toward Taihao Mountain's boulder.
"Taihao Mountain is in trouble!"
"Xu Youwei's fate won't be much better than ours."
"… …"
After Luo Zheng dealt with the Murderous Sword Sect and the Formation Sword Sect, the boulder suddenly turned around and sped away.
Xu Youwei had caused trouble for Luo Zheng multiple times, so he naturally wouldn't let him go!
Luo Zheng didn't intend to reveal the remaining words of the Eternal Sword Intent so quickly, but Qiu Yinhe must have known about him renting the yellow paper from the Sword Intent Pavilion.
Since he had already used it, he might as well kick Xu Youwei out of the game.
The dozen or so people from Taihao Mountain were terrified when they saw the Heart Flow Sword Sect's boulder turn around.
"That guy is coming!"
"At this speed, there's no way to avoid it!"
"Young Master Youwei! What should we do? "
Xu Youwei's face was as gloomy as water. How could he have thought that the situation would change so quickly?
From Luo Zheng's performance in controlling the boulder, Xu Youwei understood that he had underestimated Luo Zheng. Even if this guy's compatibility with the Eternal Sword Intent was not perfect, it was not far from it.
But now, Luo Zheng's performance had already exceeded Xu Youwei's understanding. Even a single thought Dao Comprehender could not be so relaxed!
Naturally, no one could answer the confusion in his heart. Right now, he had to avoid this fatal counterattack.
Since there was no way to escape, Xu Youwei shouted, "Lu Kuang! You control it! "
Then he stepped onto the third talisman array, and a barrier suddenly appeared on the boulder.
Although Xu Youwei could not comprehend the Dao with a single thought, his compatibility with the Eternal Sword Intent was close to perfect. The moment he stepped onto the array, an extremely thick barrier appeared.
"Boom!"
As soon as the barrier was activated, Luo Zheng's boulder came crashing down on his head.
But this collision did not shatter the barrier.
Xu Youwei fell down with the boulder. When the boulder hit the ground, the barrier displayed astonishing toughness. The boulder that had activated the barrier was like a bouncy rubber ball, forcibly bouncing up!
"Not bad, but how long … can you last?"
Luo Zheng's expression was calm. With a slight thought, he flew in the direction of Taihao Mountain's boulder and smashed it down again.
Xu Youwei gritted his teeth and desperately urged his True Intent aura. The originally dim barrier instantly recovered.
"Boom!"
The force of the second collision was even greater, and the boulder fell even more fiercely.
When it landed again, Xu Youwei's barrier was extremely compressed. An elite disciple of Taihao Mountain could not dodge in time, and his head hit the ground and he fainted.
Even so, the boulder did not touch the ground. It stubbornly bounced off the ground and into the air.
The third time, when he saw Luo Zheng fall from the sky with the boulder, Xu Youwei could not hold on any longer. His face was dispirited, and he sighed helplessly. "Everyone, give up …"
As he spoke, he pushed off the boulder with his feet.
After the thrilling torment just now, the other disciples of Taihao Mountain were also holding on. Such a violent collision could have seriously injured them. But Young Master Youwei was still bitterly holding on. How could they give up?
Now that Xu Youwei had given the order, they immediately left the boulder and let it smash into the ground.
Taihao Mountain, eliminated.
Xu Youwei, who was standing in the air, stared at Luo Zheng. His fists were tightly clenched, and the anger in his heart was almost uncontrollable.
This guy had humiliated him again and again. With Xu Youwei's pride, how could he endure it? If it were not for Ling Xi and the other powerhouses of the Sword of Grief being present, he might have immediately killed Luo Zheng.
Taijin Mountain, Taiyue Mountain, Taiming Mountain, Taihao Mountain, Murdering Sword Sect, and Absolute Formation Sword Sect had all been eliminated.
Only Taiyi Mountain, Taixiu Mountain, Feng Ge, and the Heart Flow Sword Sect remained.
Apart from blocking Feng Ge at the beginning, Taixiu Mountain had chosen to play it safe and stay far away. With such a strategy, they would not be able to compete for first place.
The top ten sword sects clashed fiercely. For example, in the last Seven Mountains Party, there was only first and second place. There was no third place. The other eight sword sects had all been eliminated.
Taixiu Mountain was waiting for them to finish their competition. If they could get third place, it would be enough. If they were lucky, they might even get second place.
While Luo Zheng was suppressing Xu Youwei, Taixiu Mountain's boulder quietly came to the side of the metal mountain and began to absorb the gravity rune.
But halfway through, Taixiu Mountain saw Luo Zheng flying over on the boulder. Taixiu Mountain tactfully gave up and slipped away, leaving the gravity rune to the Heart Flow Sword Sect.
The people who had been eliminated on the side all pointed at Taixiu Mountain and laughed.
"Zhou Zhuo is too cunning!"
"As long as someone else is eliminated, Taixiu Mountain won't have to do anything. They'll definitely get third place!"
"If we had known, we would have done the same …"
Apart from Xu Youwei, Sang Hou, and the others who were unhappy, most people who had been eliminated were in a good mood. Although they had missed the prize, the Seven Mountains Party was not a battle of life and death.
Luo Zheng stepped on the boulder and leaned against the side of the metal mountain. He began to fuse with the gravity rune. During the fusion process, there were sounds of boulders colliding with each other. He looked up and frowned slightly.
Feng Ge was still sitting on the boulder. Her beautiful face was expressionless as if it had been frozen. Under her control, the boulder kept smashing against Taiyi Mountain's boulder.

Pursuit
After Luo Zheng absorbed the gravity rune, the weight of the boulder suddenly doubled, reaching the weight of two hundred godly catties.

Hearing Ling Shuang's words, Song Feiyu also looked at the metal mountain. His eyes flashed and he smiled. "He's not caring about himself. He wants to save us!"

With Feng Ge's arrogant personality, she would either not participate in the battle, or she would definitely take first place. Previously, she only needed to solve the biggest problem, Taiyi Mountain.
But now, Luo Zheng had also reached the second level. This guy could rush to the top of the metal mountain after fusing with the second gravity rune. How could Feng Ge allow this?
She frowned slightly. The boulder instantly changed direction and rushed towards the metal mountain. She wanted to stop Luo Zheng from fusing with the second gravity rune.

Song Feiyu thought of such a boring question and said helplessly, "My lady! The important thing is that we are not eliminated. As for whether Luo Zheng wants to save us or take the first place, is it important? "

Feng Ge controlled the boulder to go straight to Luo Zheng.

In addition, Feng Ge himself was extremely outstanding, so her determination to compete for first place was naturally quite strong.
Luo Zheng leisurely let the boulder under his feet absorb the second Gravity Rune, but in fact, his attention was fixed on Feng Ge.
His thoughts were the same as Song Feiyu's. It was impossible for Feng Ge to let him absorb the second gravity rune. He would definitely stop him.
Moreover, after absorbing the second gravity rune, the weight of the boulder would double again, reaching a terrifying 400 godly tons. Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't drag such a huge burden to deal with Feng Ge.
Seeing that Feng Ge's boulder was getting closer and closer to him, Luo Zheng tiptoed lightly.
The boulder suddenly flipped over and separated from the side of the metal mountain.
"Boom!"
The boulder controlled by Feng Ge smashed into the metal mountain. The huge impact even caused the metal mountain to shake.

"Don't underestimate her!"
"You must be careful!"
The people behind Luo Zheng reminded him.
Even though Luo Zheng seemed to have a special technique to control the boulder, he still couldn't let his guard down against Feng Ge.
Feng Ge's expression didn't change in the slightest after her attack missed. She looked up at Luo Zheng. The boulder immediately spiraled upward, following Luo Zheng.
"Whoosh!"
"Whoosh!"
The Heart Flow Sword Sect's boulder was extremely agile, but Feng Ge's was not slow either, and the impact was even stronger. The two boulders spiraled up and down in the air, like an eagle chasing after a nimble swallow. It was extremely entertaining.
"This technique … Feng Ge is so strong!"
"Don't forget that the Heart Flow Sword Sect only has seven people. Luo Zheng is stronger, right?"
"But the Heart Flow Sword Sect's boulder doesn't dare to collide head-on. It will lose sooner or later!"
Many people had never imagined that someone could control the heavy boulder to such an extent. They looked at the two boulders with reverence in their eyes.
"Alas, it's a pity that we didn't live up to expectations …"

The Heart Flow Sword Sect's elites who had fallen down were filled with regret. If they could fight side by side with Luo Zheng, a mere Feng Ge wouldn't be a threat.
"It's my fault," Gao Qizheng comforted them. "If Luo Zheng had gone up from the start, this wouldn't have happened. The Heart Flow Sword Sect would have won long ago."
At the top of the cave.
He Chi, Qiu Yinhe and the other three were also admiring the scene below.
"This is probably the most intense battle in the history of the Seven Mountains Party," He Chi said with a smile.
The old man who had given out the flags said, "Brother He Chi, who do you think will win in the end?"
"It should be Feng Ge," Qiu Yinhe said.
"Oh? Brother Yin He, you don't think Luo Zheng will win this time? "Lin Zhanting asked.
Luo Zheng had been escorted by Qiu Yinhe all the way to the Heart Flow Sword Sect. In order to win over Luo Zheng, Qiu Yinhe had even fallen out with Lin Zhanting.
Qiu Yinhe smiled helplessly. "Luo Zheng's advantage lies in his speed and agility, but he doesn't have the ability to clash with Feng Ge. Other than dodging, he has no other way to deal with it …"
"Brother Yin He, don't forget that our Taiyi Mountain is still here," Lin Zhanting chuckled.
Under the pursuit of Feng Ge and Luo Zheng, Taiyi Mountain moved stealthily along the bottom and quietly circled to the metal mountain. This was an excellent opportunity for Taiyi Mountain.
Although Luo Zheng's actions had actually saved Taiyi Mountain's life, Song Feiyu did not have any awareness of it.
"Brother Feiyu, isn't this wrong …" Ling Shuang began to feel conflicted again.
"What's wrong? This is a game. The enemy of my enemy is my friend. It's just that they've all become enemies now," Song Feiyu said with a smile.
"But …"
Ling Shuang was about to retort when the sound of wind came from behind.
"Bang!"
With the sound of a huge rock crashing, Taiyi Mountain's boulder was directly knocked away, and three people were thrown off the boulder.
Song Feiyu looked back and his face sank. "Zhou Zhuo?"
The person who had crashed into Taiyi Mountain's boulder was Zhou Zhuo from Taixiu Mountain. Zhou Zhuo had formulated a strategy from the beginning to ensure that he would not be eliminated and would still be in the top three.
However, now that he saw the light of hope for first place, he made a prompt decision.
Thus, Taixiu Mountain, who had been hiding for a long time, came out at this moment …
"If there's a chance to get first place, I'd be a fool not to fight for it," Zhou Zhuo said with a laugh.

Chapter 2826

Sudden change
Song Feiyu looked at Zhou Zhuo's complacent face and snorted. He said arrogantly, "Zhou Zhuo, you're just an opportunist. You must be dreaming!"
Song Feiyu's feet suddenly sank. Without hesitation, he charged toward Mt. Taixiu's boulder.
The battle between Mt. Taiyi and Mt. Taixiu was intense, but most people still focused on Feng Ge and Luo Zheng.

Chu Mo and the others sat on the boulder, dizzy from Luo Zheng's shaking.
Fortunately, Luo Zheng had full control of the boulder. They only needed to help Luo Zheng bear the weight of the boulder.
Halfway through, Chu Mo saw that Mt. Taiyi was approaching Mt. Taiyi, so he reminded Feng Ge, hoping that Feng Ge would turn around and deal with Mt. Taiyi.
Unexpectedly, Feng Ge was indifferent and ignored Chu Mo. She was determined to eliminate the Heart Flow Sword Sect.
Step by step, the two boulders flew close to the cave wall.
As long as the boulders touched any protrusions on the cave wall, they would be eliminated. It was a dangerous situation.
Whoosh!
Luo Zheng wanted to leave the cave wall, but Feng Ge's boulder suddenly twisted and blocked his path. Luo Zheng had no choice but to dodge to the other side.
But as he dodged, he fell into a long and narrow crevice on the north side of the cave.
The people behind Feng Ge were overjoyed.
"We won, Your Highness!"
"Give him a fatal blow!"
"This kid is too good at running!"

Whoosh!

But at this moment, the speed of the boulder under Luo Zheng's feet suddenly increased by several times!

After being hit by Feng Ge, his entire body flew out of the boulder. He shouted in confusion, "Feng, Your Highness Feng … why …"
Feng Ge did not pay attention to him. She stepped into the boundary glyph array and released the aura of True Intent from her body. At the same time, her thin red lips trembled slightly.
Luo Zheng stood at the front of the boulder, his eyes fixed on Feng Ge.
Even though victory was in his hands, he did not let his guard down. When he saw Feng Ge's lips move slightly, a strange thought appeared in his mind.
Reading lips was not difficult for Luo Zheng, but he needed to think and judge. The next moment, Luo Zheng's pupils suddenly contracted. He was probably the only one here who could understand what she was chanting!
Feng Ge had also comprehended the latter part of the Sword Essence of Eternity?
Wasn't it said that even Donghuang himself could not decipher the two Sanskrit tablets? This was different from the rumors!
But it was too late …
The lines on the boulder under Feng Ge's feet began to shine strangely. A thick halo of light shot out from the boulder.
As the barrier expanded, it even caused many of the narrow cracks to open up.
After the Heart Flow Sword Sect's boulder hit the rapidly expanding boundary, it was pushed out by a powerful force. The boulder seemed to have been thrown out by a pair of strong hands and flew at high speed in the cave.
"What's happening now …"
"Isn't this game just a boulder collision? Why is it so different from what I experienced? "
"What a powerful boundary!"
The spectators were stunned. The battle between Luo Zheng and Feng Ge was indeed difficult for them to understand.
Not only them, but even Lin Zhanting, He Chi, and the others were also confused. First, Luo Zheng used a method they did not know of to control the boulder. Now, Feng Ge suddenly displayed the same ability?
Hence, He Chi, Lin Zhanting, and the old man who gave out the flags all looked at Qiu Yinhe. But seeing Qiu Yinhe's dumbfounded expression, they did not bother to ask. Qiu Yinhe probably did not know either.
"Whoosh …"
The wind whistled past everyone's ears. Chu Mo and the others gritted their teeth and clung to the boulder to prevent themselves from being thrown out. But their speed was too fast, and they were about to hit the other side of the cave.
Luo Zheng used the Strength Divine Dao to fix himself to the boulder. He did not have to worry about this. His feet slid lightly, and he also came to the boundary glyph array.
"Buzz!"
The Heart Flow Sword Sect's boulder also released a thick boundary. As soon as the boundary was released, it hit the cave wall and slowly stabilized after bouncing back.

Chapter 2827

The Final Impact
Feng Ge spoke as she stepped lightly on the Acceleration Talisman Array.
The giant rock that was originally drifting slowly turned into a beam of light and shot toward Luo Zheng. As the acceleration was too fast, two people behind Feng Ge were thrown off.
Whoosh!
Such high speed attracted the attention of Taiyi Mountain and Taixiu Mountain who were in the middle of a fierce battle.
"What a terrifying speed!" Song Feiyu mumbled.
"Such speed … Is that giant rock weightless?" Ling Shuang opened her mouth and was speechless.
Zhou Zhuo from Taixiu Mountain was also filled with fear as he watched Feng Ge's giant rock fly across the sky …
After discovering Luo Zheng, an opponent with great potential, Feng Ge no longer cared about being the first. She also did not care about the outcome of the battle between Taiyi Mountain and Taixiu Mountain.
Seeing Feng Ge coming straight for him, Luo Zheng's expression was unprecedentedly serious.
Someone from the Heavenly Palace must have deciphered the two Sanskrit characters on the yellow paper, but it was hard to say how much they had deciphered …
Luo Zheng chanted silently in his heart, and the white cyclone in his mind began to swirl again.
His current limit was a hundred words, and the white cyclone that he had just formed was only about ten words. As he chanted, the white cyclone in his mind grew larger and larger.
Hum …
The giant rock under Luo Zheng's feet shone brighter and brighter.
Seeing Feng Ge coming at him at an unstoppable speed, Luo Zheng stepped on the Control Talisman Array and Acceleration Talisman Array again.
Whoosh!
The giant rock under his feet also exploded with incredible speed, easily dodging Feng Ge's attack.
Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …
After missing his attack, Feng Ge decisively turned around and chased after Luo Zheng again. As a result, Luo Zheng and Feng Ge were in a chase.
But this time, the speed of the two of them had already exceeded everyone's imagination. It was no longer a chase between two enormous rocks, and it was completely two Nirvana Realm experts flying.
"Senior Brother Gao, are those stones weightless?" A Heart Flow Sword Sect disciple asked.
"Maybe not." Gao Qizheng looked at this scene with an incredulous smile on his face.
Gao Qizheng had asked Luo Zheng to come because he saw him as a replacement for Mo Yijian. However, if it had been Mo Yijian, the Heart Flow Sword Sect would have been eliminated long ago.
It was impossible for any disciple in the Heavenly Palace to be able to compete with Feng Ge. However, he had invited Luo Zheng to participate in the Seven Mountains Meeting on a whim. Perhaps this was also a coincidence of fate …
Qiu Yinhe, He Chi, and the others all stood up, frowning as they watched the scene below.
As people who had been in positions of power for a long time, their eyes were naturally very sharp. Luo Zheng and Feng Ge's methods had already broken through the limit of comprehending with a single thought. The information hidden within was worth thinking about.
"Luo Zheng once went to the Sword Pavilion of True Will and borrowed that piece of yellow paper," Qiu Yinhe said.
Lin Zhanting and He Chi both raised their eyebrows when they heard this.
Many people in the Heavenly Palace knew about the origin of that piece of yellow paper. He Chi and Lin Zhanting had also borrowed it before. Of course, with their status, they naturally did not need to pay so many divine crystals.
However, many people had borrowed the yellow paper, but not a single one of them had seen any clues from it.
The Sword Intent of Eternity was already very perfect. One word more or less would not form it. There had even been a fierce debate about the last two Sanskrit pieces. Many people felt that the last two Sanskrit pieces were meaningless.
"Brother Yinhe, you mean that the two Sanskrit pieces are the continuation of the Sword Intent of Eternity, and Luo Zheng has deciphered them?" He Chi asked.
If this really happened, it would be enough to shock the entire Heavenly Palace.
"It's possible," Qiu Yinhe nodded.
"Then … what's going on with Feng Ge?" He Chi asked again.
Qiu Yinhe stared at Feng Ge on the huge rock and said, "Maybe Donghuang has made a breakthrough?"
Back then, Donghuang was unable to decipher it. The excellent interpreters in the Heavenly Palace were unable to decipher it, but it did not mean that they would never be able to decipher it.
The composition of Sanskrit was complex, and there were hundreds of millions of variations to choose from. One could spend an endless lifetime studying it for hundreds or even tens of millions of years. One could always hit one or two of the countless possibilities.
"The Imperial Sword Seal only responds to the True Will of the Dao. The most coincidental thing is that this has not happened for so many years. Today, it happened to Feng Ge and Luo Zheng at the same time. As far as I know, these two people have never crossed paths before," Qiu Yinhe said.
"Let's see who will win …" the old man who gave out the flags said.
"Boom!"
Another collision occurred in the cave. It was as if two experts had attacked with all their strength. The sound of the explosion reverberated in the cave …
Two people behind Luo Zheng were thrown out, and Feng Ge's loss was even greater. He directly threw out a group of people. There were only six people standing on her huge rock.
Even so, the fighting spirit in Feng Ge's eyes was still strong. After retreating a distance, she suddenly stopped and looked at Luo Zheng with a strange and calm expression. At the same time, her lips moved slightly. "You … have seen my father?"
Luo Zheng suddenly received Feng Ge's Zhenyuan sound transmission in his ear. He also felt a little strange, so he shook his head and replied, "No."
"Who are you trying to fool? Otherwise, who would have taught you the True Comprehension Chapter?" Feng Ge sneered.
"True Comprehension Chapter?"
Luo Zheng instantly understood. The True Comprehension Chapter that Feng Ge spoke of was the two Sanskrit pieces that had been deciphered.
It seemed that his guess was right. Donghuang himself had indeed deciphered a part of it.
"I don't know why my father taught you. If you want to win, you have to show all your sincerity …" Feng Ge fell into a certain state at this moment. Her originally white and clear face was stained with a faint red color.
"Buzz!"
The huge rock under her feet also began to tremble …
Luo Zheng felt the change in her aura. He immediately said to Chu Mo and the others behind him, "You guys … leave the huge rock."
"What?"
"Why do we have to leave?"
"We have to advance and retreat together with Junior Brother Luo!"
Chu Mo and the others couldn't understand Luo Zheng's request. They had already come to this point. How could they be willing to abandon Luo Zheng?
"Listen to me. I have confidence!" Luo Zheng urged. His tone didn't allow for any disobedience.
Chu Mo frowned and thought for a moment. Finally, he gritted his teeth and waved his hand. He led the remaining people on the huge rock and left.
After they left, the boulder sank a little, and Luo Zheng alone bore the weight of two hundred godly catties of force.
At the same time, Luo Zheng began to silently recite the True Comprehension Chapter. The size of the white cyclone in his mind also began to increase.
"Whoosh!"
Feng Ge stepped on the huge rock and suddenly shot into the sky. He almost touched the ceiling of the cave. Then, he began to accelerate from a tricky angle. He turned into a comet falling from the sky and smashed towards Luo Zheng's head.

Chapter 2828
A Cheap Trick
At this moment, no one was paying attention to who would emerge as champion of the Seven Mountains Meeting.
All the spectators wanted to know was who would emerge victorious between Luo Zheng and Feng Ge.
Two boulders weighing two hundred Godly tons were moving at such speeds. No matter which mountain the elites were on, they would all die without a doubt.
Even Ling Xi and the others in the Land of Sword Grief were shocked when they saw this scene.
"Seeing the performance of these up-and-coming elites, I feel a sense of crisis …."
"I participated in the Seven Mountains Meeting before, but I'm still far from their level!"
Boom!
The two boulders collided head-on in midair. At the same time, the elites directly under Feng Ge were all flung away and smashed into the distant mountain wall, all of them heavily injured.
When Chu Mo and the others saw this, they finally understood why Luo Zheng wanted them to leave.
At this moment, only Luo Zheng and Feng Ge were still firmly fixed to the boulders.
The instant the boulders bounced off each other, the two of them once again stood on the formation array. Their thoughts were the same. In the face of such a violent collision, the most important thing was to activate the formation array.
Boom boom boom ….
The two boulders had already bounced off the walls of the cave with lightning speed, creating enormous craters.
Rubble tumbled down, and dust rustled ….
Everyone raised their heads and looked to the sides, but they didn't see a single boulder flying out.
At this moment, Ling Xi released her Divine Sense and passed through the rolling dust.
Her eyes brightened slightly, and she raised her hand. "His Highness Feng Ge … is eliminated!"
Whoosh ….
The crowd below immediately boiled over.
Feng Ge's performance this time was so strong that it was almost impossible to defeat him, but in the end, he was still eliminated!
Gao Qizheng and the others looked at Ling Xi nervously.
If Ling Xi announced that the Heart Flow Sword Sect was also eliminated, then Luo Zheng's efforts would have been in vain.
Ling Shuang also looked to the side of the mountain wall with a worried expression, her eyes filled with worry.
After such a violent collision, Luo Zheng wouldn't be injured, right?
Song Feiyu of the Taiyi Mountain Sect and Zhou Zhuo of the Taixiu Mountain Sect were full of anticipation. As long as Luo Zheng was eliminated, the competition between them would disappear. The two major sword sects only needed to compete for first and second place.
Ling Shuang stared at Luo Zheng's side, her brows slightly furrowed, revealing a hint of hesitation. "This …."
She didn't know whether Luo Zheng was eliminated or not... as she was hesitating..........
In the big hole on the wall, a layer of enchantment spread out from the surface of the huge rock. Luo Zheng, who was at the innermost part of the enchantment, pressed his hands on the enchantment runes, and his whole body lay between the wall and the huge rock!
...................................................................
....... Feng Ge may not care about the rewards of the Seven Mountains Trial, but those rewards were still very important to Luo Zheng, especially the Paramita Token made from the sealed stone!
In the end, he chose to use his body to bear a considerable amount of the impact.
This method was more or less similar to what Gao Qizheng had done before.
However, Gao Qizheng had completely left the giant rock. Once he left, he would be eliminated.
Luo Zheng didn't leave the giant rock. At least, his hands were still pressed against the giant rock's barrier, but his feet were still standing on the wall of the cave.
Ling Xi began to feel conflicted about such a decision. In the end, she still looked to the top of the cave for help.
Soon, she received an answer. "The Heart Flow Sword Sect is not eliminated!"
"Wow!"
The spectators around the cave burst into an uproar again.
Gao Qizheng and the others were so happy that they jumped up, unable to contain their joy.
The elites of the Murdering Sword Sect and the Peerless Sword Sect hung their heads, their faces full of despair.
They didn't know how the Heart Flow Sword Sect had been so lucky all these years …
They had tried their best to stop them, and even such a terrifying Feng Ge had appeared halfway, but they couldn't stop the Heart Flow Sword Sect at all.
"Dong!"
Feng Ge kicked the giant rock on the other side of the cave, and it rolled to the bottom of the cave.
Even with the rolling dust, Feng Ge's sleeves were still spotless.
The expression on her face was frozen again, but the look in her eyes couldn't be concealed.
"At the crucial moment, he used his body to block the giant rock. He's so confident in his body …" Feng Ge knew that his loss was justified.
The moment Feng Ge crashed into the mountain wall, he also thought of this plan.
However, the kinetic energy of the giant rock was too terrifying. It was very likely that he would be seriously injured or even killed. Looking at Luo Zheng's unscathed appearance, it meant that he was confident.
Luo Zheng dragged the 200-kilogram rock and slowly floated toward the metal mountain.
Other than letting Luo Zheng have the Gravity Rune, they didn't dare to move at all …
"But can Luo Zheng bear the weight of 400 Kilograms alone?" Song Feiyu and the others looked doubtful.
After fusing with the second Gravity Rune, the weight doubled again. It usually took more than ten people to lift it to the top of the metal mountain.
"It'll be great if he can't lift it! Hahaha! "The people of Mount Taixiu were also full of anticipation.
After Luo Zheng fused with the second gravity rune, the boulder once again abruptly sank downwards. However, after sinking a few feet, Luo Zheng forcibly pulled it back up.
"Re … Freak!"
"What terrifying power!"
"Did he fuse with a strengthening Paramita Token?"
Actually, with Luo Zheng carrying the 400-kilogram rock alone, Song Feiyu and Zhou Zhuo obviously had a chance.
However, on the one hand, they also understood that taking advantage of the situation would probably cause them to be cursed. On the other hand, they weren't confident of winning, so they didn't make a move.
Under everyone's gaze, Luo Zheng flew to the top of the metal mountain and threw the giant rock into the tray at the top. Then, he stood on the giant rock and raised his arm.
"Wow!"
Gao Qizheng and the other elites of the Heart Sword Sect also raised their hands and roared.
The others also kept clapping. Everyone's reaction was very excited.
Song Feiyu looked at Zhou Zhuo provocatively. "Do we still need to determine the winner?"
The most generous reward in the Seven Mountains Trial was first place. The difference between second and third place wasn't that big. Zhou Zhuo was good at calculation. Even if he had a 50% chance of getting second place, it was still not worth the risk of being eliminated.
"Brother Feiyu, please." Zhou Zhuo cupped his hands.
Thus, Song Feiyu led Mount Taiyi to absorb the Gravity Rune and threw the giant rock of Mount Taiyi on the second tray. Mount Taixiu got third place.
The people in the cave didn't leave. They all looked at the top.
After a while, Qiu Yinhe, Lin Zhanting, and Hechi floated down from the top and slowly landed beside Luo Zheng.
"Luo Zheng greets seniors." Luo Zheng cupped his hands and bowed. He had seen all four seniors before.
The only thing that surprised him was that the old man who distributed the flags in Dragon City also came.

Chapter 2829
Translator: Henyee Translations Editor: Henyee Translations
Rewards
When Luo Zheng had first entered the Heavenly Palace, Lin Zhanting had argued with Qiu Yinhe.
Although Lin Zhanting was joking, he was more or less regretful. Just based on Luo Zheng's performance in the Seven Mountains Meeting, Taiyi Mountain should not have given in.
"It's not a matter of confidence. Have you predicted their performance?" Qiu Yinhe retorted.
The methods Luo Zheng and Feng Ge had displayed on the giant rock had never been seen before. No one could use past experience to determine the success or failure of the other.
Whether it was Qiu Yinhe, He Chi, Yang Zhanting, or Lin Zhanting, they were all suspicious of Feng Ge and Luo Zheng's methods of controlling the talisman matrix, but it was naturally inconvenient for them to show it now.
And since Luo Zheng had used the "True Comprehension Chapter" that Feng Ge had mentioned, he was naturally mentally prepared. If Qiu Yinhe really did ask, he would be able to respond.
The topic came to an end here.
Next, He Chi announced, "The first place of this Seven Mountains Meeting is the Heart Flow Sword Sect, the second place is Taiyi Mountain, and the third place is Taixiu Mountain. Please come up and receive your rewards!"
Gao Qizheng and the others were already itching to move. As soon as He Chi announced this, the elites of the Heart Flow Sword Sect, Taiyi Mountain, and Taixiu Mountain who had been left behind all flew towards the metal mountain.
"Taixiu Mountain first!" He Chi said.
The twenty elites of Taixiu Mountain gathered around He Chi and extended their palms to him.
He Chi's lips moved slightly, and he extended his hand to slap those palms.
With each slap, a few simple lines were left on their palms.
Luo Zheng saw these lines and his eyes lit up. "These are soul marks?"
He Chi smiled. "Of course. With these soul marks, you can go to the Heavenly Palace Temple in three days and receive a soul crystal."
The soul mark Luo Zheng received from the Heart Flow Sword Sect was a small medallion with syllables recorded on it. It would only appear if one chanted it. He Chi had not expected it to appear so easily.
Things like soul marks were common knowledge to these elite disciples. If it was someone else who asked such a childish question, the elites of Mount Taixiu would probably laugh at them.
However, the person who asked this question was Luo Zheng. Naturally, they didn't dare to answer, but they couldn't help but mutter in their hearts.
After the disciples of Taixiu Mountain received their soul tags, they retreated to the side. It was Taiyi Mountain's turn again.
After everyone from the Taiyi Mountain Sect received their soul crystals, Gao Qizheng led them forward and cupped his hands at He Chi. "Lord He Chi, please give half of our Heart Flow Sword Sect's soul crystals to Luo Zheng."
Soul crystals were so rare that even direct descendants like Ling Shuang and Xu Youwei valued them greatly. Gao Qizheng's sudden request surprised He Chi. He asked in return, "Are you sure?"
"Back then, when we wanted to persuade Luo Zheng to fight, we already had such an agreement," Gao Qizheng said seriously.
Gao Qizheng and the others had also contributed a lot to Mo Yijian's victory in the previous Seven Mountains Trial. However, it was completely different this time. It was almost as if Luo Zheng was carrying the entire Heart Flow Sword Sect on his shoulders. Even if they gave all of it to Luo Zheng, he would be convinced, let alone half of it.
"Since that's the case, I'll do it." He Chi nodded and made some slight changes to the soul mark on his finger.
Then, Gao Qizheng and the others stretched out their palms one by one and gently touched He Chi's palm.
When it was Luo Zheng's turn, He Chi changed the soul mark on his palm again. After patting Luo Zheng's hand, the soul mark was left on his palm.
"Although we don't have many soul crystals stored in the Heavenly Palace, the Seven Mountains Trial provided far more than any other trial. You've taken almost half of it all by yourself … Hehe, use these soul crystals well!" He Chi laughed.
Whether it was the people from Mount Taixiu or Mount Taiyi, they all looked envious when they heard this. Absorbing so many soul crystals would probably bring about a qualitative leap to one's soul!
After everyone received the soul crystals, they had no intention of leaving.
At this moment, He Chi took out three longswords from his Sumeru Ring. "These three Nirvana Realm treasures are awarded to all of you in my name."
The three longswords in He Chi's hand were first-rate, second-rate, and third-rate Nirvana Realm treasures.
The conditions to create a Nirvana Realm treasure were extremely harsh. Even these elites mostly had Mystique Venerable Grade treasures.
He Chi distributed the third-rate and second-rate Nirvana Realm treasures to Mount Taixiu and Mount Taiyi. As for the last longsword, it was pitch-black from the hilt to the blade. On the pitch-black blade, there were faint patterns of birds.
"This is the first-rate Nirvana Realm treasure created by Feng Shi!" Luo Zheng stared at the black sword and was slightly moved.
"This Phoenix Seal Sword has been with me for many years. I've offered it to you for this Seven Mountains Trial." He Chi stroked the blade several times and handed it to Gao Qizheng.
Gao Qizheng did not hesitate and threw it to Luo Zheng.
"Luo Zheng again!" Ling Shuang chuckled. "Gao Qizheng, you paid a hefty price to invite Luo Zheng!"
Gao Qizheng scratched his head embarrassedly and smiled. "Looks like our foresight is quite accurate, haha …"
For these elites of the Heart Flow Sword Sect, they were not only competing for rewards but more importantly for glory. To be able to keep the first place of the Seven Mountains Trial for two consecutive sessions in the Heart Flow Sword Sect was an unprecedented feat on Mount Taiyi.
Qiu Yinhe watched as Luo Zheng gently stroked the Phoenix Seal Sword and felt a little strange. It would be normal for other people to value a Nirvana Realm treasure so much, but Luo Zheng had a Connate Realm treasure in his hands.
After He Chi gave out the rewards, everyone could leave the cave on their own. There was a feast on the top of the mountain, which Lingxi and the others were naturally in charge of.
Just as everyone was going back the way they came, He Chi called out to Luo Zheng and Feng Ge.
Feng Ge frowned slightly. With her status, even if she wanted to leave, He Chi and the others would not dare to make things difficult for her. Moreover, she knew why He Chi had asked her to stay.
However, Feng Ge was also curious as to how Luo Zheng could comprehend the [True Comprehension Chapter], and seemed to have comprehended more than her, so she simply stayed where she was.
In the blink of an eye, there were only six people left in the originally bustling cave.
Qiu Yinhe waved his finger and the boulders flew up from all directions. At the same time, all the formations on them began to flicker and gather around the metal mountain.
"The talisman formations on these boulders were all modified by the Imperial Sword Seal, but even if I personally did it, it would still be inferior to Her Highness Feng Ge. This old man is indeed curious, so I'll take the liberty to ask, did His Majesty make a breakthrough?"
Luo Zheng agreed with Qiu Yinhe's words. This was similar to his conjecture.

Chapter 2830
Translator: Henyee Translations Editor: Henyee Translations
Testing the waters
"Even His Highness Feng Ge can't see Donghuang himself?" Qiu Yinhe was surprised.
Donghuang had indeed not shown himself for a long time.
It was common for such a person to be in seclusion for hundreds or thousands of years, but the world was in a very sensitive stage.
The leaders of many major forces were moving around, but Donghuang was the only one who was in seclusion. It was a bit strange.
"Since that's the case, how does His Highness Feng Ge control these boulders?" Qiu Yinhe asked again.
Feng Ge shrugged. She was not afraid to tell them these things. "There was a piece of paper. When I was cultivating in the palace, Yao Yue gave it to me. There were 63 words in total. It was written on the paper. This was the True Comprehension Chapter of the Eternal Sword Intent!"
Hearing Feng Ge's report, Qiu Yinhe and the others were surprised again.
"True Comprehension Chapter!"
"Donghuang really has a new understanding. He deciphered 63 words. It's already very rare."
"…"
Seeing their surprised expressions, Feng Ge said straightforwardly, "You also want to cultivate the True Comprehension Chapter?"
Qiu Yinhe, He Chi, and the others all cupped their hands and said, "We don't dare."
The True Comprehension Chapter was Donghuang's painstaking effort, and they were all subordinates of the Heavenly Palace. Without Donghuang's permission, even if Feng Ge really handed over the 63 words, they would not dare to cultivate it.
"Then that's it. What's the point of keeping me here? If you really want to comprehend the True Comprehension Chapter, you can just directly find Imperial Father," Feng Ge said straightforwardly. The arrogance on her face grew thicker and thicker. She had never held back when speaking.
As soon as she said this, Lin Zhanting and the others all looked embarrassed. They were all powerful cultivators who had been famous in the Heavenly Palace for a long time, but this little girl made it sound like they were coveting her True Comprehension Chapter. For a moment, no one answered. After all, Feng Ge's identity and status were special.
But Luo Zheng was not used to hearing this. He frowned and said, "The elders are just asking for the reason. How do you know they want to cultivate the True Comprehension Chapter? His Highness Feng Ge's words are too petty. "
As for Feng Ge's lack of etiquette, Luo Zheng didn't hold back.
"Hmph, the Eternal Sword Intent is a Divine Ability of the Divine Ability, and the True Comprehension Chapter makes up for all its shortcomings. If you comprehend all of it, you can reach a whole new level. The True Comprehension Chapter is a supreme Divine Ability of the Divine Ability, and who can overlook it!"
In the Heavenly Palace, the elders did not argue with Feng Ge, and no one in the same generation dared to make her lose face. However, she had just lost to Luo Zheng, and now she was being criticized by Luo Zheng to her face. How could she be willing to back down?
Feng Ge's explanation of the Dharma referred to the principles of the True Meanings of the Great Daos.
The True Meaning of the Path itself did not contain energy, but they were the most basic techniques that could unleash the power of the Nirvana Token to the greatest extent.
As for the various divine abilities created according to the True Meaning of the Dao, it was actually a kind of supplement to the "Dao Interpretation".
Since the True Comprehension Chapter could carry the latter part of the Eternal Sword Intent, its value was indeed immeasurable. Feng Ge was aware of this, and so were Qiu Yinhe and Hechi. Of course, Luo Zheng was also aware of this.
However, looking at Feng Ge's proud face, Luo Zheng sneered, "It's only 63 words, and you think you've obtained the world's most precious treasure! But you don't know that in the eyes of others, it's worthless! "
As he spoke, he looked at Qiu Yinhe and said, "Seniors, do you have any brushes and ink?"
Qiu Yinhe, Hechi, and the others, as well as the old man who handed out the flags, looked at each other. The old man suddenly laughed heartily, "I do!"
The old man waved his hand, and a roll of white rice paper slowly appeared in front of Luo Zheng. The other brush also flew towards Luo Zheng.
When Luo Zheng took the brush in his hand, Feng Ge frowned, "Imperial Father passed on the True Comprehension Chapter to you. How dare you casually share it with others!"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng snorted coldly. The brush and ink in his hand danced like dragons and phoenixes as he wrote, "When I first entered the Heavenly Palace, I learned that Emperor Donghuang used his unparalleled talent to obtain the Eternal Sword Intent, benefiting the world and establishing the orthodox human race. I couldn't help but admire him!"
"Swish, swish, swish …"
Vigorous and powerful handwriting appeared on the white rice paper.
Under Feng Ge's gaze, his eyes were covered with a layer of haze. She was sure that Luo Zheng's True Comprehension Chapter was obtained from Emperor Donghuang. In this world, except for her father, no one could decipher it.
Therefore, the True Comprehension Chapter written by Luo Zheng must have been imparted by Emperor Donghuang himself. What he did was a great crime of disrespect!
But these elders were here, so it was inconvenient for Feng Ge to make a move. He just felt more and more sullen in his heart.
Qiu Yinhe originally wanted to stop Luo Zheng. If Luo Zheng's True Comprehension Chapter was indeed imparted by Emperor Donghuang, then Luo Zheng would have to bear a great crime. Even Qiu Yinhe and the others would be punished.
But on second thought, since Luo Zheng entered the Heavenly Palace, Qiu Yinhe knew every detail of it like the back of his hand. It was impossible for him to have come into contact with Emperor Donghuang himself, especially since Luo Zheng had rented that yellow paper!
From this point of view, it was more likely that Luo Zheng had deciphered the True Comprehension Chapter by himself. But looking back, it was still inconceivable.
Under Luo Zheng's unceasing writing, the four elders present also recited it silently in their hearts.
Their souls were much stronger than Luo Zheng's and Feng Ge's. When they recited it at the beginning, they also encountered that incomparably sharp sword ray. But this sword ray couldn't be released before it was forcibly extinguished in the depths of their minds.
But whether it was Qiu Yinhe, Lin Zhanting, Yang, or Chi Yi, they were all very surprised. A mere dozen words could erupt with such a sword ray. As expected of the True Comprehension Chapter!
Luo Zheng wrote from the first word to the hundredth word in one breath before stopping. At the same time, he glanced at Feng Ge, shook his head, and said, "It's a pity that the descendant of Emperor Donghuang doesn't have this kind of awareness."
"You!"
Hearing Luo Zheng's last sentence, she bit her lip. Clearly, she was extremely angry.
But she also had a strong curiosity about the True Comprehension Chapter!
Luo Zheng's hundred words had far exceeded her sixty-three words. It was no wonder that he performed so well on the giant rock.
"What about me?" Luo Zheng continued, "Emperor Donghuang placed the yellow paper in the True Meaning Sword Pavilion to borrow the people of the world to decipher the last two Sanskrit words. Does His Highness Feng Ge really think that Emperor Donghuang is the only one in the world who can decipher those two Sanskrit words?"
Feng Ge was originally not good with words. His rebuke made Feng Ge even more speechless.
In a rage out of humiliation, she actually rushed forward, grabbed the white paper in her hands, and tore it into pieces. Then, she ran away.
Qiu Yinhe and the others didn't stop Feng Ge …
They remembered every word that Luo Zheng wrote in their minds. It didn't matter whether they had that piece of paper or not.
Looking at Feng Ge's back, Qiu Yinhe laughed softly and said, "Luo Zheng spent most of his time cultivating in the Heart Flow Sword Sect, but he did learn 99% of Idealism. But offending His Highness Feng Ge like this isn't too good, is it?"
Chi Yi waved his hand and said confidently, "It doesn't matter. Emperor Donghuang is magnanimous, and he has more than one daughter. He won't mind."
He was not only comforting Luo Zheng, but also supporting Luo Zheng. Tian Jiedu had a lot of weight in the Heavenly Palace, far more than those princes and princesses. So what if he offended one or two of them?
"How did Luo Zheng decipher these two Sanskrit words? Did he only decipher these hundred words?" Qiu Yinhe asked again.

Chapter 2831
Making a request
The Eternal Sword Intent was the foundation of Greater One Heavenly Palace, and had a profound influence on this branch of the Human Race.
Qiu Yinhe, He Chi and the others were moved by this!
Luo Zheng was already very generous to take out a hundred characters, how could Qiu Yinhe and the others think it was too much? They were eager for Luo Zheng to write out all the characters in the [True Comprehension Chapter].
Now that Luo Zheng understood the position of Greater One Heavenly Palace, he was also happy to grow here, and even integrate into this branch of the Human Race. After all, Jiuli no longer existed, and if he really wanted to revive it, not only was his strength far from enough, his influence was also minimal...
And the [True Comprehension Chapter] was an opportunity that could be used.
"Seniors, I have more than a hundred characters," Luo Zheng cupped his hands and said.
Luo Zheng's answer immediately gave the four of them great hope. If the [True Comprehension Chapter] was completely deciphered, what kind of method of interpretation would they obtain?
"How many characters?" Qiu Yinhe quickly asked.
Luo Zheng smiled slightly, "150 characters, the rest have not been deciphered yet."
"It's already 150 words!"
"But you deciphered it yourself?"
"Did someone else do it?"
Qiu Yinhe and the others repeatedly asked.
For existences like them, it was rare for them to show such an attitude. After all, the [True Comprehension Chapter] was too important, and if it appeared, it might even change the current weak position of the Heavenly Palace!
"I deciphered it myself, but 150 characters is already the limit. The composition of the Sanskrit behind it is too complicated, so I haven't been able to decipher it yet," Luo Zheng replied.
Luo Zheng had already thought of this excuse.
The [True Comprehension Chapter] originally belonged to the Heavenly Palace, and the Heavenly Palace treated Luo Zheng well. Sooner or later, he would give the deciphered version to the Heavenly Palace, but before that, it was not too much to fight for some benefits for himself.
Luo Zheng of course could not reveal the matter of Luo Nian, otherwise they would tirelessly surround him, and even use some unknown means to get Luo Nian out.
Thus, he decided to take on everything himself. It wouldn't be too late to clear Luo Nian's name after he left his inner world.
Qiu Yinhe and the others didn't believe it. The translators in the Heavenly Palace who deciphered Sanskrit were studying Sanskrit all day long. How could Luo Zheng look like a translator?
But in the end, the True Comprehension Chapter was provided by Luo Zheng. Regardless of whether the True Comprehension Chapter was deciphered by him or someone else, they couldn't question it.
"Luo Zheng, are you sure you can decipher the rest of the content?" Lin Zhanting asked with concern.
Luo Zheng said cautiously, "There are too many uncertainties when it comes to deciphering Sanskrit. I don't dare to say that I'm 100% sure."
Luo Zheng didn't give them a hundred percent guarantee.
"Then, can we have a look at the content of the 150 words?" He Chi asked as well.
As long as it was the content of the True Comprehension Chapter, even a single word was extremely valuable.
"When I return to the Heart Flow Sword Sect, I will write down the 150 words. If you want to have a look, you can come to the Heart Flow Sword Sect and take it," Luo Zheng nodded.
Seeing how generous Luo Zheng was, He Chi, Lin Zhanting, and the others were a little embarrassed. They were also the mainstays of the Heavenly Palace, and yet they were eagerly asking for something from a junior. Although it was not proper, it was understandable.
He Chi thought for a while and said, "The 150 words of the True Comprehension Chapter is extremely rare. We can't take it for free. How about this, you can make a request of each of us. How about that?"
Qiu Yinhe and Lin Zhanting weren't stingy people. Since He Chi had said so, they nodded. Qiu Yinhe even said, "Whatever you lack, you can ask."
Since they had taken the initiative to ask, Luo Zheng didn't want to refuse. He asked, "Are there any Nirvana Realm treasures in the Heavenly Palace that are made from sealed stones?"
Hearing Luo Zheng's request, the four of them looked at each other.
Nirvana Realm treasures were rare, but for people like them, it wasn't difficult to get one or two. However, there were too few Nirvana Realm tokens made from sealed stones. This was because sealed stones could only be found by chance.
"Didn't I just give you the Luan Sword Award at the Seven Mountains Meeting? Do you still need a Nirvana Realm treasure made from sealed stones?" He Chi thought for a while and quickly grasped the main point. What Luo Zheng wanted was probably not the Nirvana Realm treasure, but the sealed stones themselves.
However, sealed stones were too special. Other than being used to make weapons, they couldn't be moved. Even if Luo Zheng wanted to find sealed stones, he could only look for Nirvana Realm treasures.
"What do you want sealed stones for?" Qiu Yinhe looked at Luo Zheng in confusion. He remembered that Jinwu was indeed fighting with the Heavenly Palace for a sealed stone in Biyun City. However, Luo Zheng's purpose in going to Guanshan Prefecture was only for merits.
However, after the sealed stone was dissolved by the Family Su, it was lost in Phoenix's pursuit. This was a suspicious point. However, even if Luo Zheng obtained the solution for the sealed stone, he couldn't do anything without the Family Su's Weapon Refining Formation.
"I need this stone for cultivation. If seniors can help me find it, I'll be very grateful," Luo Zheng answered honestly.
Luo Zheng understood that there were some things that couldn't be hidden. Since he had asked the seniors to find the sealed stone, he didn't need to hide it.
"What kind of cultivation method … You actually need sealed stones!" He Chi exclaimed.
"You're using yourself as a treasure to cultivate!" Qiu Yinhe laughed.
Lin Zhanting thought of Luo Zheng's special ability. After all, his son was saved by Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng's body was really different from ordinary people. However, it was still very strange to use sealed stones for cultivation.
"I know of a Nirvana Realm treasure made from sealed stones," the elder who gave out the flags suddenly said, "And it's in the Center God Province."
Luo Zheng's eyes lit up. He cupped his hands and said, "Senior, please tell me."
"That sealed stone was passed down from other foreign tribes. It's a war hammer. However, the craftsmanship is very poor. A sealed stone can create a first-rate Nirvana Realm treasure with 100% certainty. However, this war hammer is only a third-rate Nirvana Realm treasure … I can help you with this request!" the elder said confidently.
"Thank you, senior," Luo Zheng thanked him and said at the same time, "I've been in Dragon City for a long time. I've met you many times, but I've never asked for your name."
When he was in Dragon City, Luo Zheng had already seen that the elder's cultivation was extraordinary. However, he had asked Yin Yuehuan and the others, but they didn't know anything.
This person could talk and laugh with Qiu Yinhe and the others. It was impossible for him to be unknown in the Heavenly Palace.
Qiu Yinhe smiled at the side, "I still can't tell you the name of this senior. However, you can call him Elder Hai."
Luo Zheng thanked him again and left with satisfaction.
Although He Chi asked Luo Zheng to make a request to each of them, Luo Zheng knew when to stop.

Visit

The Heart Flow Sword Sect was also jubilant when they heard that the Seven Mountains Party had won again.
This was because in addition to the rewards from He Chi, the Heavenly Palace would also provide resources to the Heart Flow Sword Sect in the next three years. This was a good thing that benefited everyone, so it was naturally worth celebrating.
In the independent courtyard, balls of light transformed into colorful butterflies that danced in the courtyard.
Mo Yijian sat cross-legged, and with a gentle wave of his hand, all the butterflies gathered from all directions. Those butterflies of light seemed to be obeying orders, and their movements were uniform.

Therefore, many people were amazed by the Heart Flow Sword Sect as the news spread like wildfire.
The Heart Flow Sword Sect was not considered the top among the many Sword Sects in the Heavenly Palace, but they had produced many extraordinary people!

Even Mo Yijian could not fathom Feng Ge's true strength, and he was one of his opponents in the Heart Flow Sword Sect.

Now, there is only one more person, Luo Zheng … but according to the rumors, Luo Zheng and Feng Ge seem to have used special methods to control the boulders?"
As he spoke, he looked confused.
He had a perfect compatibility with the Eternal Sword Intent, so his control of the boulders should have reached its limit. How could Luo Zheng and Feng Ge surpass this limit? It was impossible for such a thing to happen.

After thinking for a while, he asked, "If Feng Ge could get these chapters from Donghuang, then where did Luo Zheng get them?"
Luo Zheng had always been in the Heart Flow Sword Sect, so how could he have the opportunity to meet Donghuang?
"I don't know, but as far as I know, Luo Zheng should have mastered more chapters than Feng Ge, so he was able to defeat Feng Ge at the Seven Mountains Meeting," Han 'er added.
Mo Yijian could no longer sit still. When Mo Yijian had to choose from the Three Great Sword Sects, his Heart Flow Sword Sect was the weakest and the most difficult, while the Murdering Sword Sect was simpler and more violent, but after reaching great success, the Heart Flow Sword Sect was stronger than both the Murdering Sword Sect and the Formation Sword Sect, so Mo Yijian joined the Heart Flow Sword Sect without hesitation.
He had always been pursuing the strongest Sword Dao, and he had never disappointed anyone. The next chapter of the Eternal Sword Intent was like a shining treasure that attracted him.

Chapter 2833
A foolish challenger.

9527's voice suddenly came out.

Those people from the Family Su hadn't really died. They could still set up the Nine Edges Refining Array. Going to the Family Su was indeed a good choice.

Such a huge dispute had occurred in Jade Cloud City because of a Sealing Stone. In the end, his whereabouts were unknown. The Family Su would probably investigate because of this.

At this moment, Xiaoyin's voice came from the courtyard, "Mr. Luo, someone is here to visit!"
"Visit …"
Recently, there had been an unusually large number of people visiting. Luo Zheng was already used to it.
Just as Luo Zheng came to the entrance of the courtyard and pushed the door open, he suddenly felt a chill in his nose. Then, three rays of light bloomed not far away. A fierce murderous aura covered the sky and covered Luo Zheng.
Although Xiaoyin had the cultivation of a True God, she was completely unable to withstand this murderous aura. Under the pressure of this murderous aura, her eyes rolled back and she fainted.
Luo Zheng looked over, and his face suddenly darkened. It was Xu Youwei of Taihao Mountain, and there were seven or eight black-clothed guards standing behind him.
Xu Youwei had long wanted to kill Luo Zheng. He wanted to do it at the Seven Mountains Meeting but was stopped by Lingxi. Later on, he was defeated by Luo Zheng in the Giant Rock Competition. The resentment in his heart deepened.
After returning to Taihao Mountain, he couldn't swallow this anger. On impulse, he rushed to the Heart Flow Sword Sect and challenged Luo Zheng to a battle.
"Luo Zheng, you and I are both disciples of the Heavenly Palace. Today, I challenge you to a deathmatch. Do you dare accept?" Xu Youwei said firmly.
Before Luo Zheng could respond, a slow voice suddenly came from not far away. "It's not impossible for the people of Taihao Mountain to challenge the Heart Flow Sword Sect, but they can't forget the order and etiquette."
The person who spoke was Mo Yijian.
When he saw Mo Yijian, Xu Youwei's face changed slightly. He said coldly, "Mo Yijian, this has nothing to do with you!"
"We are all disciples of the Heart Flow Sword Sect. Naturally, we are of the same breath and branch. How can you say we have nothing to do with you? If you want to challenge Luo Zheng, you have to go through me first, "Mo Yijian said.
Mo Yijian gently pinched his fingers in the air, and a group of glittering butterflies floated behind him.
The moment the butterfly floated out, Mo Yijian immediately felt a fatal threat.
It was as if there was an invisible sword pressed against his heart, and it could kill him at any time!
"Leave. Don't let me see you again in the Heart Flow Sword Sect." Mo Yijian's slightly childish voice was extremely cold. He didn't take Xu Youwei's identity seriously at all.
"You … you dare kill me?"
Thinking of the strange killing methods of the Heart Flow Sword Sect, Xu Youwei's back suddenly broke out in cold sweat. However, he was a direct descendant of Taihao Mountain and the son of the Mountain Master. Mo Yijian didn't dare to kill him!
"Pfft!"
Xu Youwei had just finished speaking when blood suddenly spurted out of his chest.
Xu Youwei's heart was pierced. Mo Yijian didn't hesitate at all.
"Young Master Youwei!"
"Young Master Youwei …"
"Stop the bleeding for Young Master Youwei!"

Chapter 2834

The Lesson
Xu Youwei's guards did not have high cultivation levels, but most of them had retired from Tai Yiwei and were considered retainers of the Xu family.

Chapter 2835
Request
Xiaoyin naturally knew that Mo Yijian was a very important guest, so she had to treat him seriously. She hurriedly got Xiao Xin to work.

Sitting quietly in his room, Luo Zheng recalled the events of Guanshan Prefecture.

He stretched out his hand and lightly drew a line across his chest. A wisp of flame soared up from his chest.
In the blink of an eye, the flame had spread to his entire body.
In the past, every time Luo Zheng absorbed a type of flame, the strength of his body would increase. However, as his body continued to grow, absorbing flames could no longer strengthen his body.
Moreover, when ordinary flames burned his body, they couldn't cause any reaction!
"Hua!"
Luo Zheng had reached the consummate stage of the Fledgling Divine Path. He would occasionally cultivate it.
Although the flames he cultivated weren't as strong as those experts who specialized in fire, their power wasn't weak either.
Under Luo Zheng's constant urging, his body had already burned through and turned red …
As the flames grew stronger, traces of golden light appeared on the surface of his body.
This golden light was produced by the True Meaning of Dao contained within his body. If this golden light was allowed to bloom, he would quickly escape to the other side.
This was something Luo Zheng had already confirmed long ago in the Divine Realm …
However, although Luo Zheng didn't know the true nature of this True Meaning of Dao, Luo Zheng could still choose to prevent it from taking effect.
Thus, when he encountered other flames, Luo Zheng could suppress them and not be inexplicably sent to the other side.
As the golden light was suppressed, the golden runes began to swirl again, transforming into small whirlpools that began to absorb his own flames.
As Luo Zheng maintained the raging flames burning his body, he lightly pointed at the ball of Sealing Stone Solution. The Sealing Stone Solution had already landed on his shoulder.
"Slurp …"
The Sealing Stone Solution slid down his shoulder. As it slid down, a small portion of it had already been absorbed into the whirlpool.
Last time, Luo Zheng had only learned about the Sealing Stone Solution after the fact. This time, he saw it with his own eyes and felt that it was inconceivable.
If he could absorb the Sealing Stone Solution, then wouldn't he be able to absorb all sorts of random things?
Luo Zheng suppressed this abnormal thought and focused on absorbing the Sealing Stone Solution.
After about the time it took to burn a stick of incense, the solution that slid down Luo Zheng's skin had completely disappeared.

If it was aimed at other parts of Crane Guard, it would not be able to break apart his Demonic God Possession.
However, two inches from the upper right corner of his chest was his biggest flaw.
Three cracks extended along his chest. Two went downwards and one went upwards. His entire body was like a broken porcelain, turning into pieces.
"Shua!"

Chapter 2850
?
The experts in Fu Su City, Great Xu City and other cities felt their hearts trembling slightly.
Family Su was the weakest in the Ming Hu Clan. After all, they were all blacksmiths and did not have high requirements for their cultivation.

Only they understood how powerful the Chaotic Origin Realm was! That was the first limit that all living beings had to face!
Of Crane Guard and He Zhen, one was at the Half-Step Chaotic Origin Realm.
But in less than half an incense worth of time, they were both killed by Luo Zheng!
This scene had completely defied their understanding of the world!
The Xu Clan Patriarch originally wanted to stop Luo Zheng. He even wanted to act against Luo Zheng.
But after seeing Crane Guard being killed, he froze in midair. He did not know if he should go or not …
He knew that he was not Luo Zheng's opponent, but he could not watch as Xu Youwei was killed by Luo Zheng. For a moment, he was in a dilemma.
After Luo Zheng killed Crane Guard, he no longer hesitated.
Taking advantage of the momentum, he glided through the air like a diving eagle, heading straight for Xu Youwei.
There was another Crane Guard protecting Xu Youwei!
Of the three He Brothers, He Zhen and Crane Guard were stronger by a level. Crane Guard was the weakest.
But Crane Guard's Paramita Token, the Dark Looking Mirror, was extremely useful. It allowed him to spy on the other party without anyone knowing. He was the most useful amongst the three!

Crane Guard's eyes widened like lanterns. A small mirror appeared above his head.

Luo Zheng's figure flashed. He nimbly circled around this silver turbid stream before closing the distance between him and Crane Guard. The sword in his hand trembled lightly.
"Ca!"
This time, Luo Zheng did not use his Sword Marking Technique or his Demonic Eye of Fear.
A silver light pierced through Crane Guard from top to bottom. The last of the three He Brothers died.
After killing the Crane Guard, Luo Zheng floated in the air, looking down at Xu Youwei.
Xu Youwei lay on the surface of the water, the diamond-shaped light screen still protecting him.
Just now, Luo Zheng's all-out attack had already severely injured Xu Youwei through the rhombus-shaped light curtain. This Hermit's divine ability could protect him for a while, but it couldn't protect him forever!
Crane Zhen was dead, the Crane Guards were also dead, everything was over.
After understanding his situation, Xu Youwei's eyes were full of fear. He stared at Luo Zheng and said, "You can't kill me!"
"So you can kill me time and time again?" Luo Zheng's eyes were cold. Even if they looked at each other in the hot summer, they would feel like they were in an ice cellar.
"But you didn't die!" Xu Youwei said excitedly.
"Not dead? Haha … "Luo Zheng's face showed a mocking expression.
The first time, Xu Youwei wanted to kill him indiscriminately in the temple. The second time, he restrained himself at the Seven Mountains Meeting. The third time, he killed his way into the Heart Flow Sword Sect. This time, he followed Luo Zheng here.
If Luo Zheng still let this person go, what he would face would probably be endless pursuit.
"If you kill me, you will regret it! My Taihao Mountain won't let you go, and neither will my father! "Xu Youwei threatened after realizing that arguing was useless.
He was from the Taihao Mountain, one of the seven giants of the Heavenly Palace. How could a small disciple of the Heart Flow Sword Sect provoke him?
At this time, the head of the Xu Family and Su Yu-jin flew over. The head of the Xu Family advised, "Young man, no matter what reason you have, please act rationally. If you kill Xu Youwei, the entire Heavenly Palace will be turned upside down, and there will be no place for you in the Heavenly Palace!"
After saying this, the head of the Xu Family gave Su Yu-jin a look.
Su Yu-jin didn't want to get involved in this matter at all, but the Su Family and the Xu Family had some friendship. Since the head of the Xu Family had asked for his help, he had no choice but to step in.
Objectively speaking, Su Yu-jin also felt that Luo Zheng couldn't kill Xu Youwei.
The Taihao Mountain wasn't a declining family like the Ming Hu Family. It was a colossus, and not something that Luo Zheng could provoke.
"Young Master Luo, you really need to think twice before killing a descendant of the Taihao Mountain," Su Yu-jin said, stopping there.
Luo Zheng turned around and looked at the head of the Xu Family and Su Yu-jin. He asked, "Are you saying that even though Xu Youwei has tried to kill me over and over again, I can't do anything to him? I was born lowly, so do I have to be trampled on? "

The rhombus-shaped light screen created by the Hermit's supernatural power was already very dim. Luo Zheng's sword pierced through the light screen and slashed Xu Youwei's head.
Xu Youwei wanted to say something, but it was too late …
"Splash …"
Xu Youwei fell into the water, his body bobbing up and down with the water.
"This, this is a big deal …" The head of the Xu Family beat his chest and stamped his feet.
When Xu Youwei first came to the Great Xu City, he felt supreme glory. Now that he had died here, how was he going to explain it to the Taihao Mountain?
Su Yu-jin's eyelids were also trembling. Logically speaking, he felt that Luo Zheng was very impulsive. But this person's talent was so heaven-defying, and his courage was even more extraordinary. If he grew up in the future, he would probably become a formidable person.
"Swish swish swish swish …"
When the people of the Xu Family saw Xu Youwei die, many of the family's experts also flew up.
Although these people didn't know the inside story, Xu Youwei was still a member of the Xu Family. How could they be willing to let him off so easily after killing him?
Luo Zheng was indeed powerful, but the Xu Family also relied on their numbers.
Looking at these people blocking in front of him, Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes. He held his long sword horizontally in front of his chest and said indifferently, "Those who stop me, die!"
As he spoke, a red light flashed in Luo Zheng's right eye.
The experts of the Xu Family on the lake suddenly trembled in their hearts. When they looked at Luo Zheng again, there was a shadow in their hearts. The whole family was afraid of Luo Zheng alone!
"Let him leave," the head of the Xu Family said weakly.
Even if the Xu Family did their best, they might not be able to keep Luo Zheng, and the Xu Family would probably be wiped out.
Although the head of the Xu Family was depressed, he was not a fool.
Luo Zheng flew back to the Family Su. Su Youming, Su Youhuo, and the others' faces were stiff, and they didn't dare to say anything.
Luo Zheng killed Xu Youwei, and it would implicate the Family Su, which would be a big trouble!
Only Su Kuan said with a face full of admiration, "Banner Lord is so powerful!"
Luo Zheng stepped forward and patted Su Kuan on the shoulder, saying, "I killed Xu Youwei. Aren't you afraid of being implicated?"
Su Yu-jin, Su Youming, and the others were secretly cursing Su Kuan for being an idiot. At this time, he should've distanced himself from Luo Zheng …

Chapter 2851
Slap

On the other hand, the Yan family in the northwest corner seemed to be in a dilapidated state, which did not fit in with the festive atmosphere.
