She had already agreed to return to the Family Su today to meet up with her young master, but now she was trapped in her own home!

The Yan Family used to be a large family in Minghu. Twenty years ago, the elders in charge of the Yan Family had all suddenly died, leaving behind a group of helpless women and children.
After suffering great losses, the Zhao Family quickly rose to power and continuously nibbled away at the Yan Family's businesses, causing the Yan Family to decline to its current state.

Third Young Master Zhao's face was also full of disdain. "Your Young Master is just a mere disciple of the Heavenly Palace. Does he really think that our Zhao Family is just for show? Even if he comes personally today, what does our Zhao Family have to fear? "

Only the grandsons and grandfathers would bow in such a manner.

How could he treat a mere disciple of the Heaven Palace in such a manner?

Furthermore, the head of the Zhao Family was clear of the nature of his third son. He did not need to ask around to know that he was in the wrong.

Chapter 2853
Invited to the Mountain

However, Luo Zheng also had an important chip in his hands. That was the True Comprehension Chapter of the Eternal Sword Intent.
He did not believe that the True Comprehension Chapter was less valuable than a mere Xu Youwei.
Half a day later, the three of them stepped into the transportation formation.
When they returned to Dragon City, it was dawn.
The starry sky in the sky was gradually dispersing.
Luo Zheng did not go up the mountain directly. Instead, he brought Su Kuan and Xiaoyin out of Dragon City.
Yin Yuehuan had completely adapted to her role.
Even though she was the weakest among the alliance masters, none of them dared to underestimate her.
The divine crystals Luo Zheng gave her supported the operation of her entire alliance.
The number of banner masters under her rule was increasing, and there were more than ten thousand people under her rule. She had become the largest alliance outside of Dragon City.
When Luo Zheng appeared, Yin Yuehuan's face was full of surprise, but there was a trace of unease in the depths of her eyes.
Dao Sword Academy's Yue Baicheng, Qiu Yi, and Lai Huabei also came after hearing the news. They had not seen Luo Zheng for a while.

"You can just call him Banner Master!"
"Yes! Banner Master will always be our Banner Master! "

When Luo Zheng mentioned Mu Ning and the others, Yin Yuehuan's expression hardened slightly. She then said to Luo Zheng seriously, "I also wanted to tell you about this. A few days ago, I sent someone to find you, but you were not at Heart Sword Sect …"

In this Seven Mountains Party, Luo Zheng had defeated the other mountains and won first place. He had offended a lot of people. Although those people did not chase after him as openly as Xu Youwei, they might use some underhanded means to kill him.
If anyone dared to lay a finger on his family …
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's expression darkened.

Facing such a powerful impact, he was afraid that his spine would not be able to withstand it!
Concubine Yan's eyes were emitting golden light. That was the light of the Golden Crow God Fire.
According to logic, she should have stopped him!
Even though she rarely left the Crown Prince Palace and did not know Luo Zheng, how could she not see Luo Zheng's potential?
Such a person should be treated like a treasure in the Heart Flow Sword Sect, right?
If he died in the Crown Prince Palace, even the Crown Prince Palace would not be able to explain themselves.
But seeing Luo Zheng's aura, Concubine Yan had a premonition that Luo Zheng would not be defeated by this attack.
Supported by this premonition, Concubine Yan did not stop him.
"Hong!"
The fist and palm collided, causing the entire Heavenly Refinement Palace to tremble.
Even those concubines deep in the Crown Prince Palace who rarely left their rooms could feel this tremor.
"Ka ka …"
King Stone Mound retracted his right hand. A crack extended along his right hand all the way to his shoulder!
"Ah?"
Feng Ge looked at his Paramita Token with disbelief.
The right hand of King Stone Mound actually cracked!
There were many types of tokens in the Paramita Token, and they had all sorts of strange abilities. But among the descending Paramita Tokens in the Color Realm, King Stone Mound was ranked in the top five!
This Paramita Token was considered the pinnacle of the Color Realm in terms of strength and defense. By relying on this Paramita Token, Feng Ge could fight against Chaotic Origin Realm experts.
But now, it was actually cracked by Luo Zheng's fist …
The eyes of the direct descendants of the Crown Prince's Palace widened when they saw this earth-shattering punch.
"Isn't that fellow too abnormal?"
"I've never heard of such a person in the Heaven Palace …"
"Where is he? Why is it gone? "
"It seems to have been smashed into the ground by Feng Ge's Nirvana Token. Even though he managed to shatter the King of Stone Hills with one punch, he must have been seriously injured as well."
While these direct descendants were discussing, the ground behind Feng Ge suddenly bulged up. Luo Zheng shuttled through it and rushed straight at Feng Ge!
Feng Ge turned around and was even more shocked to see Luo Zheng still alive and kicking. Even King Shi Qiu couldn't withstand the extreme palm strike of King Shi Qiu, but this guy was still unharmed?
"Phew …"
Seeing Luo Zheng coming straight for him, Feng Ge's eyes suddenly flashed with golden light, as if two blazing suns were blooming in his eyes.
"Ah … Feng Ge! No! "
Concubine Yan's expression changed when she saw that Feng Ge was about to release the Golden Crow Divine Fire.
However, when Luo Zheng came out of the ground, he was already very close to Feng Ge. It was too late for Concubine Yan to stop Feng Ge.
"Swoosh!"
Golden flames poured out of Feng Ge's body and flowed in all directions with him as the center. The golden flames almost burned everything along the way.
The direct descendants of the Heavenly Palace all turned pale when they saw the golden flames!
The Golden Crow Divine Fire was in the triangular cauldron at the entrance of the Qianlian Palace. They knew the power of the flames very well. Even a little bit of it would kill them!
If Concubine Yan didn't stop the flames, the entire Qianlian Palace would be burned down!
As the direct descendants of the Heavenly Palace fled, the ribbon on Concubine Yan's body flew out and turned into a big circle, blooming with golden light. This big circle firmly encircled the surging Golden Crow Divine Fire.
The Golden Crow Divine Fire was controlled, but Luo Zheng was in trouble!
Concubine Yan was very regretful. If she had known this would happen, she wouldn't have stopped the two children from fighting each other.
When she turned her head to look, her beautiful eyes were suddenly filled with endless shock!
In the golden flames, Luo Zheng's body was shining with golden light. He was not afraid of the Golden Crow Divine Fire at all.
He grabbed Feng Ge's snow-white neck with one hand and suddenly pushed forward. His whole body was pressed down on Feng Ge. He sneered and said, "Your flames are more powerful than those of the Hive Banshees, but it's wishful thinking to burn me to death! Hand them over! If you don't, I'll kill you! "
Feng Ge's temperament was also surprisingly strong!
She didn't have any intention of backing down. Flame emblems ignited on the surface of her body and wrapped her tightly. At the same time, she said sarcastically, "Your means are indeed beyond my expectations. But can you kill me?"
"Hahaha, you'll know soon!"
Luo Zheng laughed, and the pupil of his right eye suddenly became deep.
Specks of white light converged. At the same time, with a 'ceng' sound, a snowy sword appeared in his hand.
With Luo Zheng's current Demonic Eye of Fear, it was hard for him to see through experts who had passed through the Chaotic Origin Reconstruction. Even though Feng Ge was powerful, she was still within the Color Realm with Luo Zheng. She had never undergone the Chaotic Origin Reconstruction before.
With a single glance, he could see that there were many flaws on her body.
The only thing that could protect her was the flame emblems that wrapped around her. These flame emblems had indeed protected her tightly.
But by relying on his' True Vision ', Luo Zheng could see that these flame emblems weren't perfect. Between the gaps between the flame emblems, there were still fragments of flaws.
'Below the neck!'
In this region, the flaws of the flame emblems overlapped with those of Feng Ge.
With this sword, Feng Ge would undoubtedly die!
"Die!"
At the moment when Luo Zheng pierced down, Feng Ge finally felt a strand of fear in her heart.
Under the protection of the flame emblems, Luo Zheng should not be able to kill her. But she had a premonition that this sword would cause her to fall apart!
And at this moment, the red light in Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. The fear in her heart was mobilized!
She could vaguely see a trace of an illusion. Her body was like a porcelain that fell to the ground, shattering with a 'hua' sound. When the emotions in her heart were stirred, the line of defense that she maintained with her cold indifference completely collapsed.
"Ah! Don't kill me … "
Feng Ge's body straightened. Her hands curled up instinctively. The moment tears flowed out from her eyes, they were completely evaporated by the golden flames.
She had never feared death so much in her life.
"No!"
Concubine Yan felt anxious when she saw this scene.
"Weng!"
The snowy sword stopped abruptly. The sword tip stopped right before Feng Ge's chest, buzzing and trembling.
"Let them go, and I won't kill you," Luo Zheng said.
If he really killed Feng Ge now, it would be the worst choice.
But this evil woman was too stubborn. If he didn't force her to this point, she wouldn't give in at all!
"Don't kill me, don't kill me …"
Feng Ge turned a deaf ear to Luo Zheng's words and repeated them repeatedly.

Chapter 2859
The Outsider

A purple ribbon flew through the air and Luo Zheng was sent flying.
Concubine Yan rushed forward and held Feng Ge in her arms, her face full of worry.

It was reasonable to say that as long as the Demon Eye of Fear stopped releasing the power of fear, the corresponding fear in one's heart would disappear.
Luo Zheng just released the ability of the Demon Eye of Fear to scare her, but he didn't expect that Feng Ge would fall into an emotional state.
The Golden Crow Divine Fire gradually extinguished, and the surroundings were burned black.

Concubine Yan reached out and gently pressed Feng Ge's forehead. A soft soul aura bloomed, and Feng Ge gradually calmed down.

The True Meaning of Good and Evil was a very unpopular True Meaning of Dao in Taiyi Heaven Palace, but Concubine Yan could see it at a glance, although she didn't know that Luo Zheng used the ability of the Demon Eye of Fear.
"Yes," Luo Zheng replied.
Concubine Yan sighed, but she didn't blame Luo Zheng. Instead, she blamed herself. "I shouldn't have … When Feng Ge was born, she underwent a very cruel test in the Hive. This kind of method will evoke these memories."
The environment in which the Hive demonesses grew up was far from as gentle as the human race.
Especially Feng Ge, who was judged to be the most likely person to lead the Hive and become Phoenix since birth.
From birth, Feng Ge had to compete with the other Hive demonesses. Countless young demonesses died because of this, and she grew up little by little in blood and bones. If Donghuang hadn't single-handedly killed his way into the Hive to take Feng Ge back when she was six years old, the current Phoenix would have been Feng Ge.
Although Feng Ge returned to Taiyi Palace for many years, the memories of her childhood were difficult to erase. Concubine Yan used special means to seal those memories in the depths of her soul.
Some of the magical powers activated by the True Meaning of Good and Evil could still evoke memories of that time.
Because of those cruel experiences in her childhood, Feng Ge's indifferent character was nurtured.
"Then why didn't you stop her just now?" Luo Zheng asked with a frown.
With the existence of Concubine Yan, Luo Zheng did not expect that he would be able to fight with Feng Ge. Moreover, she had been watching from the sidelines!
Luo Zheng was also angry and could only force her to hand over Han Liusu and the others.
Concubine Yan gently rubbed the space between Feng Ge's eyebrows, a desolate look flashing across her face. "I just want to see how far you can push her …"
After returning to Taiyi Palace for so many years, Feng Ge had always been indifferent to the outside world. Even if Concubine Yan accompanied her all day long, it was difficult to see any change in her expression. As a mother, it was indeed very anxious to see her daughter like this.
No matter what means were used, she could not be changed at all. In her heart, there was always a deep fear of this world, so she was completely indifferent to everything.
"Wouldn't it be more painful for me to force her?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Concubine Yan chuckled and said, "No, at least when she faces you, her expression will be much richer. As her mother, I have never seen her angry. Today, she even cried …"
When Concubine Yan brought Luo Zheng here, Feng Ge was indeed very indifferent to her mother.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng could not help but roll his eyes in his heart. Why did he feel that this mother and son were both crazy? Do you want me to scare her every now and then?
"The Raging Fire is not a punishment, but a way of cultivation. Even the direct descendants of Taiyi Palace need to cherish this way of cultivation. The people you are looking for should not be in danger," Concubine Yan said again.
Luo Zheng was stunned for a moment. He cursed in his heart. If you two had made things clear, why did you have to fight?
Feng Ge lay for a while, then slowly woke up in Concubine Yan's arms.
After opening her eyes, she glared fiercely at Luo Zheng. She struggled out of Concubine Yan's arms, stood up, and turned to leave.
"You lost to me," Luo Zheng said behind her.
Feng Ge stopped and responded indifferently, "What do you want?"
"Take me to them," Luo Zheng said again.
Feng Ge paused for a moment and said, "Come with me."
Concubine Yan motioned to Luo Zheng to follow him. Luo Zheng followed Feng Ge into Chenghuo Hall.
Concubine Yan stood still and looked at Luo Zheng's tall and straight back. A mysterious smile appeared on her face, but she murmured, "He is not afraid of the Golden Crow Divine Fire, and his strength is greater than Feng Ge's. He seems like a suitable little enemy …"

In Chenghuo Hall, wisps of hot air came out.
Chains hung from the beams in the hall. These chains were burned red and hung down like noodles. Looking up the chains, one could see the pillars, eaves, and even the cracks on the ground in Chenghuo Hall. There were faint flames flowing in the pillars, eaves, and even in the cracks on the ground.
Luo Zheng was curious and wanted to ask what the people in Chenghuo Hall practiced. But thinking of Feng Ge's angry face, he chose to keep quiet.
After passing through a long and thin corridor, they entered a side hall.
As soon as they stepped into the side hall, they sensed a wisp of soul aura in the scorching waves …
Luo Zheng's eyes brightened. This aura was very similar to the aura of the Soul Elixir. It was similar to the aura of the starlight outside Dragon City, but it was ten times stronger here!
In this side hall, Xun, Mu Ning, and Han Liusu were sitting cross-legged. There was a burning chain hanging above their heads. Below the chain, there were small circles of fire tornadoes, which emitted strong soul aura.
It was the first time for Luo Zheng to see such a way of practicing the soul. He was quite surprised.
"Is this the Raging Fire?" Luo Zheng asked.
"What else do you think it is?" Feng Ge was still angry when facing Luo Zheng.
Now it was Luo Zheng's turn to be embarrassed. He scratched his head and said, "I thought it was some kind of torture …"
Feng Ge stared at Luo Zheng fiercely. At this time, Mu Ning opened her eyes. The fire tornadoes above her head disappeared along the chain. She saw Feng Ge and smiled, "Little Master, I feel my aura has improved again! Eh? Luo Zheng? "
"What does' eh 'mean …"
Seeing Mu Ning calling Feng Ge so intimately, Luo Zheng suddenly felt like he was an outsider.
At this time, Han Liusu and Xun woke up. Han Liusu saw Luo Zheng and smiled sweetly, "You finally found this place!"
"Little Master said that I'm afraid you won't be able to enter the Crown Prince's Palace," Xun added.

Chapter 2860
?

From the beginning, the three girls were forced to come up the mountain, and they did have some resistance.
However, Feng Ge did not make it difficult for them. Instead, he really took them into the Taishi Palace and trained them as disciples of the direct line.
The environment outside Xianglong City was naturally much better than the Taishi Palace. It could be said that even the Heart-Flow Sword Sect could not compare to the Taishi Palace. In addition, Feng Ge would give them pointers from time to time. Together with their good talent, they improved rapidly these days!
Thus, the three girls started calling him Little Master this and Little Master this …
Seeing that Luo Zheng was unhappy, Han Liusu took the initiative to hold Luo Zheng's hand and said coquettishly, "Anyway, it's safe enough here. If we were in Dragon City, we might not be able to reach the Nirvana Realm even after decades. Here, we can greatly shorten the time!"
There were all kinds of risks before reaching the Nirvana Realm, such as the Sea of Truth, the Great Whirlpool, and the Nine-Dotted Line. These were all checkpoints. Many people were trapped in the Great Whirlpool and died in the Nine-Dotted Line. However, by relying on the Taishi Palace, these risks were greatly reduced.
Luo Zheng also realized this.
Even if he earned a lot of Divine Crystals with his ability to comprehend the Dao with a single thought, he couldn't provide such a good environment.

She had lost to Luo Zheng not because she was weak, but because her understanding of the True Comprehension Chapter was not as good as Luo Zheng's.
Even if she were to directly ask for it, she couldn't possibly disregard her own reputation.

Her original goal was indeed to use the three of them to threaten Luo Zheng into handing over the True Comprehension Chapter.

After arranging for them to enter the Taishi Palace, Feng Ge discovered that he and Mu Ning and the others hit it off very well. After a few times, Feng Ge even gave them pointers on their cultivation methods. Hence, they were called "Little Master".

These past few years, Feng Ge had been indifferent to other matters. The only thing he was interested in was cultivation, which was why he took the time to give pointers to Mu Ning and the others. It seemed that teaching people how to cultivate was also a kind of pleasure.
Feng Ge grabbed the paper and read a few words, his eyes shining brightly.
She carefully folded the rice paper and asked, "Is there anything else? If the True Comprehension Chapter is completely deciphered, it will be more than this. "
How could Luo Zheng hand over the entire True Comprehension Chapter?
He just laughed and said, "I don't know when the rest will be deciphered. This is all. If they can successfully break through to the Nirvana Realm, I may be able to decipher some …"
Feng Ge raised her eyebrows again. She was skeptical about Luo Zheng's words, but she couldn't do anything about it. However, she was still satisfied with the 180 words of the True Comprehension Chapter.
Next, Luo Zheng reminded Mu Ning and the others before leaving the Chenghuo Hall.

Luo Zheng then asked, "Concubine Yan, do you have anything else to say to me?"
Concubine Yan was also a very smart person. When she saw Luo Zheng come out alone, she already understood that Luo Zheng's three women would have to stay in the Taishidi Palace.
"Cultivating in the Taishidi Palace is indeed much better than cultivating in Dragon City. They will be treated as the direct descendants of the Heavenly Palace if they stay here," Concubine Yan said.
"Thank you, Concubine Yan," Luo Zheng nodded and said.
Concubine Yan suddenly stretched out her hand, and there was a light green jade token in her hand. "This is the token of the Taishidi Palace. Next time you enter the Taishidi Palace, you don't have to force your way into the Taishidi Mirage. The Taishidi Mirage hasn't been fully opened. You're lucky to be able to break in. Next time, you won't be so lucky …"
This time, Luo Zheng casually walked into the Taishidi Palace, which would definitely attract the attention of the guards. If Luo Zheng wanted to enter again, it would be difficult for him to rely on the Demon Eye of Fear.
Luo Zheng took the token, looked at it, and directly stuffed it into the Sumeru Ring.
Before Luo Zheng left, he heard Concubine Yan say with a smile, "If you have time, you can come visit the Taishidi Palace more often …"
Looking at Concubine Yan's enigmatic smile, Luo Zheng felt a little apprehensive.
Xun and the others were cultivating here, so he would definitely come again. But what did this have to do with Concubine Yan?
When he came out of the Taishidi Palace, the thick white fog of the Taishidi Mirage receded to both sides. The road under his feet was also paved with fog, soft as cotton.
After flying along this road for a while, Luo Zheng arrived at the Cloud Mountain Palace halfway up the mountain.

Chapter 2861
Please Enter Taihao Mountain
Luo Zheng ignored them and walked toward the Coiling Dragon Ancient Path.
As he passed by the black-robed old man, one of the young men asked, "Sir, are you Young Master Luo?"
Luo Zheng stopped in his tracks. He was on high alert, but when he remembered that this was Taihao Mountain, he nodded. "Yes."
A smile appeared on the young man's face. "Very well, Young Master Luo, please come to Taihao Mountain."
Upon hearing the words' Taihao Mountain ', Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes and said, "Not interested."
After killing Xu Youwei at Ming Lake, Luo Zheng knew that Taihao Mountain would not let the matter rest.
However, he was still a disciple of the Heavenly Palace and an elite of the Heart Flow Sword Sect. Even if they really wanted to take revenge, they would only dare to do so in secret. He did not expect these people to openly wait for him here and 'invite' him to Taihao Mountain.
Luo Zheng was not an idiot. Why would he agree? With that, he started to walk away.
Two young men blocked Luo Zheng's path. The young man on the right smiled politely. "I'm afraid it's not up to you."
"You want to fight here?" Luo Zheng's expression suddenly turned cold and his voice grew louder.
At this time, the black-clothed old man sitting by the big rock faintly sighed. "It seems that you don't know what kind of disaster you have caused. Let alone making a move here, even if I kill you here, so what? However, we have no intention of killing you. We're just working for someone else... "
As the black-robed old man spoke, he released a faintly discernible aura.
This aura was not strong, but Luo Zheng found that he could not resist it!
"This person is very powerful..."
Luo Zheng's pupils shrank slightly. Just as Luo Zheng was considering whether to escape into the Cloud Mountain Palace or the Coiling Dragon Ancient Path, a silver circle of light suddenly appeared under Luo Zheng's feet. This silver circle of light was a pitch-black space.
Luo Zheng felt the air under his feet disappear. Knowing that something was wrong, he tried to fly up.
Little did he know that the power contained within this pitch-black space was firmly sucking him in, not allowing him to resist at all. In the blink of an eye, he had completely fallen into it.
The black-clothed old man gently waved his hand, and a small golden monkey jumped out from his head.
This golden monkey was emitting the aura of the Nirvana Realm. It was a living Nirvana Realm Token!

At this moment, there was a sudden roar in front of the Cloud Mountain Palace!
Boom!

Guchen stepped into the Cloud Mountain Palace and said with a dark face, "Desolation Dragon! How could you deceive me? Coming to my Greater One Mountain to capture people, this is too much! "
Early in the morning, this black-clothed old man called "Desolation Dragon" brought his disciples to visit the Cloud Mountain Palace.
Desolation Dragon was a famous expert. Although his reputation was not as good as Qiu Yinhe and He Chi, his strength was not far off.
Guchen was also puzzled. After all, the Great Hao Mountain was a branch of its own. The people of the Great Hao Mountain and the people of the Greater One Mountain did not interact much. However, Guchen respected him as a senior, so he naturally treated him well.

If Guchen did not use the formation plate of the Coiling Dragon Ancient Road to observe, Luo Zheng would have been quietly taken away by Desolation Dragon!

"Let him stay. Everything can be discussed," Guchen said with an unfriendly expression.
On the Greater One Mountain, Guchen and Wen Si played the role of guide, but Guchen's status was higher than Wen Si's. After all, he was in charge of the entire Coiling Dragon Ancient Road and a part of the Mountain Protecting Formation.
As a member of the Greater One Mountain, Guchen also knew the relationship between Luo Zheng and Qiu Yinhe. Now he naturally had to step forward to protect him. How could he let Desolation Dragon take away Luo Zheng so easily?

Among the Paramita tokens, the most precious and the most powerful ones were all living creatures.
When Guchen saw the little golden monkey, there was a look of fear on his face.

Luo Zheng's performance on the Coiling Dragon Ancient Road was still fresh in Guchen's memory.

Just as Desolation Dragon said, this matter was beyond Guchen's ability. After pondering for a while, he decided to report this matter to Qiu Yinhe.

Chapter 2862
The Soul Snatching Hand

Now that he was killed by me, you want me to give you an explanation?"
The beautiful woman in red said indifferently, "Even if it's like what you said, you're not dead. The one who died was my son!"
At first glance, what the beautiful woman in red said did make some sense.
Xu Youwei had paid with his life, and Luo Zheng was standing here perfectly fine.
It was not unreasonable for his mother to find Luo Zheng and ask for an explanation.
"Don't beat around the bush. What do you want?" Luo Zheng asked in a low voice.
The beautiful woman in red stood up from her chair, and leisurely walked to Luo Zheng's side. Her pair of breathtaking eyes seemed to want to see through Luo Zheng. Then she opened her mouth and said, "I heard that you won first place at the Seven Mountains Meeting, and displayed extraordinary strength. It seems to have something to do with the True Meaning of the Dao, right?"
Luo Zheng stood where he was and did not respond.
"Those talisman formations that Qiu Yinhe made were all made by the Imperial Sword Seal. Even if you are a genius who can comprehend the Dao with a single thought, you can't do that. What are you relying on?" The beautiful woman in red smiled.
Looking at the smiling face of the beautiful woman in red, Luo Zheng immediately understood her purpose — the True Comprehension Chapter.
Luo Zheng sighed inwardly in the face of her sudden change in attitude.
It seemed that Xu Youwei really didn't mean much to Taihao Mountain. How was this the attitude of someone whose son had died?
That was indeed the case.
For example, in the Taishi Palace, the number of Donghuang's subordinates was not small, but the number of his direct subordinates was even greater.
There were more disciples in the Taishi Palace than there were in the Heart Flow Sword Sect. The ones with real status were those who were talented and favored by Donghuang. Feng Ge was one of them.
It was the same for Taihao Mountain. Xu Shou was born in the Minghu Xu Family and had been in the Center God Province for about the same amount of time as Donghuang. After countless years, he probably had tens of thousands of sons, and the descendants of his sons also had sons. These were all considered direct descendants, which was also a huge number.
Xu Youwei was one of the best among his many sons. In terms of status and importance, he could not be compared to Feng Ge of the Taishi Palace.
"I relied on my strength," Luo Zheng answered seriously.
The beautiful woman in red stared at Luo Zheng with some disappointment in her beautiful eyes. She shook her head and smiled. "Your strength is not bad. You killed Crane Guard and Crane Gui by yourself. That is really surprising. But Qiu Yinhe's Imperial Sword Seal is a very special Theurgy. It can only be activated by the Qi of Trueness. It has nothing to do with your strength."
She changed the topic and said, "I heard that there is a sequel to the Eternal Sword Intent, and this sequel is the essence. It has never been deciphered, but now it seems that someone has done it?"
Luo Zheng looked at her blankly and shrugged. "I don't know what the sequel is."
"Is that so?"
The beautiful woman in red stretched out her hand, and a piece of yellow paper appeared in her hand. There were more than 20 tiny words recorded on the yellow paper.
Luo Zheng glanced at the small words. It really did record the first 20 words of the True Comprehension Chapter.
He had already published 150 words of the True Comprehension Chapter. Qiu Yinhe, Lin Zhanting, and He Chi were bound to publish it as well. With Taihao Mountain's ability, it was not difficult to obtain this True Comprehension Chapter.
Actually, Taihao Mountain had spent a lot of effort to obtain these 20 words.
Qiu Yinhe, Lin Zhanting, and the others attached great importance to the True Comprehension Chapter. Even though they only had 150 words, they did not publish them all at once. For example, only a few direct descendants of Taiyi Mountain had obtained 150 words, while the elites only had the 20 words version.
The Taihao Mountain had obtained these twenty words.
"These 20 words are the continuation of the Eternal Sword Intent, but it is too little. Based on our judgment, there are at least a thousand to two thousand words in the continuation. These are in your hands." The beautiful woman in red circled behind Luo Zheng and said, "Hand over the entire continuation, and I will forgive you for killing my son. How about it?"
Luo Zheng glanced at the beautiful woman in red with a strange expression.
Use the entire True Comprehension Chapter to obtain her forgiveness? This dream was a little too perfect.
The True Comprehension Chapter was very important. After all, it was related to the foundation of the Heavenly Palace. If there was an opportunity, he would definitely hand it over. However, he would need to obtain the support of the Heavenly Palace, or even the support of the entire Li Clan.
Before this opportunity arrived, Luo Zheng could not hand it over.
Luo Zheng could not agree to rely on Xu Youwei's life to obtain the entire True Comprehension Chapter.
"I did decipher these 20 words," Luo Zheng said.
After wasting so much time, Luo Zheng finally admitted it. The eyes of the beautiful woman in red and the black-robed men not far away lit up, indicating that their judgment was correct.
"But I have only deciphered 150 words in total, and I have already given them to Senior Qiu Yinhe. If you want, I can write them for you," Luo Zheng said again.
Since he had already handed over the 150 words, sooner or later it would spread to the entire Heavenly Palace, and even to the entire Human Clan, just like the Eternal Sword Intent. He did not mind writing them again.
"Not enough," the beautiful woman in red stared at Luo Zheng and said with a smile, "I want all of them."
Luo Zheng shrugged his shoulders, revealing an embarrassed expression. "That is difficult. I have not deciphered it myself. How can I give you all of them?"
Unexpectedly, the beautiful woman in red shook her head and said, "I don't believe what you said. Therefore, you have two choices in front of you. You can either write the entire continuation yourself, or …"
The beautiful woman in red looked at the black-robed men.
One of the black-robed men strode out, and a shadow appeared above his head. A green fog was rising, and a green arm faintly appeared in the green fog.
"That is the Nirvana Token of the 17th Layer of Heaven, called the Hand of Snatching Soul. It will not harm your soul, but it can write down the things in your memory stroke by stroke." The beautiful woman in red smiled with a hint of eeriness.
Divine Paths such as the Great Ruling Spell had little effect on the strong men of the Border Region. As long as Luo Zheng resisted, it would be difficult to search his soul.
However, there were some similar tokens in the Nirvana Realm, and the Hand of Snatching Soul was one of them.

Chapter 2863
Translator: Transn Editor: Transn
A Little Trick
When Luo Zheng revealed the True Comprehension Chapter to Qiu Yinhe and He Chi, Qiu Yinhe and He Chi obviously had some doubts.
They also wanted the complete True Comprehension Chapter, but from their standpoint, they naturally wouldn't force Luo Zheng to reveal it. They even tried to use Soul Searching.
They could only listen to what Luo Zheng said.
But the people of Taihao Mountain were different. If the beautiful woman in red wanted to achieve her goal, she certainly wouldn't stop just because Luo Zheng said "I haven't deciphered it."
When she invited Luo Zheng here, she had already prepared everything!
"Have you thought it through? Do you want to do it yourself, or do you want us to do it? "The beautiful woman in red stared at Luo Zheng with a complacent expression.
Luo Zheng frowned and said with a sullen face, "I've already found the way to decipher the True Comprehension Chapter. Although I've only deciphered 150 characters, I'll be able to decipher the entire chapter in a few years. When the time comes, I'll present it to Donghuang, and he'll pass it on to the entire Heavenly Palace. Do you think I'll leave only Taihao Mountain behind?"
Luo Zheng was a little puzzled by the anxious actions of the beautiful woman in red. Why was she so anxious?
Unexpectedly, she didn't pay any attention to Luo Zheng. She turned to the black-robed man and said, "Immortal Ghost, do it."
The black-robed man strode straight toward Luo Zheng. The green hand above his head also grabbed at Luo Zheng.
How could Luo Zheng be willing to comply?
There was more than one True Comprehension Chapter in his mind, and there were too many important secrets.
The Primal Chaos Ancient Gods, the Realm of the Gods, Luo Nian, the Li Clan …
Even if he died, Luo Zheng couldn't let others know.
Dong!
Luo Zheng suddenly kicked his legs and flew backward.
But before he could fly more than three meters, the beautiful woman in red waved her hand gently. Luo Zheng only saw a flash in front of his eyes, and he was back to his original position.
Bang!
Even so, Luo Zheng rushed to the right again.
The same strange scene appeared. After a distance of three meters, Luo Zheng returned to his original place.
It was as if there was an invisible space corridor around Luo Zheng. No matter how hard Luo Zheng tried, he was still going in circles.
"Little brother, don't struggle in vain." The beautiful woman in red shook her head.
Luo Zheng's expression became extremely anxious when he saw the immortal ghost walking towards him with its green hand pressing down on his head.
At this time, 9527's voice sounded in Luo Zheng's mind, "It's just the Hand of Soul Snatcher. Let him search."
"Do you have a solution?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Of course." 9527 chuckled. "Just relax. I guarantee that nothing will be found from him."
Although 9527 was only a Divided Soul and could not help much in the contest of strength, it still had many tricks up its sleeve. Luo Zheng calmed down a little after hearing what it said.
Of course, Luo Zheng was still very nervous on the surface.
"Hua!"
When the man in black approached Luo Zheng, the Hand of Soul Snatcher went straight to the top of Luo Zheng's head and clasped five fingers on the top of his head.
Luo Zheng suddenly felt a whirlpool-like soul aura rushing into his mind.
At the same time, 9527 also acted. Luo Zheng only felt a slight numbness in his soul. His whole mind became very slow. Then he stood there blankly, letting the Hand of Soul Snatcher invade him.
The man in black was originally very confident, with a pitiful smile on his face. The green gas on the surface of the Hand of Soul Snatcher was rolling, and one of his hands was emitting a green luster.
Luo Zheng's memory was also constantly being absorbed into the mind of the man in black, becoming a part of his memory.
The beautiful woman in red looked at the man in black with concern and asked, "Have you found it? Do you want me to prepare a brush and ink for you to write it down? "
"Not yet," the immortal ghost replied.
In the mind of the immortal ghost, countless jumbled memories emerged.
The life of a beggar …
A butterfly being preyed on by a spider …
Even the evolution of an Inner World …
There was a faintly strange expression on his face. According to his experience, a person's memory was distributed linearly, from childhood to adulthood. As long as there was nothing wrong with the brain, it was generally arranged neatly.
But why was this kid's memory so strange?
"Those memory fragments …"
The immortal ghost continued to investigate, and found some memory fragments with unique shapes.
Under normal circumstances, the immortal ghost would ignore these memory fragments. But he hadn't found any valuable information in the soul search, so he could only try to interpret these memory fragments.
When he deciphered the first memory fragment, a look of pleasant surprise appeared on his face.
It was the first ten words of the True Comprehension Chapter!
As for why such important information was scattered in the memory fragments, the immortal ghost couldn't figure it out.
But now that he had found a clue, how could the immortal ghost let go of such an opportunity?
The memory fragments in Luo Zheng's soul were absorbed into his own mind, and then absorbed into the depths of his soul for interpretation.
But just as he absorbed those memory fragments and began to interpret them, his eyes suddenly widened, his body began to tremble, and his face suddenly became deathly pale!
"These, these … too many … damn it!" The immortal ghost began to talk nonsense.
"Immortal ghost, what's wrong with you?" The beautiful woman in red looked surprised.
The other men in black also looked puzzled.
The immortal ghost's strength was not strong among them, but the Hand of Soul Snatcher was a very important ability. Over the years, the immortal ghost had indeed solved many difficult problems. How could there be an accident when facing a little kid like Luo Zheng this time?
The immortal ghost's body trembled, and he kept retreating. Finally, with a plop, he fell to the ground and fainted.
Luo Zheng also returned to normal, and his mind became agile again. Seeing the immortal ghost fall in front of him, he was also full of surprise, and asked in his mind, "9527, what did you do?"
The immortal ghost's soul level was much stronger than Luo Zheng's. Even if 9527 was a part of the soul of the Ancient God of Chaos, he couldn't suppress the immortal ghost to this extent. If it had been a Brain Master, it might have been possible.
"I didn't do anything. Doesn't he like to search souls? I just stuffed a lot of memories into his soul. His soul can't bear so many memories, hehe … "9527 laughed proudly.
At first, 9527's personality was similar to that of the Lord of Chaos, but now that he had been with Luo Zheng for a long time, his self-awareness had gradually awakened, and his personality had become more and more distinct. If it had been him, he would have disdained to use such a little trick.
Those memory fragments were a trap deliberately set up by 9527, to make the immortal ghost think that the important information was in the fragments, but in fact, it was in other memory fragments. 9527 stuffed a lot of memories into them.
These memories almost included the entire creation of the Chaos. Most of the observations that the Lord of Chaos had made were not practical. More importantly, these memories were not deciphered by mere humans.

Chapter 2864
Emperor Shou
As the saying goes, a man whose heart is content is like a snake trying to swallow an elephant. The Immortal Ghost absorbed the memory fragments into his mind and soul to interpret them.
What he swallowed was not an elephant, but a whale.

The strength of this beautiful woman in red was too strong. Even with the protection of the stone sealing solution, the power of her palm still slightly injured him.
The beautiful woman in red's eyes were cold and full of astonishment.
She thought that this palm landing could beat Luo Zheng half-dead. This brat actually forcefully endured this palm and did not faint!

The beautiful woman in red said sternly, then turned around and walked away.
The other two men in black came forward and dragged Luo Zheng to the back of the palace. One of the men in black stamped his foot, and the ground of the palace suddenly split open. A square hole appeared, and then they threw Luo Zheng into it.
During the whole process, Luo Zheng did not resist.
These people were far stronger than him. Resistance was useless, and would only lead to more severe revenge.
In the hole, Luo Zheng was unusually calm. He sat cross-legged.
On one hand, they were afraid of Greater One Heavenly Palace. On the other hand, they also wanted to get the True Comprehension Chapter. From these two points, he should be safe. But he could not guarantee what they would do next.
This time he relied on 9527's means to get through, but the next time might not necessarily be the same.

"Greetings, Consort Xuan!"

The black-armored imperial guards knew that she was extremely furious, but Hao Ran Sword Hall was not a place that could be casually barged in. Even Lady Xuan was not allowed to do so. They could only helplessly stop her.

"He's lying!" Lady Xuan raised her eyebrows.
Whether Luo Zheng was lying or not was actually only fifty-fifty. However, people like Lady Xuan usually wouldn't give up until they reached their goal. Naturally, she believed that Luo Zheng was lying.
"So what if he's lying? Didn't I send the immortal ghost of the dark department over?" Xu Shou asked.
At the mention of the immortal ghost, Lady Xuan's eyes flashed with a trace of sharpness. "I don't know what that kid did. The immortal ghost did successfully read his memories, but it fainted halfway!"
Although Luo Zheng's soul level was outstanding, it was still worlds apart from the immortal ghost. Naturally, he couldn't have used a soul attack. This was something that Lady Xuan couldn't understand.
Xu Shou's face showed a trace of interest. "He made the immortal ghost faint? Bring the immortal ghost over for me to see! "
Lady Xuan helplessly conveyed Xu Shou's summons. Very quickly, the immortal ghost was carried to Hao Ran Sword Hall by several black-clothed people.
Xu Shou raised his hand slightly, and the unconscious immortal ghost slowly floated up. Xu Shou flicked a finger at the immortal ghost, and a strand of brown light entered the immortal ghost's mind. Only then did the immortal ghost slowly wake up.
Seeing Xu Shou sitting on the golden dragon throne, the immortal ghost's expression changed. It respectfully knelt on the ground and said, "The immortal ghost greets Emperor Shou!"
"You've already used the Soul Seizing Hand for many years. You're already very proficient in it. Why would such a problem occur when you're investigating a mere brat from a perverted realm?" Xu Shou asked calmly.
The immortal ghost recalled that moment, and its face was still full of shock.
"That kid, that kid isn't human at all!" the immortal ghost said with wide eyes.
It was impossible for a person to have such a huge amount of memories.
There were too many memories in those fragments. One of them was watching the sun rise and set for a thousand years, ten thousand years, a hundred million years, ten billion years. Moreover, it recorded everything, no matter how big or small, until the sun self-destructed!
Not to mention that Luo Zheng's skeletal age was only a hundred years. How could there be such a bored person in this world?
Even the ancient Stone Clan and the slow-moving Turtle Clan would not be able to do such a thing.
After hearing the immortal ghost's words, Xu Shou, Lady Xuan, and even the two Dark Tribe leaders in the shadows were all baffled. How could there be such a ridiculous memory in this world?
"Perhaps … he's holding some special Paramita Token. These memories belong to the Paramita Token," Xu Shou said thoughtfully.
Most Paramita Tokens carried a portion of memories. Such a huge amount of memories was unheard of, but it was also Xu Shou's speculation.
"The key is how to make that kid talk," Lady Xuan said with a sigh.

Chapter 2865
Searching the Mountain
The dark department had countless torture methods, and they didn't think Luo Zheng could withstand it.

Outside Taihao Mountain.

On the other side, He Chi's momentum was much smaller. He was dressed in a green robe and held a long sword in one hand. His eyes were like torches.
Every time a flaw appeared in the Backwind Thousand Sunset Sword Formation, He Chi would strike with his sword.

Although Qiu Yinhe and He Chi were part of the Heavenly Jiedu and could be considered protectors of a region, it was very unwise to directly attack Taihao Mountain's protective formation.

Just now, the two of them were outside Taihao Mountain and wanted to see the Longevity Emperor.

The two flew towards the top of Taihao Mountain and quickly entered the Haoran Sword Palace.

Heavenly Jiedu's status wasn't as high as the Mountain Lord's, but Heavenly Jiedu was bestowed by the Eastern Emperor and could be considered a king. Naturally, he didn't need to kowtow like the others.
Xu Shou sat on the golden dragon throne with an ashen face. He slapped his hand on the armrest of the throne, "Greetings? Is this how the two Heavenly Jiedu greet me? "
Seeing Xu Shou's attitude, He Chi's expression was serious. He found it funny. This person's acting skills were quite profound.
Qiu Yinhe didn't buy it and said loudly, "To be honest, I didn't come to Taihao Mountain to greet Lord Longevity Emperor. A disciple of my Heart Sword Sect called Luo Zheng was brought to Taihao Mountain by Huang Long. This person is related to the fate of Taiyi Heaven Palace. I was blocked by the mountain protection formation and could only choose to break it in desperation. If I offended you in any way, please forgive me!"
Anyone with a discerning eye could see that Xu Shou was stalling for time. How could Qiu Yinhe give him this chance?
"Forgive?" Xu Shou sneered, "When did my Taihao Mountain become so forgiving?"
Qiu Yinhe's expression didn't change as he continued, "Whether Lord Longevity forgives me or not, please hand over Luo Zheng today. In the future, I will personally apologize to Lord Longevity!"
His words were directed at Luo Zheng. Xu Shou didn't know what to do. He knew that this Heavenly Jiedu was a carefree person, but he didn't expect him to be so unreliable!
Xu Shou could only say with a sullen face, "There is no such person as Luo Zheng in my Taihao Mountain. I'm afraid you've come to the wrong place!"
"I'm not wrong. If I search the mountain, I can find Luo Zheng in a moment!" Qiu Yinhe said.
"Ah, search the mountain …" Xu Shou's anger became more and more intense. He was really angry. A terrifying aura flowed out and wreaked havoc in the Haoran Sword Palace. He said in a gloomy tone, "My Taihao Mountain and the Heavenly Palace are the same age. I, Xu Shou, have been in the Center God Province longer than the East King! For countless years, no one has dared to search my Taihao Mountain! "
A powerful aura pressed down like a mountain, almost suffocating. Even He Chi's expression changed several times.

Chapter 2866
Someone came.
In the depths of the dark palace, the cover above was opened once again. Faint light penetrated down from above.

Below, Luo Zheng sat cross-legged, turning a deaf ear to the voice.

Seeing Luo Zheng's fearless appearance, Empress Xuan felt exceptionally vexed in her heart.
Qiu Yin He and He Chi had reached the Great Hao Mountain so quickly. Time was tight.
The Illusionist was originally undergoing an important cultivation in the Paramita Realm. She had forcefully awakened the Illusionist, forcing him to leave the Paramita Realm.

This person wore a white robe. That robe had many human eyes embroidered on it. These human eyes were so lifelike that they seemed to be embedded on the robe.
"Rest assured, Empress Xuan. He is merely a little imp from the Perverted Realm." The Illusionist chuckled.
Empress Xuan originally wanted to remind the other party of what happened to the Immortal Ghost. But thinking of the Illusionist's cultivation and strength, she felt that she was looking down on him.
When Luo Zheng saw the Illusionist, he revealed a look of vigilance.
The Immortal Ghost was an outstanding existence amongst the Chaotic Origin Realm. In the eyes of Luo Zheng, it was extremely powerful. But this Illusionist in front of him was someone that Luo Zheng could not see through. This Illusionist's cultivation should have reached the level of Qiu Yin He!

At the moment when Luo Zheng saw those eyes, he felt his mind falling into a trance.
'Not good! Don't look them in the eye! "
Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat, and he quickly closed his eyes.
After he closed his eyes, everything else disappeared. However, those eyes were still there, and they were getting closer and closer!
"It's useless, Luo Zheng. This is the Eye of Illusion. You can't escape by closing your eyes," 9527 reminded him.
"Then what should we do?" Luo Zheng asked anxiously.
Luo Zheng's ability to control souls was indeed limited …

Chaotic Origin Great World

Luo Zheng cursed in his heart. It would be strange if you cared about your son's life or death. You're clearly doing this for the True Comprehension Chapter!
With that said, the logic was clear.
As a mother, she had just brought Luo Zheng to Mount Taihao out of anger. The favor and reason behind this were all justifiable.
Whether it was Qiu Yinhe, Concubine Yan, or Elder Ying, it was difficult for them to make a fuss about it.
"An eye for an eye. Since Xu Youwei wanted to kill Luo Zheng, he deserved to be killed by Luo Zheng instead. It's normal for Concubine Xuan to make trouble for Luo Zheng for her son. Since Luo Zheng is fine, let's forget about today's matter," said Concubine Yan.
Her appearance now was the final word.
These people were all extremely intelligent people. She knew that she wouldn't be able to get an explanation for Luo Zheng.
If Concubine Xuan continued to act pitiful, there was a chance that she would make a false accusation. It was better to end things here.
Xu Shou clasped his hands behind his back and let out a long sigh. "It's because I didn't teach my son well that he caused such a disaster. Today, I actually had to ask Concubine Yan and Elder Ying to come out. I've troubled everyone."
Elder Ying stretched out his hand on the golden dragon throne. Ling Shuang hurriedly helped him up and walked all the way to Luo Zheng's side.
Elder Ying had heard Ling Shuang mention Luo Zheng as early as in the Realm of the Gods, but this was the first time he had seen Luo Zheng.
His dim eyes looked up and down at Luo Zheng. His wrinkled face smoothed out, and he said, "When I first heard of you, you had just entered the Sea of True Meaning. Your cultivation speed is pretty good. Your Sun Soul is about to reach Indigo Soul, right?"
Luo Zheng cupped his hands towards Elder Ying. "It's still lacking a lot of maturity."
If the Sun Soul continued to cultivate, the soul would gradually turn from brown to indigo. The common name of the Human Race was Sun Soul Entering Indigo, which was Indigo Soul.
After the Seven Mountains Meeting, Luo Zheng once again went to the temple to receive a large number of soul crystals. His Sun Soul also faintly emitted a purple light.
Recently, Luo Zheng rarely went to the Nirvana Realm, nor did he step into the Upper Heaven. He still didn't know where his limit was, but there was still some distance to the true Sun Soul Entering Indigo.
"I've prepared a soul crystal for Shuang 'er to help her step into the Desire Realm. It seems that I'll have to prepare another one for you," Elder Ying said again.
Luo Zheng was slightly stunned. He didn't know what Elder Ying meant.
However, if one soul crystal could help Ling Shuang step into the Desire Realm, the number of soul crystals was probably not small. He couldn't accept such a big gift.
Luo Zheng cupped his hands and said: "Luo Zheng appreciates Elder Ying's good intentions. But this junior isn't in a hurry to cultivate."
Elder Ying shook his head and chuckled: "The Chaotic Origin Great World is about to reopen. You shouldn't miss such an opportunity. I'll send you and Shuang 'er over with these two tickets. That way, I'll be at ease. You can't reject it."
Ling Shuang added from the side: "Luo Zheng, the Chaotic Origin Great World will reopen once every ten years. It's fine if you miss it. But you have to wait for another ten years. Don't reject Grandma's good intentions!"
Luo Zheng roughly knew that he had to break through to the Chaotic Origin Realm after entering the Desire Realm.
That was a complete strengthening of his fleshy body. Only through this strengthening would he be able to withstand a stronger Paramita Energy.
But he knew nothing about how to do it.
If it was like what Ling Shuang said, that the Chaotic Origin Great World would only reopen once every ten years, he shouldn't miss such an opportunity.
At this moment, Concubine Yan flew over. She lightly landed on the other side of Luo Zheng. At the same time, she chuckled: "What a coincidence. My family's Feng Ge is also planning to head to the Chaotic Origin Great World. I still have quite a few tickets with me. If Luo Zheng wants them, I can give you eight or ten tickets … Also, my family's Feng Ge doesn't need you to take care of her. She might become your assistant."
Eight or ten tickets …
When Qiu Yinhe and the others heard the tone of Concubine Yan, they were dumbfounded.
The tickets mentioned by Concubine Yan and Elder Ying were not ordinary items.
The Chaotic Origin Great World was a supplement to the Great World Cultivation Technique.
Initially, when the sentient beings of the Mother World entered the Paramita Realm, they would gradually unearth many powerful Paramita Talismans. Every sentient being could control an explosive amount of energy.
But when their energy reached a limit, which was around 1,000 Divine Jun, they would discover the limit of their fleshy body.
Other than a few powerful alien races that could barely surpass this limit, the vast majority of sentient beings from other races were trapped at this limit.
Until the Middle Ancient Era, there were no sentient beings who could break through this limit.
Some experts had stepped into the higher dimension of the Paramita Realm and fused with the Paramita Talisman, allowing them to wield a Paramita Energy of thousands or even tens of thousands of Divine Jun. But the moment they fought, the Paramita Energy they could use was still limited to 1,000 Divine Jun.
Hence, the Paramita Talisman of the Descent Type was the first choice. Because the Paramita Talisman of the Descent Type did not require the fleshy body to bear the energy. The Paramita Energy it could unleash was far beyond the limit of the user's body.
But the Strengthening Type, Auxiliary Type, and Mysterious Type Paramita Talismans were extremely outstanding. It was impossible to ignore these Paramita Talismans.

Chapter 2871
Origin of Chaotic Origin
This technique Nuwa discovered was none other than the Chaotic Origin Recreation.
After the Rage God Body Refining Technique failed, the Body Refining Technique gradually declined in the Mother World.
Some of the barbarians were fond of cultivating their bodies. However, the power they could unleash was ultimately prioritized and could not compete with the power of the Nirvana Realm.
Whether it was Nuwa or the other superpowers in the Mother World, all of them had been vexed over how to strengthen their bodies for a long time.
At that time, Nuwa had already stepped into the 22nd Heaven. She discovered a strange Paramita Talisman. This Paramita Talisman was known as the Chaotic Origin Spirit.
The Chaotic Origin Spirit was an auxiliary Paramita Talisman.
After summoning it, it could fuse with one's physical body and greatly enhance one's physical strength.
The Chaotic Origin Spirit was an ordinary Nirvana Token within the 22 Levels of Heaven. It did not provide a significant boost to the fleshy body. The Nirvana Energy it contained was also ordinary. Hence, these Nirvana Tokens were not valued by the 22 Levels of Heaven.
But coincidentally, Nuwa discovered a unique characteristic of the Chaotic Origin Spirit.
This Spirit of Paramita was actually a living creature!
Although living creatures in the Nirvana Realm were rare, they were nothing to Nuwa.
But the nature of the Chaotic Origin Spirit was different. Even though it was an auxiliary Nirvana Token, it had the characteristics of a Descent Nirvana Token!
After discovering this characteristic, Nuwa made her first attempt.
She got Fuxi to grab a Chaotic Origin Spirit from the Paramita. After that, the two of them summoned their Chaotic Origin Spirits out at the same time.
Descending Paramita Talismans could appear in the Mother World for a long period of time. These two Chaotic Origin Spirits were able to recognize each other as the same species. They could communicate with each other using an unseen language. They could depend on each other and even reproduce!
After reaching the stage of reproduction, Nuwa discovered an incredible phenomenon.
The Chaotic Origin Spirits that were produced did not return to the Paramita. Instead, they remained in the Mother World.
What was even more interesting was that these Chaotic Origin Spirits were ownerless!
Neither Fuxi nor Nuwa could determine what exactly the Chaotic Origin Spirit was. Was it a lifeform from the Mother World? Or the token in the Nirvana Realm? Both of them were, but none of them were …
A single Chaotic Origin Spirit could strengthen a portion of his fleshy body. What if there were many Chaotic Origin Spirits?
When Nuwa thought of this, she was also very excited.
Through the initial two Chaotic Origin Spirits, the Chaotic Origin Spirits gathered in Nüwa's hands continued to multiply, increasing the number of Chaotic Origin Spirits gathered in the hands of Nüwa.
When the number of Chaotic Origin Spirits exceeded a hundred, Nuwa allowed Fuxi to absorb them all.
These reproduced Chaotic Origin Spirits were all absorbed into Fuxi's body, directly raising the fleshy body of Fuxi by a level. Hence, Fuxi was considered the first person to undergo Chaotic Origin Reconstruction.
The news of Li Shan nurturing a Chaotic Origin Spirit spread like wildfire.
In the past, the Chaotic Origin Spirit was an ordinary Paramita Token. Yet now, it had suddenly become extremely popular in the Paramita Realm.
Various superpowers had begun fighting over the Chaotic Origin Spirits. For a period of time, the Chaotic Origin Spirits within the 22 levels of Heaven were nearly extinct.
Similar to what happened to Nuwa, the experts from other super organizations had also nurtured the descendants of the Chaotic Origin Spirit. These strange lifeforms had become a way for them to strengthen themselves.
The main road!
The appearance of the Chaotic Origin Spirit signified that those strengthening-type and other heaven-defying mysterious Nirvana Talismans had become more practical. At least, they could surpass the limits of strengthening the fleshy body.
But the superpowers did not rejoice for too long before they discovered that this road was no longer feasible.
In the past, Nvywa's reproduction of the Chaotic Origin Spirits was extremely smooth. In just a short year, she managed to nurture several thousand Chaotic Origin Spirits. When the super powers started nurturing the Chaotic Origin Spirits, they discovered that the reproduction rate of the Chaotic Origin Spirits had greatly decreased.
Some superpowers had spent several years nurturing them and only managed to nurture a hundred Chaotic Origin Spirits. There were even some who did not manage to nurture any more Chaotic Origin Spirits.
After that, those superpowers investigated the reason in the Deity Temple and finally came to a conclusion.
There was a limit to the number of Chaotic Origin Spirits. Their limit was 200,000 Chaotic Origin Spirits.
Once the number of Chaotic Origin Spirits reached 200,000, they could no longer nurture another one.
Only when one of them was refined would it be possible for the next one to be born!
As Nuwa was the first to discover the Spirits of Chaotic Origin, she had already nurtured more than 100,000 Spirits of Chaotic Origin by the time the other superpowers started taking action. As for the other superpowers, most of them had only nurtured a few hundred Spirits of Chaotic Origin. They were truly pitiful.
Furthermore, after the living beings from those superpowers refined the Chaotic Origin Spirits, there was a high probability that new Chaotic Origin Spirits would be born in Li Mountain. After all, Li Mountain had the largest number of Chaotic Origin Spirits …
After a period of time, the number of Chaotic Origin Spirits in Li Mountain had reached 150,000. The number of Chaotic Origin Spirits from other races was even lesser.
Many superpowers were envious. They worked together to force Li Mountain to hand over the Chaotic Origin Spirits.
Under the immense pressure, Nuwa chose to compromise. She was forced to construct the Chaotic Origin Great World. She worked together with all the superpowers in the Mother World and placed all the Chaotic Origin Spirits into the Chaotic Origin Great World!
According to their deduction, the number of Chaotic Origin Spirits would reach 200,000 in about ten years. By then, all the races could come and harvest them. As for how much they could harvest and how much they could reconstruct their Chaotic Origin, it would depend on their own fortune.
But the Chaotic Origin Great World was not a place that anyone could casually enter. The entrance tickets there were scarce even in the Mother World.
A single entrance ticket represented a Chaotic Origin Expert. Many geniuses in the Seven Mountains of the Great Yi Heaven Palace were eyeing these entrance tickets. It was simply too exaggerated for Concubine Yan to give out eight to ten entrance tickets.
Old Ying stabbed his thin walking stick into the ground. He snorted: "What's the point of having so many entrance tickets?"
Concubine Yan did not argue with Old Ying. Instead, she chuckled at Luo Zheng: "The Heart Flow Sword Sect is very close to our Tai Shi Palace. I've already given you the command token. You must come and visit often."
Saying that, Concubine Yan drifted away …
Old Ying spat at the back of Concubine Yan. He then slowly walked towards the entrance of the Hao Ran Sword Hall.
Ling Shuang played with Old Ying. She looked at Luo Zheng. She had been holding back for a long time before asking, "Luo Zheng, why did you visit the Tai Shi Palace?"
Concubine Yan's attitude was too abnormal. Ling Shuang was guessing what had happened between Luo Zheng and Feng Ge.
"Previously during the Seven Mountains Little Meeting, I've offended Feng Ge." Luo Zheng said.
Ling Shuang nodded, "Mn. At that time, I even reminded you that Feng Ge was quite angry at that time. You must be on guard against her revenge."
Luo Zheng shrugged and said: "She did take revenge. She kidnapped my people into the Tai Shi Palace. I went to the Tai Shi Palace and fought with her."
Autumn Yin River, He Chi. Lin Zhanting and Old Hai were following behind Old Ying. These few famous experts had a gossipy heart. When they heard Luo Zheng's words, they felt strange.
Why would Concubine Yan fancy him after a fight? Was there such a good thing in the world?

Chapter 2872
Backing Mountain
When Elder Ying and Concubine Yan were fighting, Luo Zheng was not in the Hao Ran Sword Hall.
Hence, Elder Ying's attitude towards Concubine Yan left Luo Zheng baffled. He only thought that the two of them had some grudges.
As for the entrance ticket to the Chaotic Origin Great World, Luo Zheng did not realize its value. Giving away eight or ten tickets did not sound amazing. Hence, why did Elder Ying and Concubine Yan fight so fervently? Luo Zheng was still in a state of confusion.
"What attitude?" Luo Zheng asked.
Ling Shuang revealed a playful expression, "She wants to recruit you as her son-in-law!"
"Ah?"
Luo Zheng opened his mouth wide.
He and Concubine Yan had only met once before. Even though Concubine Yan was more enthusiastic, what was up with calling him a son-in-law?
"Are you moved?" Ling Shuang deliberately asked.
No matter how stupid Luo Zheng was, he understood the meaning of Ling Shuang's words. He helplessly smiled: "That Feng Ge and I are not familiar with each other. If Concubine Yan said that, she might just be joking."
Was it really just a joke?
Ling Shuang's big eyes stared at Luo Zheng. Disbelief was written all over her face.
She was ultimately still a young girl. Her skin was thin. If she continued asking, others would only think that she was jealous. At this moment, she fell silent.
Everyone left the Hao Ran Sword Hall. Qiu Yinhe unfolded a huge sword. Everyone stepped on it and flew out of the Great Sky Mountain.
Within the Hao Ran Sword Hall.
Xu Shou returned to his golden dragon throne.
Concubine Xuan clapped her hands, causing a fine green light screen to spread out in the entire hall.
After doing all this, Xu Shou's humble and courteous appearance suddenly changed. His eyes that were brimming with sharpness turned ferocious, just like an enraged wild beast.
"How many words did you force out!" Xu Shou almost roared.
Concubine Xuan and the two Dark Tribe leaders revealed a look of fear. Today, being bullied by the Great Yi Mountain and Greater One Mountain was indeed a great humiliation to Xu Shou.
"More than a hundred words …" Concubine Xuan pursed her lips.
"Ka!"
"Ah …"
An invisible hand grabbed Consort Xuan's neck. Consort Xuan was lifted in front of Xu Shou. She struggled like a little lamb, but she couldn't make a single sound.
"After working so hard, I only managed to obtain a little over a hundred words. I was almost embarrassed today," Xu Shou said angrily.
"Sovereign Shou, please calm down!"
"Sovereign Shou, you can't blame Lady Xuan for what happened today!"
The two leaders of the Dark Group pleaded.
"Plop!"
Xu Shou threw Lady Xuan to the side, the anger in his eyes still not extinguished. "The most troublesome thing is … if we interrogate him about the continuation of the Eternal Sword Intent, that brat will definitely tell Qiu Yinhe!"
"So what if I tell you? Our Taihao Mountain is now upright and upright. We don't have any weaknesses. What can they do to us? "Lady Xuan said with an aggrieved expression as she lay on the ground.
Xu Shou glared at Lady Xuan and said, "You underestimate them too much. As long as there are any clues, they'll be able to find some clues!"
"Sovereign Shou worries too much. According to the current situation of the Heavenly Palace, it doesn't matter if they find something. At most, we'll be able to sit back and relax in two or three years. Besides … there's more than one sect on our side!" One of the leaders of the Dark Group said.
Xu Shou shook his head and finally let out a long breath. The murderous light in his eyes gradually grew stronger. "I hope so!"
… …
… …
On the giant sword.
Luo Zheng recounted what happened at Taihao Mountain.
Qiu Yinhe, He Chi, and the others also had uncertain expressions.
"They actually care so much about the True Comprehension Chapter. No matter how you look at it, it's a little abnormal," Old Hai said from the side of the giant sword.
"When Luo Zheng decodes the complete True Comprehension Chapter, it will spread throughout the Heavenly Palace sooner or later. Will Taihao Mountain miss out on a copy?" He Chi asked.
"I was also curious about this at the time." Luo Zheng smiled helplessly.
Everyone must have a motive for doing something, and Taihao Mountain was no exception.
"Unless they can't wait until then," Qiu Yinhe analyzed carefully.
"Why can't they wait until then? Taihao Mountain has been here for so long, it can't be that they moved away?" Lin Zhanting said in surprise.
When he said this, Qiu Yinhe, He Chi, and Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly.
Old Ying snorted coldly. "It seems that some people seem to be harboring disloyalty!"
Unless Taihao Mountain wanted to break away from Taiyi Heavenly Palace, it was impossible for them to be so anxious to get the True Comprehension Chapter.
One-sided speculation naturally couldn't confirm Taihao Mountain's motive, but this matter was worth paying attention to. At least it could be prevented before it happened.
"I will report this matter to the Eastern Sovereign," Old Hai said.
Qiu Yinhe controlled the giant sword to fly all the way and soon arrived at Taiyi Mountain.
Before Luo Zheng left the giant sword, Elder Ying warned him again. She wasn't joking when she said that she had prepared a Soul Crystal for Luo Zheng. She asked Luo Zheng to prepare for it and go to the other side to collect it.
Old Ying was considered the number two figure in the Heavenly Palace. If Luo Zheng were to refuse, it would be too disrespectful. Hence, he could only nod obediently.
Furthermore, the Chaotic Origin Great World was about to open. Luo Zheng would definitely not miss this opportunity.
