…
Heaven Domain Prefecture.
The black ship floated silently in the middle of it.
Shen Tu sat on the ship's railing. Her slender legs were swinging in the air.
A gentle mountain range appeared in her field of vision, and there were many buildings scattered all over the mountain range.
These buildings were of different sizes. The largest building was thousands of meters tall, and the smallest building was as small as a palm. The disparity was huge. Naturally, it was meant for living beings of different clans to live in.
Most of the clans in the Mother World occupied their own territory. It was rare for them to live together like this.
"We have arrived at the Black Mountain Sect …"
Shen Tu pressed one hand on the ship's railing and gently pulled with her other hand.
"Sou!"
A person was directly pulled out from the black ship. He was heavily smashed onto the ship's railing, emitting a muffled "peng" sound.
Evil Deities crawled up from the deck with an unhappy expression, "Your father was still cultivating when you forcefully dragged me over!"
"There is a place more suitable for you to cultivate here." Shen Tu pointed downwards.
Evil Deities extended her head to look downwards. That mountain range was emitting a dense Evil Energy. Clearly, it was not a good place.
"That is the Black Mountain Sect. It is a huge sect made up of more than 800 clans. The competition within is very cruel. Furthermore, you do not have a backer to help you. Even if it is your own clan – the Human Race – you have to be wary of them." Shen Tu indifferently said.
"Backer?" Evil Deities laughed with disdain, "I don't need a backer. I am my own backer!"
Shen Tu was already used to Evil Deities' arrogance. This person's talent had exceeded her expectations time and time again. But seeing a fellow who had just entered the Nirvana Realm being so arrogant, she still felt slightly displeased.
Shen Tu curled her lips and said, "Well said. Then you can leave!"
"Peng!"

Chapter 2873
Black Mountain Sect
The gravitational force exerted on Evil Deities was extremely powerful, and Evil Deities had no way of resisting.
Falling at such a high speed and crashing into the ground, he probably wouldn't be able to withstand the force.
Seeing that he was only a few dozen meters above the ground, Evil Deities revealed a ruthless expression.
A brilliant burst of energy erupted!
"Boom!"
As if the sun had exploded, a huge crater appeared in the ground, and Evil Deities was lying in it.
"Damn woman …"
Evil Deities raised his head and cursed.
"Dong!"
Just as he cursed, his head was smashed by a heavy object, and he almost fainted.
When Evil Deities came back to his senses, he saw that there was a black token at his feet. He picked up the token and examined it, but aside from a strange pattern, there was no other information on it.
Evil Deities put away the token and cursed Shen Tu a few more times before climbing out of the crater.
Just as he climbed out of the crater, he heard rustling sounds not far away. Two black wolves as tall as a person were lurking not far away.
The eyes of these two black wolves were glowing, and they clearly saw Evil Deities as their prey.
"You want to eat me?"
Evil Deities sneered and walked towards the Black Mountain Sect.
Just as Evil Deities left his back to the two black wolves, the two black wolves suddenly pounced towards Luo Zheng.
"Weng, weng!"
Two balls of light erupted from Evil Deities' back, and the two black wolves were annihilated by the Dao Fusion energy.
"Beasts will always be beasts …"
Evil Deities sneered as he looked at the black wolves' limbs scattered all over the ground.
Just as he turned his head, his brows furrowed.
In front of him was a low mountain stream, and strange creatures appeared around the stream. Some were shaped like leopards, and they were tall and sturdy. Some were as thin as mice, but they contained great power. Most of the creatures were so strange that they were almost indescribable.
"Shen Tu has thrown me into a beast's den …" Evil Deities grinned, the fighting spirit in his heart blazing.
At this moment, the leading creature suddenly spoke in human language, "Hand over the Black Mountain Order in your hands! Human! "
"The Black Mountain Order is not something you can possess. Hand it over and we can leave your corpse intact!"
"Haha …"
For a moment, all kinds of strange sounds could be heard.
Actually, the two Black Wolves' goal in the beginning was not to eat Evil Deities. They had sensed the aura of the Black Mountain Token.
The Black Mountain Sect was a famous sect in this area, and no matter what kind of demon or ghost it was, it could be absorbed into it.
For those demons who had no backing and could not rely on their clans, the Black Mountain Sect was an excellent place to go.
However, the Blackmountain Sect didn't accept just anyone. Just this Blackmountain Medallion alone was very difficult for ordinary foreign races to obtain. Aside from killing and devouring each other, the Blackmountain Sect's goal was to obtain a Blackmountain Medallion.
Facing the aggressive non-humankind beings, the arrogant look on the evil god's face finally faded quite a bit.
Although Shen Tu did not give any hints, the Black Mountain Medallion was definitely a medallion to enter the Black Mountain Sect.
Seeing that the strange foreign races were about to make a move, a rhombus-shaped halo suddenly appeared under Evil Deities' feet.
Over the past few days, Evil Deities had received some guidance, and his use of Dao Fusion energy had improved by another level.
"Weng …"
As the light continued to accumulate, the foreign races also began to charge towards him.
"Tear him to shreds!"
"The Black Mountain Medallion is mine!"
"…"
"Boom!"
In the instant that the foreign races rushed over, the halo in the air exploded layer by layer.
Scorching shockwaves spread out in all directions, as if they could annihilate everything.
Borrowing the force of the shockwaves, Evil Deities shot forward like an arrow.
Some of the foreign races were devoured by the Dao Fusion energy, while the rest chased after Evil Deities!
The foreign races entrenched in the Black Mountain Sect grew up in slaughter and slaughter from the moment they were born. Death was a common occurrence for them. Even if Evil Deities was powerful, he could not intimidate them.
"Squeak squeak …"
Among the many pursuers, an extremely agile gray mouse jumped onto Evil Deities' back.
Evil Deities immediately crushed it, but the gray mouse still tore a piece of flesh from his shoulder …
"Whoosh!"
A foreign beast as burly as an elephant whipped Evil Deities' back with a long whip covered in thorns, immediately tearing his clothes and leaving a bloody mark on his back.
"If I really get surrounded by these monsters, I'm afraid I won't even have a complete corpse. Damn Shen Tu!" Evil Deities cursed in his heart.
On the Black Ship, Evil Deities had indeed broadened his horizons.
Even though Shen Tu was arrogant, she had helped Evil Deities reconstruct his worldview.
She told Evil Deities that it was meaningless to fuse the Dao Fusion energy. She even told him about another method to fuse the Dao Fusion energy. She warned him that many had tried this path and failed.
Evil Deities did not accept this suggestion. He felt that it was only a matter of time before these energies fused together.
But he still chose to listen to Shen Tu's suggestion. After all, this woman had promised him that she would help him unseal the Soul Lodging Land after he stepped into the Chaotic Origin Realm!
But to reach the Chaotic Origin Realm, he had to head to the Chaotic Origin Great World …
It was getting closer to the opening of the Chaotic Origin Great World. Before that, he had to raise his cultivation level.
This was the reason why Shen Tu wanted to send Evil Deities into the Black Mountain Sect.
This sect that was a mix of several hundred races was a typical chaotic sect. It was filled with killing and conspiracies. This was both a cultivation ground and a testing ground.
"Xiu!"
After Evil Deities rushed out a few dozen kilometers in a single breath, he tunneled into two mountain streams.
At the moment he stepped into the mountain stream, the other races behind him stopped in their tracks. Be it the gigantic beasts or the fist-sized insects, they all stopped chasing after him.
"What should we do? He has already entered the sect!"
"Without the Black Mountain Token, those who enter without permission will definitely die …"
"Let's go, let's go. Consider this human brat lucky. I reckon he won't survive more than half a day in the Black Mountain Sect!"
Seeing those other races gradually dispersing, Evil Deities revealed a ruthless look on his face.
It had always been him who ran over others. Why would these cats and dogs dare to chase after him?
Wait until he had some accomplishments in the Black Mountain Sect. After he left the sect, he would definitely wipe out all these demons and monsters!
As he thought of this, a strand of cold air suddenly invaded his neck. At the same time, a miserable voice sounded, "Human, do you have the Black Mountain Token …"
Behind him, there were many tattered rags floating in the air. Under the rags was a withered skeleton. It was unknown what kind of lifeform this skeleton belonged to. Behind the withered skeleton, there was even an enormous wing bone folded behind it.
The eyes of Evil Deities shrunk when he saw this withered skeleton. He handed over that Black Mountain Token.
"Those who can walk out alive after entering my Black Mountain Sect are all powerful characters. But I hope you can live until that day. Ge ge ge … follow me!"

Chapter 2874
Translator: Henyee Translations Editor: Henyee Translations
Mo Yijian's Return
When they returned to the Heart Flow Sword Sect's courtyard, Xi Youqin was the first to rush out and ask all sorts of questions.
The last time Luo Zheng had gone to Ming Hu, he had also left without saying goodbye.
Most importantly, Xiaoyin had returned, but Luo Zheng had not. Xi Youqin, Ning Yudie, and the others were also quite worried when they heard this.
They only relaxed when they saw that Luo Zheng was safe and sound.
Luo Zheng only told them that he had gone to Ming Hu to cultivate. As for the matter of Taizhi Palace and Taihao Mountain, he naturally did not mention it.
Xiaoyin knew that Luo Zheng was hiding some of the truth, but she did not point it out. As a servant girl, it was not her place to ask how her master would deal with these matters.
After staying in the courtyard for half a day, someone knocked on the courtyard door again.
Xiaoyin pushed open the door and saw Mo Yijian's excited face.
"I heard that your Young Master Luo has returned?"
Mo Yijian was becoming more and more polite to Luo Zheng. At first, he had not even been interested in Luo Zheng's life.
"My Young Master has just returned …"
Xiaoyin was puzzled. Had Mo Yijian hired someone to keep an eye on their courtyard?
Xiaoyin had guessed correctly.
After Mo Yijian had obtained a portion of the True Comprehension Chapter, he had devoted himself to studying it almost nonstop and had discovered a huge mystery.
A few days ago, Mo Yijian had come to find Luo Zheng, but when he learned that Luo Zheng was not here, he could only return.
For the past two days, he had sent someone to keep an eye on Luo Zheng's courtyard. When he saw that Luo Zheng had returned, he immediately came to see him.
"I have something to discuss with him," Mo Yijian said with a smile.
Xiaoyin couldn't refuse, so she nodded and went to invite Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng looked at Mo Yijian's excited face and felt a little strange. Could it be that this kid wanted to ask for the True Comprehension Chapter again?
"Brother Luo Zheng has been in the Heart Flow Sword Sect for quite some time. I wonder to what extent has Brother Luo Zheng cultivated the Sword Intent Mantra?" Mo Yijian suddenly asked.
This question was beyond Luo Zheng's expectation.
He had indeed been in the Heart-Flow Sword Sect for some time, but it had not been long since he obtained the Heart-Flow Sword Mantra. Furthermore, he had been busy with the Seven Mountains Meeting and had gone to Ming Lake. Although he had taken some time to comprehend the Heart-Flow Sword Mantra, he had not gained much.
"Just a little comprehension," Luo Zheng answered truthfully. "Why do you ask, Junior Brother Yijian?"
Mo Yijian's excitement grew. "Then Brother Luo Zheng, you have to hurry. The first 150 words of your True Comprehension Chapter are a perfect match for the Heart-Flow Sword Mantra, especially the Killing Heart Mantra. It is extremely mysterious when used together with the Yang Soul …"
At this point, Mo Yijian looked around. Perhaps he could not find a suitable target, so he directly chose Luo Zheng as his target.
"What are you doing?" Luo Zheng immediately became vigilant.
Unexpectedly, Mo Yijian's eyes flashed slightly and he began to gather the Sword Intent of Eternity. He gently raised his hand and wisps of invisible aura spiraled around him. At this moment, Mo Yijian seemed to have changed into a different person.
In Luo Zheng's eyes, Mo Yijian had become a sharp giant sword that was about to stab him.
"Killing Heart Mantra?"
Luo Zheng's complexion changed greatly.
The killing intent was too strong. Luo Zheng had to be on guard.
"Whoosh!"
Luo Zheng quickly retreated, his feet leaving deep marks on the ground. At the same time, he was preparing to counterattack.

Seeing him apologize so seriously, Luo Zheng was both angry and amused. This guy was really a sword maniac.

The Sword of Mind in the True Comprehension Chapter was similar to the Killing Heart Mantra.
Luo Zheng had been too busy recently, so he didn't have time to study it. After Mo Yijian got the True Comprehension Chapter, he cultivated tirelessly. With his talent, he easily mixed the two together.
"Yes!" The apology on Mo Yijian's face was swept away. He said with an expression that said, "It's very powerful, right?"

"Of course!" Mo Yijian laughed. "These days, I've been killing in all directions in the Nirvana Realm. The people of the You Xiong Clan are afraid of me! Hahaha! "
In the Four Heavens of the Nirvana Realm, the three temples of the Human Tribe were built together.
Most of the strong people at this level had just stepped into the Nirvana Realm not long ago. They were relatively young. Of course, they would provoke each other and often compete on the soul level.
In the beginning, the Four Great Temples kept each other in check and got along peacefully.
Following the destruction of the Li Tribe's temple, the temples of You Xiong and Shennong rose day by day. The temple of the Taiyi Heavenly Palace fell into a disadvantage. The disciples of the Heavenly Palace were often bullied outside the temple. This time, with the help of the True Comprehension Chapter, Mo Yijian's strength improved greatly. He was in the limelight.
"I see …" Luo Zheng nodded slightly.
In the Nirvana Realm, it was indeed a competition on the soul level. If the Heart Sword could be combined with the first 150 words of the True Comprehension Chapter, it would be a great help!
Mo Yijian took Luo Zheng's 150 words and was grateful.
With his character of not asking for help, he had to repay this kindness. This time, he explained his understanding of the Heart Sword, the key points, and even the ingenious method of combining it with the True Comprehension Chapter.
Luo Zheng could also discover these things by himself, but Mo Yijian's unreserved guidance could greatly shorten this process.
Luo Zheng had already read through the Heart Sword Code, but Mo Yijian had pointed out some parts that he didn't understand. He was itching and couldn't wait to verify this process.
After sending off Mo Yijian, Luo Zheng asked Xiaoyin to help him apply to enter the Heart Flow Pagoda.
For ordinary disciples of the Heart Sword Sect, they could only enter the Heart Flow Pagoda once a month for two hours.
But for elites like Luo Zheng, there was no limit to the number of times they could enter. Of course, there was still a time limit.
Most of the elites wouldn't stay in the Heart Flow Pagoda for more than four hours. If they exceeded this time, they would be burned by the Heart Fire.
In the entire Heart Sword Sect, only Luo Zheng and Mo Yijian could stay in the Heart Flow Pagoda for a long time. It wasn't that they could comprehend the Tao with a single thought, but that they both cultivated the crucial Emotion Severing Divine Path.
This time, Luo Zheng stayed in the Heart Flow Pagoda for a full day and night. He comprehended the Heart Flow Sword Code in the Heart Flow Pagoda, so it was naturally very suitable.

Yang Soul in Indigo

"Is a Soul Battle worth so many Soul Soul Crystals?" Ling Shuang asked.

Objective

Tearing Everything Down

Most divine temples were equipped with soul-cleansing light beams. Even indigo souls would be turned into ashes within a moment if they were swept across by these soul-cleansing light beams. The conflicts that happened in the fourth layer of the sky were nothing but normal conflicts. Even if both sides were extremely angry, they would restrain themselves and only take revenge to a certain extent. If two large families started an all-out war, too many things would be involved. Killing the Heaven Palace people out of anger could be talked about in You Xiong Family, but it would be a joke to scare people.

The divine temple in the fourth layer of the sky was the first stop for You Xiong Family people after they arrived at the Nirvana Realm. Give in
Just as the You Xiong Clan's Five Elemental Envoys were still worried, Luo Zheng had already arrived at the central hall.
There was a wall on one side of the central hall. On this wall, there was a huge bear statue. The statue was extremely exquisite.
Luo Zheng looked at it for a while, then walked over and pressed his hand against the wall.
Just as Luo Zheng was about to destroy the wall, a person behind him said, "You can't destroy this wall …"
The person who spoke was an indigo soul level man. He suddenly appeared in the temple. He should be a member of the You Xiong Clan.
"What right do you have to stop me?" Luo Zheng turned his head and sneered.
"I just can't!"
As that person spoke, he pounced towards Luo Zheng.
Why would Luo Zheng care about a mere indigo soul? With a wave of his hand, that person flew to one side and smashed into the wall on the other side.
Luo Zheng had held back in this attack. Otherwise, it would have directly shaken his Yang Soul into ashes.
Following which, Luo Zheng pressed his right hand against the wall. Fly!
"Ka!"
The entire wall was shattered.
The surface of the wall cracked, revealing rows of hidden compartments.
There were a total of 13 rows of hidden compartments. In each of these compartments, there was a nirvana token!
In particular, the three Nirvana Realm Tokens on the thirteenth row were extremely eye-catching. The first one was a golden calf sculpture, the second one was a dark green diamond-shaped gem, and the third one was a pair of curved monster feet.
From left to right, Luo Zheng counted in his heart. From the 1st Heaven to the 13th Heaven, there were more than 100 nirvana tokens.
"You can't touch those nirvana tokens! They belong to our You Xiong Clan! "Two more people appeared behind Luo Zheng and shouted.
Like the Heavenly Palace, the You Xiong Clan would also collect a large number of nirvana tokens and distribute them to their clansmen. These nirvana tokens were stored in these walls. The You Xiong Clan didn't expect Luo Zheng to barge into the temple. They didn't have time to take these nirvana tokens away.
"It's all mine now," Luo Zheng said with a chuckle.
These Nirvana tokens must be precious. Luo Zheng might not need them, but he could trade them.
However, in his physical state, he did not have a Sumeru Space. Storing these Nirvana tokens was a problem. This was also the reason why Luo Zheng called Ling Shuang along.
When the two of them saw that Luo Zheng really wanted to take away the Nirvana tokens, they became anxious and rushed towards Luo Zheng again without any regard for their own safety.
Luo Zheng was getting impatient. These guys would keep pestering him if he didn't teach them a lesson.
"Puff, puff!"
This time, Luo Zheng's attack was a bit heavier. Although he didn't kill anyone, the two's Yang Souls had suffered heavy injuries.
In the dark corners of the divine temple, many You Xiong Family people had been watching this. They were all dispirited.
They more or less knew that Luo Zheng was here for revenge. After all, their You Xiong Family had been the first to attack the Heavenly Palace. They had actually summoned such an evil star. They could only swallow the bitterness in their hearts.
For safety reasons, Luo Zheng still didn't call Ling Shuang in.
He clasped his hands into the bottom of the wall and exerted his strength. He actually dug the wall up from the ground and carried it on his shoulder. Step by step, he walked out of the divine temple.
"This guy …"
"Damn it, entering the Nirvana Realm with his physical body is too terrifying!"
"We can't fight against him at all!"
Luo Zheng walked to Ling Shuang step by step. Only then did he take the wall off his shoulder. He smiled and said, "Put it away!"
Ling Shuang smiled gently. Her Yang Soul danced around the wall. A blue square frame appeared on her slender fingers. Those Nirvana tokens were all put into the square frame.
"This is a big harvest!" Ling Shuang was in a good mood.
However, Luo Zheng shook his head and said, "There will be an even bigger harvest. Just you wait …"
At this moment, a person rushed over from the divine temple of the Heavenly Palace. At the same time, he shouted, "Wait, wait …"
Luo Zheng glanced at that person and asked, "What's the matter?"
That person approached Luo Zheng and said in a low voice, "Senior Qiu, he asked you to stop tearing it down."
Luo Zheng frowned.
Nearly half of the divine temple of the You Xiong Family had been torn down. He planned to tear down the remaining half as well. However, Qiu Yinhe actually wanted to stop him now … Either the You Xiong Family had pressured the Heavenly Palace, or they had promised some conditions.
"Then I won't tear it down," Luo Zheng smiled and said.
The person who delivered the message was stunned. He didn't expect Luo Zheng to really listen to Qiu Yinhe.
In the eyes of the disciples of the Heavenly Palace, this person who had entered the Nirvana Realm with his physical body was probably not someone the Heavenly Palace could control. Qiu Yinhe probably asked him to deliver the message just to test it out. He didn't expect him to trust Qiu Yinhe so much. What was the relationship between him and Qiu Yinhe?
"But I will definitely get what I need," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
These hundreds of Nirvana tokens were not important to Luo Zheng. In the future, he would find a way to exchange them for soul pills and soul crystals.
Logically speaking, there should be a lot of soul crystals in the divine temple of the You Xiong Family. He didn't care whether the divine temple was torn down or not, but he had to get the soul crystals!
At present, it seemed that the indigo soul was not the limit of the soul. In the future, when he crossed the color realm and stepped into the desire realm, he would definitely need more soul crystals!

Plunder.
Luo Zheng stepped into the temple again. He kept his promise and didn't destroy the temple.
The structure of the You Xiong Clan's temple wasn't complicated. Luo Zheng walked through the second half of the temple for a while. Their clansmen were still watching Luo Zheng's every move. Judging from the emotions emitted from their Sun Souls, they were very nervous.
The You Xiong Clan's clansmen saw Luo Zheng looking around, looking for something. Naturally, they knew what he was looking for.
The two most valuable things in the temple were the Nirvana token and the soul crystal.
Those Nirvana tokens were taken by Luo Zheng. Only the soul crystal was left!
Luo Zheng glanced in front of him. You Xiong Clan's clansmen were standing on the forked paths, but the path in front had the most people.
He smiled. The actions of these clansmen were telling Luo Zheng that the soul crystal was on that path!
When Luo Zheng walked closer, these Sun Souls were still blocking his path. They didn't have any intention of moving aside.
"You can't go in," said a clansman. "This is the most important place of our temple!"
Luo Zheng shook his head slightly. "Do you think you have the ability to stop me?"
He had already destroyed half of the temple. How could he reason with these people?
But these people still didn't move …
Luo Zheng stepped forward. With a light bump, he pushed this group of people away.
Someone tried to grab Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng swung his arm and threw these people away. He walked along this path and entered the deepest part of the temple.
"Sigh …"
"This time, we asked for it. We had to go against the Heavenly Palace and provoke such an evil star …"
"I haven't received my share of the soul crystal. Looks like there's no hope."
"…"
The You Xiong Clan's clansmen sighed.
How difficult was it for ordinary people to improve their soul realm in the Nirvana Realm?
Occasionally, they could find a little bit of soul crystal in the Nirvana Realm. It was like luck!
However, the clansmen and disciples of the Super Powers relied on the protection of their own Super Powers. They were able to obtain sufficient resources and progress by leaps and bounds. For example, Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang had reached the Indigo Soul Rank in a single step.
Now that the soul crystals here had been taken away, the hope of these clansmen being promoted had been cut off. Their hearts were filled with unwillingness, but there was nothing they could do.
In the innermost room, there was another thick wall. The wall was covered in small holes, and in front of the wall, there were several bear clansmen who were madly absorbing soul crystals using sumeru space.
These people were acting under orders. Before Luo Zheng broke in, they would take away as many people as they could.
Unfortunately, the design of the temple had a set of strict restrictions. The speed at which they absorbed the soul crystals was not considered fast. As these people continued to absorb, they only managed to absorb a small portion of the soul crystals.
"Get out of my way!"
Luo Zheng shouted coldly.
The clansmen were shocked. They were afraid that Luo Zheng would kill them, so they fled the Nirvana Realm.
Luo Zheng's method of retrieving the soul crystals was naturally much simpler and more crude. He once again pressed his hand on the wall, and with a sudden slap, the wall cracked open. Behind the wall was a huge rock. The rock was filled with densely packed small holes, like a beehive. The soul crystals were all placed in this beehive, and each of the small holes was emitting a bright white light.
He lifted the huge rock and walked out as if there was no one around.
When the clansmen saw the huge rock, their hearts were bleeding. One could imagine how they felt.
Walking all the way to the temple, Luo Zheng threw the beehive in front of Ling Shuang.
Seeing this huge rock, Ling Shuang's face was filled with shock. "You've brought out all of their trump cards!"
This rock was called the Ten Thousand Holed Rock. It was an excellent material for preserving soul crystals. When the temple was built, it would be sealed in thick walls. Some of the soul crystals that the disciples found in the Nirvana Realm would also be stored in it. The Heavenly Palace also stored soul crystals in the same way.
"Quickly put it into the Sumeru Space, so as to avoid any unexpected problems," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
Ling Shuang nodded and reached out to hold the rock. The soul crystals in the countless holes were like flowing water, gathering in the square frame in her hand.
Looking at the square frame in Ling Shuang's hand, Luo Zheng secretly thought that he should get another Sumeru Space.
His body was not like his soul, which could hold objects. Every time he obtained something, he had to rely on Ling Shuang to store it and transfer it to his Yang soul. There were many inconveniences in this process. Could the Heavenly Palace add a Sumeru Space to his body?
When Ling Shuang finished absorbing the soul crystals, Luo Zheng lifted the empty Ten Thousand Holed Rock and threw it back in front of the temple that had turned into a pile of ruins. Only then did the two of them leave.
When they arrived at the entrance of the Heavenly Palace Temple, Luo Zheng's body quietly left the other side …
The amount of soul crystals this time was really not small. Although the You Xiong Clan had already transferred a portion of them, the remaining amount was enough for eight people to step into the Indigo Soul Level.
When Luo Zheng's Yang soul returned to the temple, Ling Shuang and Luo Zheng went to the secret room together. She actually transferred all the soul crystals to Luo Zheng.
"Shouldn't we split it equally?" Luo Zheng asked.
"I don't want it." Ling Shuang shook her head resolutely.
These soul crystals were all obtained by Luo Zheng himself. She only helped to store them. Ling Shuang could not do such a thing.
Seeing Ling Shuang's resolute attitude, Luo Zheng did not force her. If he needed it in the future, he would distribute it again.
After Luo Zheng stepped into the Indigo Soul Level, he wanted to go to the higher levels of the Nirvana Realm. However, something like this had suddenly happened. He was afraid that this matter would be delayed.
"I originally wanted to go to the Thirteenth Heaven with you, but now it seems that I can't," Luo Zheng said helplessly.
When she heard Luo Zheng say that he would bring her along, Ling Shuang covered her mouth and laughed. "Let's go back and take a look. I can guarantee that both the Heavenly Palace and the You Xiong Clan are in a mess now!"
Just as Luo Zheng left the Nirvana Realm, the surrounding environment distorted. He was already sitting in his room.
When he removed the enchantment and opened the door, he saw three more people in the courtyard.
In the pond, Lin Zhanting and Qiu Yinhe were sitting upright. Xiaoyin and Little Xin were carefully brewing tea. Three important people came to visit Mister Luo at the same time. Xiaoyin and Little Xin felt a lot of pressure. Their faces were filled with worry. In their hearts, they were randomly guessing. Did Mister Luo get into big trouble?
Ning Yudie stood on the second floor. She and Xiaoyin felt the same.
Although she could not see the strength of these people, the aura that they exuded was so strong that it made her tremble in fear.

Naturally, they didn't have the leisure to get involved in this matter. Today, they came for the temple.
To be on the safe side, Qiu Yinhe gently waved his hand, and an invisible wave spread out.
Ning Yudie, Xiaoyin, and Xin couldn't hear the conversation between the three and Luo Zheng.
Ning Yudie also knew that these people must have something important to talk to Luo Zheng, so she stood quietly to the side.
"Kid, you are quite capable. You directly tore down half of their temple. You must have gained a lot, right?" Qiu Yinhe laughed.
Luo Zheng saluted with his hands folded and said, "The Nirvana token and soul crystal in the temple are all in my hands. If it is really inconvenient for the Heavenly Palace, I can also hand them over."
Destroying the other party's temple would certainly put a lot of pressure on the Heavenly Palace. Luo Zheng didn't want to make things difficult for the Heavenly Palace.
"That's easy." Lin Zhanting looked at Luo Zheng with appreciation. If it were him, he probably wouldn't be willing to hand them over.
Hechi snorted, "The Youxiong Clan has been bullying our Heavenly Palace all these years. This time, we rely on you to suppress them. What do they dare do to us? Since it's Luo Zheng's loot, it naturally belongs to you! "
"But, I'm afraid You Xiong Family will take revenge …" Luo Zheng hesitated.
Qiu Yinhe took a sip of tea and said, "The Mother World has been bustling with activity all these years. There will eventually be a fierce battle. When the time comes, even if we don't provoke them, they will still retaliate. When the time comes, they may not dare to make a move."
These people looked at the problem very calmly. They each chose their own Dao and followed their own destinies. Compromising would not solve the problem.
"In short, you must hide your identity in the Nirvana Realm well. Don't let anyone get a handle on you, including the people in our Heavenly Palace!" He Chi exhorted.
After the incident in the Nirvana Realm, the Heavenly Palace did a count of the trapped elite disciples.
The elites of the Taiyi Mountain, the Taiyi Mountain, and the other six mountains were all trapped, but the Taihao Mountain was not. The disciples of the Taihao Mountain were not involved, which was a suspicious point. Therefore, He Chi believed that even the people in the Heavenly Palace could not be completely trusted.
Luo Zheng understood what they meant. He nodded and asked, "How is Mo Yijian's injury?"
Luo Zheng had no ability to escape from the joint forces of the Youxiong Clan and the Divine Farmer Clan.
It was only because Mo Yijian used amazing means at the end that Luo Zheng was able to escape. Otherwise, the elites of the Heavenly Palace, including himself, would have been annihilated this time.
When Mo Yijian was mentioned, Qiu Yinhe's face showed a trace of haze and said, "He hasn't woken up yet."
Luo Zheng also sighed and said, "Is his soul so seriously damaged?"
At that time, Mo Yijian was completely surrounded by those winning fish and was constantly bitten by them. How could he bear it?
Qiu Yinhe shook his head and said, "It's not his soul that is damaged."
"It's not?" Luo Zheng was puzzled.
"You should have seen Mo Yijian's sword attack," Qiu Yinhe said.
"Yes," Luo Zheng nodded.
Mo Yijian's sword attack at the end was completely beyond Luo Zheng's imagination. Not only did it directly freeze all the winning fish, but it also indirectly helped Luo Zheng break the wall.
Luo Zheng also had doubts in his heart. Mo Yijian's Sun Soul had not reached the Indigo Soul Level, and the power of that sword attack was far beyond that of an ordinary powerhouse at the Indigo Soul Level.
"He overused his talent, and naturally he will be severely punished. We will find a way to revive him, but it will take time," Qiu Yinhe said.
As for what talent Mo Yijian had relied on to make that sword attack, Qiu Yinhe did not tell Luo Zheng.
The purpose of the three of them was to appease and also to confirm.
Of course, the Heavenly Palace would not admit that the person who destroyed the temple was Luo Zheng. However, the outside world still believed that Luo Zheng was inextricably linked to the Heavenly Palace.
In this way, the other forces would not blame the Heavenly Palace for Luo Zheng's wrongdoings in the Paramita. At the same time, it could also intimidate the other super forces. In the Paramita, casually provoking the Heavenly Palace would bring about a disaster.
In the next few days, the You Xiong Clan and the Heavenly Palace had another round of negotiations. They asked the person who destroyed the temple to return the soul crystal and the Paramita token.
However, the Heavenly Palace only insisted that they did ask the person to stop destroying the temple. As for the soul crystal and the Paramita token that he took from the temple, the Heavenly Palace said that they could not do anything about it. After all, they did not know who the person was.
This excuse was almost irrefutable. You Xiong Family had no choice but to suffer the loss.
Although they were indignant, they still did what they said. They asked the Jinwu to retreat from the Guanshan State. The war between the Heavenly Palace and the Jinwu Clan came to an end for the time being.
…
…
The Four Heavens of the Paramita.
In front of the You Xiong Clan's temple, many Yang souls were shuttling back and forth.
The broken ruins needed to be cleaned up, and the collapsed beams needed to be rebuilt. For the Yang souls in the Color Realm, this was a troublesome project.
After Luo Zheng returned to the Paramita Realm with his physical body, he went straight to the Heavenly Palace's temple. He wanted to find the Void Beast in the temple to get a Sumeru ring.
This Void Beast had stayed in the Heavenly Palace's temple for many years. It was probably the first time it saw the physical body of a human. When facing Luo Zheng, it felt a little uneasy.
However, when Luo Zheng reached out his hand, the Void Beast still lowered its head and stretched out its tentacles to carve on his finger.
A bright square frame gradually formed on his fingertip. However, when the square frame was about to take shape, there was a "hua" sound. Like a piece of glazed tile being broken, the whole square frame suddenly collapsed.
"Huh?"
The Void Beast did not expect this to happen either. It was stunned for a moment.
Luo Zheng looked at his fingertip and was also stunned. This Void Beast could actually fail.
"Let's try again," Luo Zheng requested.
The Void Beast did not refuse. It tried a second time. When it carved the Sumeru ring again, the situation was exactly the same as last time.
"Wu wu wu …"
The Void Beast shook its head. It was unwilling to help Luo Zheng make another Sumeru ring.
Luo Zheng's face was full of depression.
He turned his head and said to Ling Shuang, "It seems that the physical body cannot carve a Sumeru ring."
Ling Shuang smiled and said, "Your body can already do whatever you want in the Paramita Realm. If you have more Sumeru Spaces, I'm afraid that all the good things in the Paramita Realm will be taken by you."
In fact, Ling Shuang was a little selfish.
In the Paramita Realm, she was the only one who knew Luo Zheng's secret. If Luo Zheng wanted to plunder something, he needed her cooperation to pass it to his Yang soul.

Chapter 2892
Translator: Nyoi-Bo Studio Editor: Nyoi-Bo Studio
Dazzling Heaven
Luo Zheng naturally didn't know what Ling Shuang was thinking.
Then, Luo Zheng changed his Sun Soul and entered the Paramita Realm. He left the temple with Ling Shuang and headed to the plaza not far away.
A star ring in the plaza was still emitting a piercing chill.
Many people sat cross-legged around the star ring, welcoming the chill.
Absorbing spirit crystals to advance to the Sun Soul was a privilege that only disciples of superpowers had.
The humans scattered all over the Mother World didn't have the right to enter the temple.
Most of these people could only cultivate step by step. Using the piercing chill emitted by the star ring was one of the methods.
Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang, the two Indigo Souls, walked over, attracting the attention of many people.
Within the Fourth Heaven, Indigo Souls were definitely unparalleled. They could reach the Thirteenth Heaven, which was enough to make people look up to them.
"Let's go!"
Ling Shuang didn't hesitate at all. She stepped into the star ring.
As the star ring continued to spin,
Her Sun Soul was constantly being stretched, turning into a ray of light that shot into the sky like a rising comet.
After Ling Shuang was sent up, Luo Zheng also entered the star ring.
When he had just entered the Fourth Heaven, the star ring still gave him a lot of pressure. Now that his Sun Soul had improved greatly, the chill contained in the star ring couldn't hurt him at all.
Swish!
In the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng also left the Fourth Heaven.
Just as Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang left the Fourth Heaven, a Sun Soul slowly condensed in the plaza.
This was the first time this Sun Soul had set foot in the Fourth Heaven. He was full of curiosity about everything around him.
He looked around and walked to the side of the star ring. He caught a cultivator and asked, "What are you people doing sitting here?"
That cultivator looked at that person in confusion and said snappily, "Of course it's cultivation!"
"Cultivating?"
The cultivator looked at the star ring strangely. The principle of cultivation wasn't difficult to understand. He then laughed and said, "This kind of cultivation method is like an ant crawling on the ground. It's really too slow! I heard that those super powers have built temples in the Fourth Celestial Layer. Why don't you go there? "
When the cultivator heard this, he became even more depressed. He was not a disciple of some big clan, so how could he be qualified to enter the temple? Was this person deliberately looking for trouble?
"What does it have to do with you how I want to cultivate? Stay where it's cool! "The cultivator waved his hand, wanting to chase this fellow away.
"What does it have to do with me … Hehe, people of the 4th Celestial Layer are indeed different. In the 3rd Celestial Layer, no one dares to speak to me like that!"
As the man spoke, he stretched out his hand and lightly slit the cultivator's arm. The cultivator's arm was directly detached from the body. The man picked up the arm and stuffed it into his mouth!
He could actually directly devour the Sun Soul of others!
What was even stranger was that the cultivator was still sitting cross-legged, and there was no pain at all when his arm was broken. Until the cultivator was curious about what that person was eating, he took a closer look and realized that it was his own arm!
"You, you … can directly devour the Sun Soul of others!" The cultivator was shocked.
"What's so strange about that? The foreign races of the 2nd Celestial Layer and the 3rd Celestial Layer are too weak. I've only reached the 4th Celestial Layer and actually ascended to this damned place. I don't really like to devour the souls of my own race, but this place seems to only have humans?" The man said casually.
Of course, there were not only humans in the 4th Celestial Layer. However, most of the time, the same race would ascend in the same direction.
"You … You're crazy!" The cultivator screamed. His figure became blurry and he had already escaped to the other side.
"This person actually directly devoured the Sun Soul!"
"Isn't he afraid of a backlash?"
"How can there be such a lunatic!"
When the other cultivators in the square saw this scene, they were all shocked.
Every soul had its own will and could not devour each other. If they devoured too much, they would no longer be themselves.
Absorbing soul crystals was also a method to devour other souls. However, soul crystals needed to be accumulated over many years. Furthermore, soul crystals were incomparably pure. Naturally, there was a layer of risk in their beauty.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
In the blink of an eye, all the Sun Souls in the square had left.
Looking at the empty square, the man curled his lips. "I already said that I don't really devour my own race. Just now, I was just tasting it. Seems like the 4th Celestial Layer is nothing special …"
He didn't really care.
There were too many Sun Souls in the other side. For every Nirvana Realm expert in the mother world, there would be one more Sun Soul in the other side.
Other than the human race, there were also countless foreign races.
This person devoured from the 1st Celestial Layer all the way to the 4th Celestial Layer in just a few days.
Standing on the spot and looking around, he saw the broken temple and the busy You Xiong Clan disciples not far away. A strange expression appeared on his face.
"It's said that the temple keeps the purest soul crystals. I'll go ask for some. I wonder if they'll give it to me …"
According to his personality, if he wanted to do something, he would do it. Thus, he walked towards the temple.
…
…
Luo Zheng felt that his soul had transformed into a new shape. When he opened his eyes, he had already reached the 5th Celestial Layer.
This heaven and earth was different from the 4th Celestial Layer. Everywhere was white, and the ground seemed to be sprinkled with a layer of salt.
"The 5th Celestial Layer is actually so desolate?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Ling Shuang nodded and said, "Most races use the 4th Celestial Layer as a starting point. Naturally, the 5th Celestial Layer is neglected. Other than exploring some unique temples, usually no one stays in the 5th Celestial Layer."
The Nirvana Realm tokens in the 5th Celestial Layer were not much different from those in the 4th Celestial Layer. Moreover, those forces with Sun Souls that belonged to them could often obtain Nirvana tokens from higher Celestial Layers. They were not very interested in exploring the 5th Celestial Layer.
Not only the 5th Celestial Layer, but also the 6th and 7th Celestial Layer.
Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang didn't stay here for long. Once again, they entered the star ring and continued to ascend.
The 6th Celestial Layer Seven Planets' terrain was dangerous. The environment was complicated, and there were huge red rocks exposed on the surface everywhere.
In the 7th Celestial Layer Seven Planets of Nothingness, there was a patch of pink plants. The entire world was occupied by these plants.
The 8th Celestial Layer Eight Celestial Layer was a world of ice and snow …
The 9th Celestial Crimson Sun was a blazing sun …
The 10th Celestial Layer Nine Planets of Nothingness …
The two of them ascended in the star ring, then entered the star ring and ascended again.
As they continued to ascend, the pressure from the star ring also increased.
If it were an ordinary person, they would need to spend a few years or even more to ascend to the next Celestial Layer.
However, the two Indigo Souls turned a blind eye to this pressure. Their journey was very smooth.
The 11th Celestial Layer Illuminating Celestial Layer!
When the two of them just appeared, dozens of huge Sun Souls appeared not far away. Although the original faces of these Sun Souls couldn't be seen clearly, just by looking at their silhouettes, one could tell that their appearances were terrifying.
And behind Luo Zheng, there was a densely packed star ring. There were simply too many to count.
"Human, this way!"

Chapter 2893
Translator: Transn Editor: Transn
Raising the price on the spot
Luo Zheng looked at the star rings, then at the foreign races stationed here, his eyes full of confusion.
He didn't know how the star rings came to be, but they were the key to ascending to the Upper Heavens.
From what he knew so far, the star rings should be shared by the same race!
For example, there should be a considerable number of star rings in the First Heaven of the Paramita Realm, but the human race only had one star ring. All the humans had to use this star ring to ascend to the Upper Heavens.
It wasn't just the First Heaven. From the Second Heaven to the Third Heaven, the human race all shared one star ring.
However, the star rings were distributed according to region. Normally, the star rings of the human race were surrounded by humans, while the star rings of the foreign races were surrounded by foreign races.
But what was going on in front of him?
Countless star rings were piled up together, and these foreign races were even ordering him around, as if the entire Eleventh Heaven was their territory!
"Do as they say." Ling Shuang tugged at Luo Zheng.
Ling Shuang obviously knew more about the Paramita Realm than Luo Zheng. He frowned and followed behind her.
He looked again and found that the star rings were arranged in an orderly manner. Not far away, hundreds of Sun Souls were lined up in two lines.
When Luo Zheng walked to the front of the line, he heard the tall foreign race say, "The left side is the path to the Upper Heavens, and the right side is the path to explore the temple. Choose your line and stand!"
In the two lines, there were small Ear Mice and huge beasts. They were of different shapes and sizes, but they all lined up in an orderly manner.
After the two of them lined up at the back of the line, Luo Zheng asked in a low voice, "What's going on? This First Heaven seems to be very different from the previous ones. "
"Because the Glorious Sect Heaven is controlled by the Liyuan Race," Ling Shuang replied.
The Liyuan Race was considered a top superpower in the Mother World. Whether it was in terms of soul or strength, they were far stronger than the average race. A single orthodox human race would not be able to contend against this large race.
"A single race controls a Level One Heaven?" Luo Zheng was astonished.
Ling Shuang nodded and said, "It's an exception. It's said that the Li Yuan Tribe has a good relationship with one of the Nirvana Tribes. With the help of the Nirvana Tribes, it's not difficult to do this. So, any superpower that comes here has to follow their rules."
"What are the rules..." Luo Zheng asked.
Ling Shuang pouted her lips and said, "Well, if you want to go to the 12th Heaven through Yao Zong Sky, you have to pay 10,000 Soul Dans. Just line up."
"Everyone has to pay?"
"Yes, that should be the case."
Although Ling Shuang had heard of the Eleventh Heaven, it was her first time here.
In fact, not only did one have to pay Soul Cores to pass through the Eleventh Heaven, one had to pay even more Soul Cores to explore the Eleventh Heaven.
In the Color Realm, most Yang Souls would travel up and down, and these ten thousand Soul Pills were the toll.
"Isn't the Liyuan Clan's actions no different from highway robbery? Won't the other great clans of the Mother World join forces to resist? "Luo Zheng asked.
Although 10,000 Soul Pills was nothing to an Indigo Soul Rank powerhouse, they had to pay every time they passed by. This was not a small expense.
"Hehe, first of all, the Liyuan Clan was originally very strong, and the other superpowers couldn't afford to provoke them. Second, the number of Soul Cores they collected was fair, so everyone naturally tolerated it," said a foreign race person in front of Luo Zheng.
It was unknown which race this foreign race person belonged to. He looked similar to a human, but he was only half the size and had a few sharp horns on his head. He could speak the human language fluently.
"That's basically what it means," Ling Shuang agreed.
"No wonder Mingwei and the others warned me that the human race in the Mother World wasn't considered strong..."
Understanding this, Luo Zheng felt a little emotional.
In Luo Zheng's eyes, the Heavenly Palace, You Xiong, and Shennong were all extremely powerful forces.
But even the three superpowers of the human race couldn't monopolize a Layer of Heaven. How powerful was the Liyuan Clan?
After queuing for almost an hour in the Eleventh Layer of Heaven, it was finally their turn. Suddenly, a pitiful pleading voice came from the front.
"I only have 8,000 Soul Cores here. I wonder if the lords of the Liyuan Clan can make an exception?" The speaker was a human girl. Judging from her tender voice, she should be very young.
"Hey, human, don't you know the rules here? 10,000 Soul Cores is a small amount. If you don't have it, you can go over there and do hard labor. "The Liyuan Clan member pointed to the west.
The Liyuan Clan occupied the Eleventh Layer of Heaven. They didn't just build a temple there, but a huge home.
Building in the Paramita World was an extremely arduous task. The You Xiong Clan's temple had been destroyed by Luo Zheng, and it was extremely difficult to repair it in a short period of time.
The Liyuan Clan wanted to build a city in the Paramita World, so they naturally lacked hard labor.
The human girl looked in the direction the Liyuan Clan member was pointing and pursed her lips. Many of the outsiders could sense the fear emanating from her. Doing hard labor for the Liyuan Clan meant nine out of ten deaths. How could she be willing to go?
"I, I won't go up …. How about I go back to the Tenth Layer of Heaven?" The human girl gave up.
"Go back to the Tenth Layer of Heaven? Heh, of course you can. But you have to pay 10,000 Soul Cores. Haha! "
"You have to pay 10,000 Soul Cores if you go up or down! That's the rule of the Eleventh Layer of Heaven! "
"Hahaha …."
The tall Liyuan Clan members all roared with laughter.
The human girl seemed somewhat helpless. She turned to the people around her for help, asking for 2,000 Soul Cores. Once she reached the Twelfth Layer of Heaven, she would definitely return it. But she was surrounded by outsiders. How could they show any mercy to the human race? Not a single person stepped forward to help her.
"Then …. Can I leave the Paramita World and pay after I collect enough Soul Cores?" the human girl asked.
The Liyuan Clan member said impatiently, "Either pay 10,000 Soul Cores, or get the hell out of here and do hard labor. Little human girl, did you not understand what I said?"
The human girl stood in place and hesitated for a moment. Then, she really did walk towards the west.
At this time, Luo Zheng stepped out from the group and blocked the human girl's path. At the same time, he said to the Liyuan Clan member, "I will pay for the 2,000 Soul Cores."
The human girl was stunned for a moment. Then, she revealed a pleasantly surprised expression and said, "Thank you! Once we reach the Twelfth Layer of Heaven, I will return it to you! "
"No need," Luo Zheng generously said. Then, he prepared to take out 2,000 Soul Cores from his spatial ring.
But at this time, the Liyuan Clan member chuckled and said, "Boy, if you help pay for it, then it won't be just 2,000 Soul Cores!"
Luo Zheng frowned and asked, "Then how much is it?"
The Liyuan Clan member gestured with his finger, "100,000 Soul Cores!"
Hearing this number, Luo Zheng's face sank.
He had intercepted so many soul crystals from the You Xiong Clan. He had become rich all of a sudden. If he sold these soul crystals, he could exchange them for a sea of Soul Cores. To Luo Zheng, 100,000 was only a small number.
But for the Liyuan Clan member to raise the price like this, this was going too far.
"Isn't it 10,000 Soul Cores per person?" Luo Zheng asked in response.
"Haha …" The Liyuan Clan member's laughter contained a trace of ridicule, "This is our territory. However much we want, you have to give us. If you are willing to stand up for a fellow clansman, then not only will she have to pay 100,000 Soul Cores, but you will also have to pay 100,000 Soul Cores!"
